// ==================== HELPER AMAN UNTUK KOLOM SHEET ====================
window.safeGet = function(obj, key, defaultVal = '') {
  if (!obj) return defaultVal;
  const keysToTry = [
    key, key.toUpperCase(), key.toLowerCase(),
    key.replace(/ /g, '_'), key.replace(/ /g, ''),
    key.replace(/_/g, ' ')
  ];
  for (let k of keysToTry) {
    if (obj.hasOwnProperty(k) && obj[k] !== undefined && obj[k] !== null) {
      return String(obj[k]).trim();
    }
  }
  return defaultVal;
};

    // ==================== CORE HELPERS ====================
   // ==================== LOADING DENGAN KETERANGAN JELAS ====================
window.showLoading = function(show, message = '') {
  const loader = document.getElementById('loader');
  if (!loader) return;

  if (show) {
    loader.classList.remove('hidden');
    const loadingPhrases = ['Santai bestie, data lagi dipoles ✨','Sebentar ya, lagi gaskeun prosesnya 🚀','Lagi ngeracik tampilan biar makin kece 😎','Tunggu bentar, engine lagi ngebut ⚡'];
    let text = message || loadingPhrases[Math.floor(Math.random()*loadingPhrases.length)];
    
    // Update teks loading dinamis
    const existingText = loader.querySelector('p');
    if (existingText) {
      existingText.textContent = text;
    } else {
      const p = document.createElement('p');
      p.className = 'mt-4 font-extrabold text-indigo-600 animate-pulse uppercase tracking-widest text-[10px]';
      p.textContent = text;
      loader.appendChild(p);
    }
  } else {
    loader.classList.add('hidden');
  }
};
    window.formatIDR = function(n) { return new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', { style: 'currency', currency: 'IDR', minimumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(n || 0); }
    window.fileToBase64 = function(file) { return new Promise((resolve, reject) => { const reader = new FileReader(); reader.readAsDataURL(file); reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result.split(',')[1]); reader.onerror = error => reject(error); }); }
    
    window.serverCall = function(func, ...args) { 
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => { 
        if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
          const runner = google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(resolve).withFailureHandler(reject);
          const fn = runner[func];
          if (typeof fn !== 'function') {
            reject(new TypeError(`Function "${func}" tidak tersedia di Apps Script.`));
            return;
          }
          fn(...args);
        } else {
          console.warn("GAS Environment not detected. Mocking server call:", func);
          resolve([]); 
        }
      }); 
    }
    
    window.calculateOrderFinance = function(o) {
      const gross = Number(o['TOTAL HARGA']) || 0; 
      const discountP = Number(o['DISCOUNT']) || 0; 
      const net = gross - (gross * discountP / 100); 
      const dpP = Number(o['DP']) || 0; 
      const dpAmount = net * dpP / 100; 
      const balance = net - dpAmount;
      
      const layName = String(o['LAYANAN']).trim().toLowerCase();
      const servicesArray = (DASHBOARD_CACHE && DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices && DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices.length > 0) ? DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices : ALL_SERVICES;
      const svc = servicesArray.find(s => String(s['Detail Layanan'] || '').trim().toLowerCase() === layName);
      
      const f1 = svc ? (Number(svc['fee Admin(%)']) || 0) : 0; 
      const f2 = svc ? (Number(svc['fee perusahaan(%)']) || 0) : 0;
      
      const feeAdmin = net * (f1 / 100);
      const feeCompany = net * (f2 / 100);
      const workerNet = net - feeAdmin - feeCompany;

      return { gross, discountP, net, dpP, dpAmount, balance, feeAdmin, feeCompany, workerNet, f1, f2 };
    }

    window.copyText = async function(text) {
      try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
        alert("Berhasil disalin: " + text);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Gagal copy: ', err);
      }
    }

    window.setRunningTextModern = function(message = '') {
      const el = document.getElementById('running-text-el');

          <i data-lucide="megaphone" class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600"></i>
          <span>${msg}</span>
        </span>
      `;
      // Duplikasi konten agar animasi marquee mulus (seamless looping)
      el.innerHTML = `${item}${item}${item}${item}`;
      if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
    }

    window.applyWebIdentity = function(settings = {}) {
      const name1 = settings.name1 || 'Lentera';
      const name2 = settings.name2 || 'Cendekia';
      const slogan = settings.slogan || 'Premium Academic Support';
      const logoUrl = (settings.logoUrl || '').trim();
      const discountLabel = settings.discountLabel || '$ DISKON SPESIAL';
      window.WEB_SETTINGS = { ...(window.WEB_SETTINGS || {}), ...settings, discountLabel };

      const titleEl = document.getElementById('web-title');
      if (titleEl) titleEl.innerHTML = `${name1} <span class="text-indigo-600">${name2}</span>`;
      const footerTitle = document.getElementById('footer-web-title');
      if (footerTitle) footerTitle.textContent = `${name1} ${name2}`;
      const sloganEl = document.getElementById('web-slogan-display');
      if (sloganEl) sloganEl.textContent = slogan;

      ['app-logo-nav','app-logo-footer'].forEach(id => {
        const img = document.getElementById(id);
        if (!img) return;
        if (logoUrl) {
          img.src = logoUrl;
          img.classList.remove('hidden');
          img.onerror = () => img.classList.add('hidden');
        } else {
          img.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('.app-logo-fallback').forEach(el => el.classList.toggle('hidden', !!logoUrl));
    }

// ==================== SWITCH VIEW (FIXED & ROBUST) ====================
window.switchView = function(view) {
  // Sembunyikan semua view
  document.querySelectorAll('div[id^="view-"]').forEach(div => {
    div.classList.add('hidden');
  });

  // Tampilkan view yang diminta
  const target = document.getElementById('view-' + view);
  if (target) {
    target.classList.remove('hidden');
    console.log('✅ Berhasil pindah ke view:', view); // debug
  } else {
    console.error('❌ View tidak ditemukan:', view);
  }

  // Update header & footer
  updateAuthHeader();
  toggleFooter();
  if (typeof handlePromoBubbleVisibility === 'function') handlePromoBubbleVisibility();

  // Khusus login → reset form
  if (view === 'login') {
    const usernameInput = document.getElementById('login-username');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('login-password');
    const rememberCheckbox = document.getElementById('remember-username');
    const rememberedUsername = localStorage.getItem('lc_remembered_username') || '';
    if (usernameInput) usernameInput.value = rememberedUsername;
    if (passwordInput) passwordInput.value = '';
    if (rememberCheckbox) rememberCheckbox.checked = !!rememberedUsername;
  }
};

// ==================== FIX BUG: LOGOUT & PROMO BUBBLE ====================
window.processLogout = function(e) {
  if (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  }
  if (confirm("Apakah Anda yakin ingin keluar dari dashboard?")) {
    CURRENT_USER = null;
    DASHBOARD_CACHE = {};
    switchView('landing');
    updateAuthHeader();
    customAlert("👋 Berhasil", "Anda telah keluar dari dashboard", "info");
  }
};

// ==================== PROMO BUBBLE & MODAL CLOSE (FINAL FIX) ====================
window.handlePromoBubbleVisibility = function() {
  const bubble = document.getElementById('promo-bubble');
  const landingView = document.getElementById('view-landing');
  const isLandingVisible = !!landingView && !landingView.classList.contains('hidden');

  if (!isLandingVisible) {
    if (bubble) bubble.classList.add('hidden');
    if (window.bubbleInterval) {
      clearInterval(window.bubbleInterval);
      window.bubbleInterval = null;
    }
    return;
  }

  if (typeof showPromoBubble === 'function') showPromoBubble();
};

window.hidePromoBubble = function(e) {
  if (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  }
  const bubble = document.getElementById('promo-bubble');
  if (bubble) {
    bubble.style.transition = 'all 0.4s ease';
    bubble.style.opacity = '0';
    bubble.style.transform = 'translateY(30px)';
    setTimeout(() => {
      bubble.classList.add('hidden');
      bubble.style.opacity = '1';
      bubble.style.transform = 'none';
    }, 400);
  }
};



// Pastikan closeModal tetap kuat
window.closeModal = function() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.hidden.fixed.inset-0').forEach(modal => {
    if (!modal.classList.contains('hidden')) {
      modal.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  });
};


// ==================== AUTH HEADER (VERSI FINAL - TOMBOL MASUK PASTI JALAN) ====================
window.updateAuthHeader = function() {
  const container = document.getElementById('auth-header-actions');
  if (!container) return;

  container.innerHTML = '';

  if (CURRENT_USER) {
    container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div onclick="switchView('dashboard')" class="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 hover:bg-indigo-50 rounded-3xl cursor-pointer text-sm font-semibold">
          <i data-lucide="layout-dashboard"></i>
          <span>Dashboard</span>
        </div>
        <div onclick="processLogout(event)" class="flex items-center gap-2 bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 text-white px-6 py-2.5 rounded-3xl cursor-pointer text-sm font-semibold">
          <i data-lucide="log-out"></i>
          Keluar
        </div>
      </div>`;
  } else {
    // Tombol Masuk (belum login)
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.innerHTML = `<i data-lucide="log-in" class="w-5 h-5"></i><span class="ml-2 font-bold">Masuk</span>`;
    btn.className = `flex items-center px-7 py-3 bg-white text-[#7c3aed] hover:bg-indigo-50 rounded-3xl font-semibold shadow-lg transition-all active:scale-95`;
    btn.onclick = function(e) {
      e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      e.preventDefault();
      switchView('login');
    };
    container.appendChild(btn);
  }

  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

    window.processLogout = function() {
      CURRENT_USER = null;
      if (notificationPolling) clearInterval(notificationPolling);
      const adminMobile = document.getElementById('admin-only-nav-mobile');
      const bendaharaMobile = document.getElementById('bendahara-only-nav-mobile');
      if (adminMobile) adminMobile.classList.add('hidden');
      if (bendaharaMobile) bendaharaMobile.classList.add('hidden');
      switchView('landing');
      updateAuthHeader();   
    }

    window.togglePassword = function() {
      const passwordField = document.getElementById('login-password');
      const button = document.getElementById('toggle-password-btn');
      if (!passwordField || !button) return;
      passwordVisible = !passwordVisible;
      if (passwordVisible) {
        passwordField.type = 'text'; button.innerHTML = `<i data-lucide="eye-off" class="w-5 h-5"></i>`;
      } else {
        passwordField.type = 'password'; button.innerHTML = `<i data-lucide="eye" class="w-5 h-5"></i>`;
      }
      if(window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
    }

    window.handleAccountUpdate = async function(e) {
      e.preventDefault(); showLoading(true);
      try {
        const payload = { id: CURRENT_USER['ID UNIK'], name: document.getElementById('acc-name').value, password: document.getElementById('acc-password').value, bank: document.getElementById('acc-bank').value, rekening: document.getElementById('acc-rekening').value, email: document.getElementById('acc-email').value };
        const res = await serverCall('updateWorkerAccount', payload);
        if (res.success) { customAlert("Berhasil", "Akun diperbarui!"); refreshData(); }
      } catch (e) {} finally { showLoading(false); }
    }

window.showSubView = function(sub) {
  showLoading(true, `Memuat ${sub === 'monitor' ? 'Ringkasan' : sub === 'orders' ? 'Daftar Pesanan' : sub === 'services' ? 'Katalog Layanan' : sub === 'users' ? 'Manajemen Tim' : sub === 'pengeluaran' ? 'Riwayat Pengeluaran' : sub === 'pendapatan' ? 'Pendapatan Lainnya' : sub}...`);

  // Sembunyikan semua subview
  document.querySelectorAll('[id^="sub-"]').forEach(s => s.classList.add('hidden'));

  // Tampilkan subview yang dipilih
  const target = document.getElementById(`sub-${sub}`);
  if (target) {
    target.classList.remove('hidden');

    // Animasi masuk (fadeInUp)
    target.style.animation = 'none';
    void target.offsetWidth;                    // trigger reflow
    target.style.animation = 'fadeInUp 0.6s ease forwards';
  }

  // Update active nav-link
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link').forEach(l => l.classList.remove('active'));
  const activeLink = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link')).find(l => 
    l.getAttribute('onclick') && l.getAttribute('onclick').includes(`'${sub}'`)
  );
  if (activeLink) activeLink.classList.add('active');

  // Logic rendering lama (tetap dipertahankan)
  if (sub === 'orders') {
    if (!DASHBOARD_CACHE || !DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders) {
      refreshData().then(() => filterOrdersFull());
    } else {
      filterOrdersFull();
    }
  }
  if (sub === 'services') renderAdminServices();
  if (sub === 'users') renderAdminUsers();
  if (sub === 'walog') renderWALog();
  if (sub === 'notifications') loadNotificationSettings();
  if (sub === 'website') loadWebSettings();
  if (sub === 'settings') initSettingsForm && initSettingsForm();
  if (sub === 'pendapatan') {
    currentPendapatanPage = 1;
    renderPendapatanTable && renderPendapatanTable();
  }
  if (sub === 'pengeluaran' || sub === 'gaji') {
    // Refresh data keuangan jika diperlukan
    updateMonitorStats && updateMonitorStats();
  }

  // Animasi tambahan pada tabel & kartu setelah loading
  setTimeout(() => {
    showLoading(false);
    document.querySelectorAll('.bg-white.rounded-\\[2\\.5rem\\], table, .catalog-card').forEach(el => {
      el.style.animation = 'none';
      void el.offsetWidth;
      el.style.animation = 'fadeInUp 0.5s ease forwards';
    });
  }, 420);
};

    window.initSettingsForm = function() {
      if (!CURRENT_USER) return;
      document.getElementById('acc-name').value = CURRENT_USER['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || '';
      document.getElementById('acc-email').value = CURRENT_USER['Email'] || '';
      
      let bankKey = Object.keys(CURRENT_USER).find(k => k.toLowerCase().includes('bank')) || 'Nama Bank';
      let rekKey = Object.keys(CURRENT_USER).find(k => k.toLowerCase().includes('rekening')) || 'No Rekening';
      
      document.getElementById('acc-bank').value = CURRENT_USER[bankKey] || '';
      document.getElementById('acc-rekening').value = CURRENT_USER[rekKey] || '';
      document.getElementById('acc-password').value = '';
    }

window.renderDashboardData = function() {
  if (!DASHBOARD_CACHE) {
    console.warn("⚠️ DASHBOARD_CACHE belum terisi saat render");
    return;
  }

  console.log("✅ renderDashboardData dijalankan | Orders:", DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders?.length || 0);

  const orders = DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders || [];
  const roleStr = String(CURRENT_USER.Role).toLowerCase();
  const isAdmin = roleStr === 'admin';
  const isBendahara = roleStr === 'bendahara' || roleStr === 'finance';
  const isWorker = !isAdmin && !isBendahara;

  // Update Profil Sidebar
  document.getElementById('user-display-name').innerText = CURRENT_USER['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || CURRENT_USER.Username || '-';
  document.getElementById('user-display-role').innerText = CURRENT_USER.Role || '-';
  document.getElementById('profile-initial').innerText = 
    (CURRENT_USER['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || CURRENT_USER.Username || '?')[0].toUpperCase();

  // ==================== KARTU PROYEK AKTIF ====================
  let pending = 0;
  if (orders.length > 0) {
    pending = orders.filter(o => {
      const status = window.safeGet(o, 'TAHAPAN PESANAN');
      return ['Antrean', 'Proses', 'Revisi'].includes(status);
    }).length;
  }
  const statPending = document.getElementById('stat-pending');
  if (statPending) statPending.innerText = pending;

  // ==================== INFO GAJI TERAKHIR (RINGKASAN) ====================
  const tableMonitor = document.getElementById('table-monitor-body');
  if (tableMonitor) {
    let gajiList = DASHBOARD_CACHE.gaji || [];
    if (isAdmin || isWorker) {
      const loginUsername = String(CURRENT_USER.Username || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      gajiList = gajiList.filter(g => String(g['Worker Username'] || '').trim().toLowerCase() === loginUsername);
    }
    gajiList = gajiList.slice(0, 5);
    if (gajiList.length === 0) {
      tableMonitor.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
          <td colspan="4" class="text-center py-12 text-slate-400 font-medium">
            Belum ada data gaji terbaru untuk akun ini
          </td>
        </tr>`;
    } else {
      tableMonitor.innerHTML = gajiList.map(g => `
        <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
          <td class="px-10 py-5 font-bold">${g['Bulan/Tahun'] || '-'}</td>
          <td class="px-10 py-5">${g['Worker Username'] || '-'}</td>
          <td class="px-10 py-5 font-black text-emerald-700">${formatIDR(g['Total Terima'])}</td>
          <td class="px-10 py-5">
            <button onclick="downloadSlipGaji('${g['ID Gaji']}')" class="text-indigo-600 font-bold text-xs hover:underline">Detail & Preview Slip</button>
          </td>
        </tr>
      `).join('');
    }
  }

  // ==================== TABEL GAJI ====================
  const gTable = document.getElementById('gaji-table-body');
  if (gTable && DASHBOARD_CACHE.gaji) {
    let gajiData = DASHBOARD_CACHE.gaji;
    if (isWorker) {
      gajiData = gajiData.filter(g => g['Worker Username'] === CURRENT_USER.Username);
    }
    gTable.innerHTML = gajiData.length === 0 
      ? `<tr><td colspan="7" class="text-center py-12 text-slate-400">Belum ada data penggajian</td></tr>`
      : gajiData.map(g => `
        <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
          <td class="px-6 py-4 font-bold">${g['Bulan/Tahun']}</td>
          <td class="px-6 py-4">${g['Worker Username'] || g['Nama Pegawai'] || '-'}</td>
          <td class="px-6 py-4 text-right">${formatIDR(g['Gaji Pokok'])}</td>
          <td class="px-6 py-4 text-right text-emerald-600">+${formatIDR(g['Bonus'])}</td>
          <td class="px-6 py-4 text-right text-red-600">-${formatIDR(g['Potongan'])}</td>
          <td class="px-6 py-4 text-right font-black text-indigo-600">${formatIDR(g['Total Terima'])}</td>
          <td class="px-6 py-4 text-center">
            <button onclick="downloadSlipGaji('${g['ID Gaji']}')" class="p-2 text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50 hover:bg-indigo-100 rounded-lg"><i data-lucide="download" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>
          </td>
        </tr>
      `).join('');
  }

  // Render semua tabel lain
  renderOrdersFull(orders);
  renderPengeluaranTable();
  if (typeof renderPendapatanTable === 'function') renderPendapatanTable();

  // Update statistik kartu
  updateMonitorStats();

  // Admin-only
  if (isAdmin) {
    const runningInput = document.getElementById('input-running-text');
    if (runningInput) runningInput.value = DASHBOARD_CACHE.runningText || '';
    renderAdminServices();
    renderAdminUsers();
  }

  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

window.renderPengeluaranTable = function() {
  const pTable = document.getElementById('pengeluaran-table-body');
  const paginationContainer = document.getElementById('expense-pagination');
  if (!pTable || !DASHBOARD_CACHE || !DASHBOARD_CACHE.pengeluaran) {
    if (pTable) pTable.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center py-12 text-slate-400">Belum ada data pengeluaran</td></tr>`;
    return;
  }

  let data = DASHBOARD_CACHE.pengeluaran;
  if (currentExpenseFilter !== 'all') {
    data = data.filter(p => p['Kategori'] === currentExpenseFilter);
  }

  const totalPages = Math.ceil(data.length / EXPENSES_PER_PAGE) || 1;
  currentExpensePage = Math.min(Math.max(1, currentExpensePage), totalPages);

  const startIdx = (currentExpensePage - 1) * EXPENSES_PER_PAGE;
  const paginatedData = data.slice(startIdx, startIdx + EXPENSES_PER_PAGE);

  if (paginatedData.length === 0) {
    pTable.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center py-12 text-slate-400">Belum ada data pengeluaran untuk filter ini</td></tr>`;
  } else {
    pTable.innerHTML = paginatedData.map(p => `
      <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors border-b border-slate-100">
        <td class="px-6 py-4">${new Date(p['Tanggal'] || p['TANGGAL']).toLocaleDateString('id-ID')}</td>
        <td class="px-6 py-4 font-mono text-xs"><button onclick="openExpenseDetail('${p['ID Pengeluaran']}')" class="text-indigo-600 hover:underline font-bold">${p['ID Pengeluaran']}</button></td>
        <td class="px-6 py-4">
          <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <span class="px-3 py-1 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 rounded-full text-xs font-bold w-fit">${p['Kategori']}</span>
            <span class="text-sm leading-relaxed break-words">${p['Deskripsi']}</span>
          </div>
        </td>
        <td class="px-6 py-4 text-right font-bold text-red-600">${formatIDR(p['Jumlah'])}</td>
        <td class="px-6 py-4 text-xs">${p['Input By']}</td>
        <td class="px-6 py-4 flex justify-center gap-2">
          <button onclick="editPengeluaran('${p['ID Pengeluaran']}')" class="p-3 bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200 text-amber-700 rounded-xl transition-all">
            <i data-lucide="edit" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
          </button>
          <button onclick="deletePengeluaran('${p['ID Pengeluaran']}')" class="p-3 bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-700 rounded-xl transition-all">
            <i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
          </button>
        </td>
      </tr>
    `).join('');
  }

  // Pagination
  let pageButtons = '';
  for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
    pageButtons += i === currentExpensePage 
      ? `<button class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold text-xs">${i}</button>` 
      : `<button onclick="changeExpensePage(${i})" class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white border hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 font-bold text-xs">${i}</button>`;
  }

  paginationContainer.innerHTML = `
    <button onclick="changeExpensePage(${currentExpensePage - 1})" ${currentExpensePage === 1 ? 'disabled class="opacity-50"' : ''} class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white border flex items-center justify-center text-slate-600"><i data-lucide="chevron-left" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>
    ${pageButtons}
    <button onclick="changeExpensePage(${currentExpensePage + 1})" ${currentExpensePage === totalPages ? 'disabled class="opacity-50"' : ''} class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white border flex items-center justify-center text-slate-600"><i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>
  `;

  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

    window.changeExpensePage = function(page) {
      currentExpensePage = page;
      renderPengeluaranTable();
    };

    window.changeExpenseFilter = function(filter) {
      currentExpenseFilter = filter;
      currentExpensePage = 1;
      renderPengeluaranTable();
    };

    window.openExpenseDetail = function(id) {
      const p = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.pengeluaran || []).find(x => x['ID Pengeluaran'] === id);
      if (!p) return;
      const desc = String(p['Deskripsi'] || '');
      let gajiInfo = '';
      if (desc.toLowerCase().includes('pembayaran gaji')) {
        const match = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.gaji || []).find(g => desc.includes(g['Bulan/Tahun'] || '') && desc.toLowerCase().includes(String(g['Worker Username'] || '').toLowerCase()));
        if (match) {
          const u = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.users || []).find(x => x.Username === match['Worker Username']) || {};
          const bankKey = Object.keys(u).find(k => k.toLowerCase().includes('bank')) || 'Nama Bank';
          const rekKey = Object.keys(u).find(k => k.toLowerCase().includes('rekening')) || 'No Rekening';
          const bank = u[bankKey] || '-';
          const rek = u[rekKey] || '-';
          const totalTerima = Number(match['Total Terima']) || 0;
          const adminBank = getBankAdminFee(bank, totalTerima);
          const transfer = Math.max(0, totalTerima - adminBank);
          gajiInfo = `
            <div class="bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-100 rounded-2xl p-4 text-sm">
              <p class="font-black text-indigo-700 mb-2">Rincian Gaji Terkait</p>
              <div class="space-y-1">
                <p>Worker: <strong>${match['Worker Username']}</strong></p>
                <p>Periode: <strong>${match['Bulan/Tahun']}</strong></p>
                <p>Gaji Pokok: <strong>${formatIDR(match['Gaji Pokok'])}</strong></p>
                <p>Bonus: <strong>${formatIDR(match['Bonus'])}</strong></p>
                <p>Potongan: <strong>${formatIDR(match['Potongan'])}</strong></p>
                <p>Bank Tujuan: <strong>${bank}</strong></p>
                <p>No Rekening: <strong>${rek}</strong></p>
                <p>Biaya Admin Bank: <strong>${formatIDR(adminBank)}</strong></p>
                <p>Total Tercatat Pengeluaran: <strong>${formatIDR(totalTerima)}</strong></p>
                <p>Total Transfer Diterima: <strong class="text-emerald-700">${formatIDR(transfer)}</strong></p>
              </div>
            </div>`;
        }
      }
      const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
      body.innerHTML = `
        <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-4">Detail Pengeluaran</h3>
        <div class="space-y-3 text-sm">
          <p><span class="text-slate-500">ID:</span> <strong>${id}</strong></p>
          <p><span class="text-slate-500">Tanggal:</span> <strong>${new Date(p['Tanggal'] || p['TANGGAL']).toLocaleDateString('id-ID')}</strong></p>
          <p><span class="text-slate-500">Kategori:</span> <strong>${p['Kategori']}</strong></p>
          <p><span class="text-slate-500">Jumlah:</span> <strong class="text-red-600">${formatIDR(p['Jumlah'])}</strong></p>
          <p><span class="text-slate-500">Input By:</span> <strong>${p['Input By'] || '-'}</strong></p>
          <div class="bg-slate-50 border rounded-xl p-3"><p class="text-slate-500 text-xs mb-1">Deskripsi</p><p class="font-medium">${p['Deskripsi'] || '-'}</p></div>
          ${gajiInfo}
        </div>
      `;
      document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
    };

   window.deletePengeluaran = async function(id) {
  if (!confirm("Yakin ingin menghapus pengeluaran ini?")) return;
  showLoading(true);
  try {
    const p = DASHBOARD_CACHE.pengeluaran.find(x => x['ID Pengeluaran'] === id);
    await serverCall('deletePengeluaran', id);   // backend akan handle sync Gaji

    // Refresh semua data
    refreshData();
    customAlert("Berhasil", "Pengeluaran dihapus (dan Gaji terkait otomatis dihapus jika ada)");
  } catch(e) {
    customAlert("Error", "Gagal menghapus pengeluaran");
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};

    window.editPengeluaran = function(id) {
      const p = DASHBOARD_CACHE.pengeluaran.find(x => x['ID Pengeluaran'] === id);
      if(!p) return;
      
      const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
      body.innerHTML = `
        <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6">Edit Pengeluaran</h3>
        <form onsubmit="handleEditPengeluaran(event, '${id}')" class="space-y-6">
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Tanggal</label><input type="date" id="edit-pengeluaran-tanggal" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Kategori</label><select id="edit-pengeluaran-kategori" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"><option value="Operasional">Operasional</option><option value="Marketing">Marketing</option><option value="Aset">Aset</option><option value="Gaji Pegawai">Gaji Pegawai</option><option value="Lainnya">Lainnya</option></select></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Deskripsi</label><input type="text" id="edit-pengeluaran-deskripsi" value="${p['Deskripsi']}" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Jumlah (Rp)</label><input type="number" id="edit-pengeluaran-jumlah" required min="0" value="${p['Jumlah']}" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-4 gradient-primary text-white rounded-2xl font-bold shadow-lg">Update Pengeluaran</button>
        </form>
      `;
      
      let d = new Date(p['Tanggal'] || p['TANGGAL']);
      let month = '' + (d.getMonth() + 1);
      let day = '' + d.getDate();
      let year = d.getFullYear();
      if (month.length < 2) month = '0' + month;
      if (day.length < 2) day = '0' + day;
      document.getElementById('edit-pengeluaran-tanggal').value = [year, month, day].join('-');
      document.getElementById('edit-pengeluaran-kategori').value = p['Kategori'];
      
      document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
    };

    window.handleEditPengeluaran = async function(e, id) {
      e.preventDefault();
      showLoading(true);
      const payload = { 
          id: id,
          tanggal: document.getElementById('edit-pengeluaran-tanggal').value, 
          deskripsi: document.getElementById('edit-pengeluaran-deskripsi').value, 
          jumlah: document.getElementById('edit-pengeluaran-jumlah').value, 
          kategori: document.getElementById('edit-pengeluaran-kategori').value 
      };
      try { 
          await serverCall('updatePengeluaran', payload); 
          closeModal(); 
          customAlert("Berhasil", "Data pengeluaran diupdate."); 
          refreshData(); 
      } catch (e) { 
          customAlert("Error", "Gagal mengupdate pengeluaran"); 
      } finally { 
          showLoading(false); 
      }
    };

    // ==================== NOTIFICATIONS ====================
    window.showNotifications = async function() {
      showLoading(true);
      try {
        const res = await serverCall('getNotifications', CURRENT_USER.Username, CURRENT_USER.Role);
        const list = document.getElementById('notification-list');
        list.innerHTML = res.length === 0 
          ? `<div class="text-center py-12 text-slate-400">Tidak ada notifikasi baru saat ini</div>`
          : res.map(n => `
              <div class="flex gap-4 p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl hover:bg-white transition-all">
                <div class="w-9 h-9 bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-600 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center">
                  <i data-lucide="${n.type === 'new_order' ? 'plus-circle' : n.type === 'completed' ? 'check-circle' : 'refresh-cw'}" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
                </div>
                <div class="flex-1">
                  <p class="text-sm font-medium">${n.message}</p>
                  <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">${new Date(n.time).toLocaleString('id-ID')}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            `).join('');
        document.getElementById('notification-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
        if(window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
      } catch(e) { console.error(e); customAlert("Error", "Gagal memuat notifikasi"); } finally { showLoading(false); }
    }

    window.loadNotificationCount = async function() {
      if (!CURRENT_USER) return;
      try {
        const res = await serverCall('getNotifications', CURRENT_USER.Username, CURRENT_USER.Role);
        const badge = document.getElementById('notification-badge');
        if (res.length > 0) {
          badge.textContent = res.length > 99 ? '99+' : res.length;
          badge.classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
          badge.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      } catch(e) {}
    }

    window.startNotificationPolling = function() {
      if (notificationPolling) clearInterval(notificationPolling);
      notificationPolling = setInterval(loadNotificationCount, 15000);
    }

    window.markAllAsRead = function() {
      serverCall('markNotificationsAsRead', CURRENT_USER.Username);
      loadNotificationCount();
      closeNotificationModal();
    }

    window.closeNotificationModal = function() {
      document.getElementById('notification-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    // ==================== LANDING PAGE & CATALOG ====================
    window.typeHeadline = function() {
      const settings = { tagline3: document.getElementById('web-tagline3')?.value || 'Karya Akademik' };
      const text = settings.tagline3;
      const element = document.getElementById('typing-text');
      if (!element) return;
      let i = 0; element.innerHTML = '';
      function type() {
        if (i < text.length) { element.innerHTML += text.charAt(i); i++; setTimeout(type, 80); } 
        else { element.style.borderRight = '3px solid #6366f1'; setTimeout(() => { element.style.borderRight = 'none'; }, 2500); }
      }
      setTimeout(type, 600);
    }

    window.renderCategoryTabs = function() {
      const tabs = document.getElementById('category-tabs');
      if(!tabs) return;
      tabs.innerHTML = `<button onclick="filterCatalog('Semua')" class="tab-category px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full font-bold text-sm bg-white border shadow-sm hover:bg-indigo-50 whitespace-nowrap">Semua</button>` + ALL_CATEGORIES.map(cat => `<button onclick="filterCatalog('${cat}')" class="tab-category px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full font-bold text-sm bg-white border shadow-sm hover:bg-indigo-50 whitespace-nowrap">${cat}</button>`).join('');
      tabs.querySelector('.tab-category')?.classList.add('active');
    }

    window.filterCatalog = function(cat) {
      CURRENT_CATALOG_CATEGORY = cat || 'Semua';
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-category').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
      const activeTab = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.tab-category')).find(t => t.getAttribute('onclick').includes(`'${cat}'`));
      if (activeTab) activeTab.classList.add('active');
      const term = (document.getElementById('catalog-search')?.value || '').trim();
      renderCatalog(CURRENT_CATALOG_CATEGORY, term);
    }

  window.renderCatalog = async function(category = CURRENT_CATALOG_CATEGORY, searchTerm = null) {
  const container = document.getElementById('catalog-grid');
  if (!container) return;
  const normalizedCategory = !category || category === 'all' ? 'Semua' : category;
  CURRENT_CATALOG_CATEGORY = normalizedCategory;
  const activeSearch = searchTerm !== null ? searchTerm : (document.getElementById('catalog-search')?.value || '').trim();

  container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="col-span-full text-center py-12">
      <div class="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-[#7c3aed] border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto"></div>
      <p class="text-slate-400 mt-3">Memuat katalog 3D...</p>
    </div>`;

  try {
    let services = await serverCall('getPublicServices');

    // Filter KATEGORI
    if (normalizedCategory !== 'Semua') {
      const catLower = normalizedCategory.toLowerCase();
      services = services.filter(s => {
        const serviceCat = String(s.Kategori || s.Jenis || s['Target Profesi'] || '').toLowerCase();
        return serviceCat.includes(catLower);
      });
    }

    // Filter pencarian teks
    if (activeSearch) {
      const term = activeSearch.toLowerCase();
      services = services.filter(s => 
        String(s['Detail Layanan'] || '').toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
        String(s.Deskripsi || '').toLowerCase().includes(term)
      );
    }

    if (services && services.length > 0) {
      container.innerHTML = services.map(service => {
        const nama = String(service['Detail Layanan'] || service['ID LAYANAN'] || 'Layanan').trim();
        const deskripsi = String(service.Deskripsi || 'Layanan akademik premium').trim();
        const basePrice = Number(service['Harga Publish'] || service['Harga'] || service['Harga Dasar'] || 0);
        const minPrice = Number(service['Harga Min'] || service['Harga Minimum'] || Math.max(0, basePrice * 0.85));
        const maxPrice = Number(service['Harga Max'] || service['Harga Maksimum'] || Math.max(basePrice, basePrice * 1.15));
        const rangePrice = basePrice > 0 ? `${formatIDR(minPrice)} - ${formatIDR(maxPrice)}` : 'Konsultasi dulu untuk harga';
        const discountLabel = (window.WEB_SETTINGS && window.WEB_SETTINGS.discountLabel) || '$ DISKON SPESIAL';

        return `
          <div class="tilt-card group bg-white/75 backdrop-blur-xl border border-white/60 rounded-3xl overflow-hidden shadow-lg hover:shadow-2xl transition-all duration-500" data-tilt data-tilt-glare data-tilt-max-glare="0.5" data-tilt-scale="1.03">
            <div class="h-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#7c3aed] to-[#c026d3]"></div>
            <div class="p-6 flex flex-col h-full">
              <div class="w-14 h-14 flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-[#7c3aed]/10 to-[#c026d3]/10 rounded-2xl mb-6 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                <i data-lucide="${getServiceIcon(service)}" class="w-8 h-8 text-[#7c3aed]"></i>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-2"><h3 class="text-lg md:text-xl font-semibold text-slate-800 line-clamp-2">${nama}</h3><span class="text-[10px] px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 font-black whitespace-nowrap">${discountLabel}</span></div>
              <p class="text-slate-600 text-sm leading-relaxed line-clamp-3">${deskripsi}</p>
              <div class="mt-3 bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-100 rounded-xl p-2 text-xs"><p class="font-black text-indigo-700">Range Harga</p><p class="text-indigo-600">${rangePrice}</p></div>
              <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-2">*Harga final menyesuaikan tingkat kesulitan & deadline.</p>
              
              <div class="flex gap-3 mt-auto">
                <button onclick="showConsultModal('${nama.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" 
                        class="flex-1 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium border border-[#7c3aed]/30 hover:border-[#7c3aed] text-[#7c3aed] rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:bg-[#7c3aed]/5 transition-all">
                  <i data-lucide="message-circle" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                  Konsultasi
                </button>
                <button onclick="showOrderModal('${nama.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" 
                        class="flex-1 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold bg-gradient-to-r from-[#7c3aed] to-[#c026d3] text-white rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:shadow-lg hover:scale-[1.02] transition-all">
                  <i data-lucide="shopping-cart" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                  Pesan Sekarang
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>`;
      }).join('');
    } else {
      container.innerHTML = `<p class="col-span-full text-center py-12 text-slate-400">
        ${normalizedCategory === 'Semua' ? 'Belum ada layanan yang tersedia saat ini' : `Belum ada layanan untuk kategori <strong>${normalizedCategory}</strong>`}
      </p>`;
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Render catalog error:", e);
    container.innerHTML = `<div class="col-span-full text-center py-12 text-red-400">Gagal memuat katalog</div>`;
  }

  VanillaTilt.init(document.querySelectorAll(".tilt-card"), { max: 15, speed: 400, glare: true, "max-glare": 0.5 });
  lucide.createIcons();
};

// Helper icon
function getServiceIcon(service) {
  const cat = String(service.Kategori || service.Jenis || '').toLowerCase();
  if (cat.includes('tugas akhir') || cat.includes('skripsi')) return 'graduation-cap';
  if (cat.includes('presentasi') || cat.includes('ppt')) return 'presentation';
  if (cat.includes('medis') || cat.includes('keperawatan')) return 'stethoscope';
  if (cat.includes('data') || cat.includes('statistik')) return 'bar-chart';
  return 'book-open';
}



    window.populatePublicServicesSelect = async function() {
      try {
        const services = await serverCall('getPublicServices');
        const select = document.getElementById('select-layanan-public');
        if(!select) return;
        let options = '<option value="">Pilih layanan...</option>';
        services.forEach(s => {
          options += `<option value="${s['Detail Layanan']}" data-harga="${s['Harga Publish'] || 0}" data-satuan="${s.Satuan || 'Unit'}">${s['Detail Layanan']}</option>`;
        });
        select.innerHTML = options;
      } catch (error) { console.error("Gagal fetch layanan publik:", error); }
    }

	    window.scrollToCatalog = function() { document.getElementById('catalog-section').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' }); }


window.renderWhatsAppTestimonials = async function() {
  const row1 = document.getElementById('wa-testi-row-1');
  const row2 = document.getElementById('wa-testi-row-2');
  if (!row1 || !row2) return;

  let testimoni = [];
  try {
    const recent = await serverCall('getRecentOrdersPublic');
    if (recent && recent.length > 0) {
      testimoni = recent.map((r, i) => ({
        nama: `Pelanggan ${i + 1}`,
        pesan: r.text || "Pelayanan cepat dan hasil memuaskan banget!",
        waktu: r.time || "Baru saja",
      }));
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  if (testimoni.length === 0) {
    testimoni = [
      { nama: "Aulia • S1 Manajemen", pesan: "Respon admin super cepat, revisi langsung ditangani.", waktu: "5 menit lalu" },
      { nama: "Rani • S1 Keperawatan", pesan: "Aku order malam, pagi langsung dapat update progres.", waktu: "12 menit lalu" },
      { nama: "Fikri • S1 Informatika", pesan: "Penjelasan detail dan hasilnya rapi banget.", waktu: "20 menit lalu" },
      { nama: "Maya • D4 Kebidanan", pesan: "Bantuanku selesai sebelum deadline, recommended!", waktu: "30 menit lalu" },
      { nama: "Bayu • S1 Hukum", pesan: "Fast respon, komunikatif, dan hasil sesuai brief.", waktu: "45 menit lalu" },
      { nama: "Dina • S1 Akuntansi", pesan: "Sangat terbantu buat finalisasi tugas akhir.", waktu: "1 jam lalu" },
    ];
  }

  const makeCard = (t) => `
    <div class="wa-chat-card bg-white rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 shadow-sm p-4">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
        <p class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm">${t.nama}</p>
        <i data-lucide="message-circle" class="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500"></i>
      </div>
      <p class="text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">"${t.pesan}"</p>
      <p class="text-[11px] text-emerald-600 font-semibold mt-3">${t.waktu}</p>
    </div>
  `;

  const half = Math.ceil(testimoni.length / 2);
  const list1 = testimoni.slice(0, half);
  const list2 = testimoni.slice(half).length ? testimoni.slice(half) : testimoni;

  row1.innerHTML = [...list1, ...list1].map(makeCard).join('');
  row2.innerHTML = [...list2, ...list2].map(makeCard).join('');
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

// PROMO BUBBLE - DATA ORDER ASLI (Floating Bottom Left)
window.renderPromoBubble = async function() {
  const bubble = document.getElementById('promo-bubble');
  const content = document.getElementById('bubble-content');
  if (!bubble || !content) return;
  const landingView = document.getElementById('view-landing');
  const isLandingVisible = !!landingView && !landingView.classList.contains('hidden');
  if (!isLandingVisible) {
    bubble.classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }

  try {
    const orders = await serverCall('getRecentOrdersPublic');
    
    if (orders && orders.length > 0) {
      let index = 0;

      const showNext = () => {
        const o = orders[index];
        content.innerHTML = `
          <span class="font-semibold">${o.text}</span><br>
          <span class="text-emerald-600 text-xs">${o.time}</span>
        `;
        bubble.classList.remove('hidden');
        index = (index + 1) % orders.length;
      };

      showNext(); // tampilkan pertama
      clearInterval(window.bubbleInterval);
      window.bubbleInterval = setInterval(showNext, 4000); // ganti setiap 4 detik

      // Klik bubble → langsung cek status
      bubble.style.cursor = 'pointer';
      bubble.onclick = () => {
        const o = orders[index === 0 ? orders.length - 1 : index - 1];
      };
    } else {
      bubble.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Promo bubble error:", e);
    bubble.classList.add('hidden');
  }
};



    
// ==================== HANDLE PUBLIC ORDER + DEADLINE (FIXED) ====================
window.handlePublicOrder = async function(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  showLoading(true, "Mengirim pesanan...");

  const fd = new FormData(e.target);

  // Perbaikan nomor HP (+62)
  let hp = String(fd.get('hp') || '').trim();
  if (hp.startsWith('+')) hp = "'" + hp;

  const filesInput = document.querySelector('input[name="files"]');
  let files = [];

  if (filesInput && filesInput.files.length > 0) {
    files = await Promise.all(Array.from(filesInput.files).map(async f => ({
      filename: f.name,
      mimeType: f.type,
      data: await fileToBase64(f)
    })));
  }

  const svc = ALL_SERVICES.find(s => s['Detail Layanan'] === fd.get('layanan'));

  const payload = {
    nama: fd.get('nama'),
    hp: hp,
    email: fd.get('email'),
    layanan: fd.get('layanan'),
    rincian: fd.get('rincian'),
    qty: fd.get('qty') || 1,
    satuan: svc ? svc.Satuan : 'Unit',
    harga: svc ? svc['Harga Publish'] : 0,
    total: (svc ? svc['Harga Publish'] : 0) * (fd.get('qty') || 1),
    files: files,
    deadline: fd.get('deadline') || ''          // ← INI YANG DITAMBAHKAN
  };

  try {
    const res = await serverCall('submitOrder', payload);
    if (res.success) {
      customAlert("✅ Berhasil", `Pesanan telah dikirim!<br>ID: <strong>#${res.id}</strong>`, "success");
      e.target.reset();
      hideOrderModal();
    } else {
      customAlert("❌ Gagal", res.message || "Terjadi kesalahan", "error");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(err);
    customAlert("❌ Error", "Gagal mengirim pesanan", "error");
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};
    // ==================== DASHBOARD: ORDERS MANAGEMENT ====================
    window.filterOrdersByStatus = function(status) {
      CURRENT_ORDER_TAB = status;
      document.querySelectorAll('.order-tab').forEach(tab => tab.classList.remove('active'));
      const activeTab = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.order-tab')).find(tab => tab.getAttribute('onclick').includes(`'${status}'`));
      if (activeTab) activeTab.classList.add('active');
      filterOrdersFull();
    }

    window.filterOrdersFull = function(query = '') {
      const searchBox = document.getElementById('search-orders');
      const q = (searchBox ? searchBox.value.toLowerCase().trim() : query);
      if (!DASHBOARD_CACHE || !DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders) return;
      
      const f = DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.filter(o => {
        const matchesQuery = (o['NAMA PELANGGAN']||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (o['ID PESANAN']||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (o.LAYANAN||'').toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchesStatus = CURRENT_ORDER_TAB === 'all' || o['TAHAPAN PESANAN'] === CURRENT_ORDER_TAB;
        return matchesQuery && matchesStatus;
      });
      if (typeof renderOrdersKPI === 'function') renderOrdersKPI(f);
      renderOrdersFull(f);
    }

    window.renderOrdersKPI = function(orders = []) {
      const box = document.getElementById('orders-kpi-cards');
      if (!box) return;
      const total = orders.length;
      const antrean = orders.filter(o => o['TAHAPAN PESANAN'] === 'Antrean').length;
      const proses = orders.filter(o => o['TAHAPAN PESANAN'] === 'Proses').length;
      const selesai = orders.filter(o => o['TAHAPAN PESANAN'] === 'Selesai').length;
      box.innerHTML = `
        <div class="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border"><p class="text-[10px] uppercase font-black text-slate-400">Total</p><h4 class="text-xl font-black text-slate-800">${total}</h4></div>
        <div class="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-blue-50 border border-blue-100"><p class="text-[10px] uppercase font-black text-blue-400">Antrean</p><h4 class="text-xl font-black text-blue-700">${antrean}</h4></div>
        <div class="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-100"><p class="text-[10px] uppercase font-black text-amber-400">Proses</p><h4 class="text-xl font-black text-amber-700">${proses}</h4></div>
        <div class="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100"><p class="text-[10px] uppercase font-black text-emerald-400">Selesai</p><h4 class="text-xl font-black text-emerald-700">${selesai}</h4></div>
      `;
    }

   window.renderOrdersFull = function(allOrders) {
  const list = document.getElementById('orders-full-list');
  if (!list) return;

  const isAdmin = String(CURRENT_USER.Role).toLowerCase() === 'admin';
  const isWorker = !isAdmin;

  // Pagination
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(allOrders.length / ORDERS_PER_PAGE) || 1;
  currentOrdersPage = Math.min(Math.max(1, currentOrdersPage), totalPages);
  const start = (currentOrdersPage - 1) * ORDERS_PER_PAGE;
  const paginated = allOrders.slice(start, start + ORDERS_PER_PAGE);

  list.innerHTML = paginated.map(o => {
    const id = o['ID PESANAN'];
    const fin = calculateOrderFinance(o);
    const canEdit = isAdmin || (isWorker && o.WORKER === CURRENT_USER.Username);
    const status = window.safeGet(o, 'TAHAPAN PESANAN');
    const statusClass = String(status || '').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '-');
    const workerAssigned = window.safeGet(o, 'WORKER');
    const canClaim = isWorker && (!workerAssigned || workerAssigned.trim() === "" || workerAssigned === "0") && status === 'Antrean';

    // Format Deadline
    let deadlineText = 'Belum diatur';
    if (o.DEADLINE) {
      const d = new Date(o.DEADLINE);
      deadlineText = d.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
    }

    // ==================== BADGE LAMA WAKTU PENGERJAAN (LIVE & MODERN) ====================
    let durationBadge = '';
    if ((status === 'Proses' || status === 'Selesai') && o['WAKTU DITERIMA WORKER']) {
      const duration = calculateWorkingDuration(o['WAKTU DITERIMA WORKER'], status === 'Selesai' ? o['WAKTU SELESAI'] : null);
      durationBadge = `
        <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-500 to-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded-3xl shadow-inner">
          <i data-lucide="clock" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
          ${status === 'Selesai' ? ('Final • ' + duration) : duration}
        </span>`;
    }

    return `
      <div class="order-row-modern flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4 relative shadow-sm">
        <input type="checkbox" class="lc-checkbox absolute top-4 left-4" onchange="toggleOrderSelection('${id}', this.checked)">
        
        <div class="flex-1">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
            <h4 class="font-black">#${id}</h4>
            <span class="status-pill status-${statusClass}">${status}</span>
            ${o.WORKER ? `<span class="text-xs bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full">👷 ${o.WORKER}</span>` : ''}
            ${durationBadge}
          </div>
          <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 font-medium">${o['NAMA PELANGGAN']}</p>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">Layanan: ${o.LAYANAN}</p>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">Deadline: <span class="font-medium">${deadlineText}</span></p>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">Total: ${formatIDR(fin.net)}</p>
        </div>

        <!-- TOMBOL ACTION — SERAGAM -->
	        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 w-full md:w-auto mt-4 md:mt-0">

          ${CURRENT_USER && String(CURRENT_USER.Role).toLowerCase() === 'worker' && status === 'Antrean' ? `
	            <button onclick="startProsesOrder('${id}')" 
	                    class="order-action-btn bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white transition-all active:scale-95">
	              <i data-lucide="play-circle" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> Proses
	            </button>` : ''}

          ${CURRENT_USER && String(CURRENT_USER.Role).toLowerCase() === 'worker' && status !== 'Selesai' ? `
	            <button onclick="showTransferModal('${id}')" 
	                    class="order-action-btn bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white transition-all active:scale-95">
	              <i data-lucide="users" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> Serahkan
	            </button>` : ''}

	          <button onclick="openOrderDetail('${id}')" 
	                  class="order-action-btn bg-indigo-50 hover:bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 transition-colors">
	            <i data-lucide="eye" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> Detail
	          </button>

          ${canClaim ? `
	            <button onclick="claimOrder('${id}')" 
	                    class="order-action-btn bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white transition-all active:scale-95">
	              <i data-lucide="hand" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> Ambil
	            </button>` : ''}

          ${canEdit ? `
	            <button onclick="openOrderUpdate('${id}')" 
	                    class="order-action-btn bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 transition-colors">
	              <i data-lucide="edit-3" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> Edit
	            </button>` : ''}

          ${isAdmin ? `
	            <button onclick="handleDeleteOrder('${id}')" 
	                    class="order-action-btn bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-700 transition-colors">
	              <i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> Hapus
	            </button>` : ''}
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }).join('');

  // Pagination Bar Modern
  let paginationHTML = `
    <div class="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2 mt-8 bg-white border rounded-2xl p-3">
      <button onclick="currentOrdersPage=${currentOrdersPage-1};filterOrdersFull()" ${currentOrdersPage===1?'disabled class="opacity-40 pointer-events-none"':''} class="px-4 py-2 border rounded-xl text-xs md:text-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-1">‹ Sebelumnya</button>`;
  
  for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
    paginationHTML += i === currentOrdersPage 
      ? `<button class="w-9 h-9 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-sm">${i}</button>` 
      : `<button onclick="currentOrdersPage=${i};filterOrdersFull()" class="w-9 h-9 border hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold">${i}</button>`;
  }
  
  paginationHTML += `
      <button onclick="currentOrdersPage=${currentOrdersPage+1};filterOrdersFull()" ${currentOrdersPage===totalPages?'disabled class="opacity-40 pointer-events-none"':''} class="px-4 py-2 border rounded-xl text-xs md:text-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-1">Selanjutnya ›</button>
    </div>`;
  
  list.innerHTML += paginationHTML;

  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

 window.openOrderModal = function(id = null) {
  const o = id ? DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.find(x => x['ID PESANAN'] === id) : null;
  const workers = DASHBOARD_CACHE.users.filter(u => String(u.Role).toLowerCase() === 'worker');
  const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
  
  body.innerHTML = `
    <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6">${id ? 'Edit Pesanan' : 'Tambah Pesanan Manual'}</h3>
    <form onsubmit="handleSaveOrder(event)" class="space-y-6">
      <!-- Pelanggan -->
      <div class="relative">
        <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Nama Pelanggan</label>
	        <input type="text" id="admin-order-nama" value="${o ? o['NAMA PELANGGAN'] : ''}"
	               required class="w-full p-5 rounded-3xl border focus:border-[#7c3aed]"
	               onkeyup="showCustomerSuggestions(this.value)" placeholder="Ketik nama pelanggan...">
        <div id="customer-suggestions" class="hidden"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">No. Handphone</label>
	          <input type="tel" id="admin-order-hp" value="${o ? o['NO HANDPHONE'] : ''}" required class="w-full p-5 rounded-3xl border focus:border-[#7c3aed]">
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">Email</label>
	          <input type="email" id="admin-order-email" value="${o ? o['EMAIL'] : ''}" required class="w-full p-5 rounded-3xl border focus:border-[#7c3aed]">
        </div>
      </div>
      <!-- Layanan -->
      <div class="space-y-3">
        <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end gap-3">
          <div class="flex-1">
            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">Cari & Pilih Layanan</label>
            <div class="relative">
              <i data-lucide="search" class="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-slate-400"></i>
              <input type="text" id="admin-order-svc-search" placeholder="Ketik nama layanan..." onfocus="filterAdminServiceOptions(this.value)" oninput="filterAdminServiceOptions(this.value)" class="w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 focus:border-[#7c3aed] outline-none text-sm sm:text-base bg-slate-50">
            </div>
            <p class="text-[11px] sm:text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">💡 Smart search: ketik nama/deskripsi layanan, lalu klik hasil untuk auto-fill harga, fee, dan satuan.</p>
            <div id="admin-order-svc-results" class="hidden mt-2 max-h-56 overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-indigo-100 bg-white shadow-lg"></div>
          </div>
          <button type="button" onclick="openQuickAddServiceModal()"
                  class="px-5 py-4 bg-indigo-100 hover:bg-indigo-200 text-indigo-700 rounded-2xl font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm">
            <i data-lucide="plus"></i> Tambah Layanan
          </button>
        </div>

	        <select id="admin-order-svc" onchange="updateAdminPrice('service');syncAdminServiceSearchFromSelect()" 
	                required class="w-full p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 focus:border-[#7c3aed] outline-none text-sm sm:text-base font-medium bg-white">
          <option value="">Pilih layanan...</option>
          ${DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices.map(s => {
            const nama = s['Detail Layanan'] || s['ID LAYANAN'] || 'Layanan';
            const harga = Number(s['Harga Publish']) || 0;
            const hargaFormatted = harga.toLocaleString('id-ID');
            const feeA = Number(s['fee Admin(%)']) || 0;
            const feeP = Number(s['fee perusahaan(%)']) || 0;
            const satuan = s.Satuan || 'Unit';
            return `
              <option value="${nama}" 
                      data-price="${harga}"
                      data-fee-admin="${feeA}"
                      data-fee-company="${feeP}"
                      data-satuan="${satuan}"
                      ${o && o.LAYANAN === nama ? 'selected' : ''}>
                ${nama} • Rp ${hargaFormatted} • ${satuan} • A:${feeA}% | P:${feeP}%
              </option>`;
          }).join('')}
        </select>
        <div id="admin-service-info" class="hidden bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-100 text-indigo-900 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-xs sm:text-sm"></div>
      </div>

      <!-- KUANTITAS + SATUAN — BERDAMPINGAN -->
      <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">Kuantitas</label>
	          <input type="number" id="admin-order-qty" value="${o ? o.QTY || 1 : 1}" min="1" required
	                 class="w-full p-5 rounded-3xl border text-2xl font-bold" oninput="updateAdminPrice()">
        </div>
        <div class="space-y-2">
          <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600">Satuan</label>
          <select id="admin-order-satuan"
                  class="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 rounded-2xl focus:border-indigo-500 outline-none text-sm">
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Harga Jual -->
      <div>
        <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">Harga Jual ke Pelanggan (Rp)</label>
	        <input type="number" id="admin-order-harga" value="${o ? o['TOTAL HARGA'] || 0 : 0}" min="1" required
	               class="w-full p-5 rounded-3xl border text-2xl font-bold focus:border-[#7c3aed]"
	               oninput="updateAdminPrice()">
        <div id="harga-badge" class="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#7c3aed] to-[#c026d3] text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-3xl shadow-xl text-2xl font-bold">
          <span class="text-white/80 text-base">Rp</span>
          <span id="harga-jual-formatted">0</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Rincian -->
      <div class="space-y-3">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
          <label class="block text-sm font-bold">Rincian / Topik</label>
          <span class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Template cerdas berdasarkan layanan</span>
        </div>
        <div id="admin-rincian-templates" class="flex flex-wrap gap-2"></div>
	        <textarea id="admin-order-rincian" rows="3" required class="w-full p-5 rounded-3xl border focus:border-[#7c3aed]" placeholder="Pilih template atau tulis rincian manual...">${o ? o['RINCIAN PESANAN'] || '' : ''}</textarea>
      </div>

      <!-- Deadline + Worker -->
      <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">Deadline Proyek</label>
	          <input type="date" id="admin-order-deadline" value="${o ? o.DEADLINE || '' : ''}" required
	                 class="w-full p-5 rounded-3xl border focus:border-[#7c3aed]">
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">Assign ke Worker</label>
          <select id="admin-order-worker" class="w-full p-5 rounded-3xl border focus:border-[#7c3aed]">
            <option value="">Belum diassign</option>
            ${workers.map(w => `<option value="${w.Username}" ${o && o.WORKER === w.Username ? 'selected' : ''}>${w['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || w.Username}</option>`).join('')}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Diskon + Fee -->
      <div class="bg-slate-50 p-6 rounded-3xl border">
        <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">Diskon (%)</label>

                   class="w-full p-5 rounded-3xl border" oninput="updateAdminPrice()">
          </div>

            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">Fee Admin (%)</label>

          </div>

            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">Fee Perusahaan (%)</label>
            <input type="number" id="admin-order-feeperusahaan" value="15" min="0" max="100"
                   class="w-full p-5 rounded-3xl border" oninput="updateAdminPrice()">
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Pembayaran -->

        <h4 class="font-bold mb-4">Pengaturan Pembayaran</h4>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          <div>

              <option value="Unpaid Invoice/Bill">Belum Bayar</option>
              <option value="Down Payment (DP)">Down Payment (DP)</option>
              <option value="Paid Full">Lunas (Paid Full)</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">Jenis Pembayaran</label>

              <option value="QRIS" selected>QRIS</option>
              <option value="Transfer">Transfer Bank</option>
              <option value="Cash">Cash / Tunai</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div id="dp-section-manual" class="hidden mt-6 bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border">
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            <div>
              <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">DP (%)</label>

                     class="w-full p-5 rounded-3xl border" oninput="syncDPManual('percent')">

              <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2">Nominal DP (Rp)</label>
              <input type="number" id="manual-dp-nominal" value="0"
                     class="w-full p-5 rounded-3xl border" oninput="syncDPManual('nominal')">
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Ringkasan -->
      <div class="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl border text-sm space-y-3" id="perhitungan-ringkasan">
        <div class="flex justify-between"><span>Gross</span><span id="summary-gross" class="font-bold">Rp 0</span></div>
        <div class="flex justify-between text-red-600"><span>Diskon</span><span id="summary-diskon" class="font-bold">- Rp 0</span></div>
        <div class="flex justify-between font-bold border-t pt-3"><span>Net</span><span id="summary-net" class="text-xl text-emerald-600">Rp 0</span></div>
        <div class="flex justify-between text-amber-600"><span>Fee Admin</span><span id="summary-feeadmin" class="font-bold">Rp 0</span></div>
        <div class="flex justify-between text-orange-600"><span>Fee Perusahaan</span><span id="summary-feeperusahaan" class="font-bold">Rp 0</span></div>

      <button type="submit" class="w-full py-6 gradient-primary text-white rounded-3xl font-bold text-xl shadow-lg">
        Simpan Pesanan
      </button>

  lucide.createIcons();

  // ==================== FIX UTAMA: LOAD SATUAN & AUTO FILL ====================

    loadUniqueSatuan();           // ← Ini yang sebelumnya hilang

    if (typeof syncAdminServiceSearchFromSelect === 'function') syncAdminServiceSearchFromSelect();
    if (typeof renderAdminRincianTemplates === 'function') renderAdminRincianTemplates(document.getElementById('admin-order-svc')?.value || '');
    toggleDPSection();
  }, 150);
};
    window.openOrderDetail = function(id) {
  const o = DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.find(x => x['ID PESANAN'] === id);
  if (!o) return customAlert("Error", "Pesanan tidak ditemukan");

  const fin = calculateOrderFinance(o);
  const isAdmin = String(CURRENT_USER.Role).toLowerCase() === 'admin';
  const hasFinalFiles = !!String(o['FOLDER DOKUMEN FINAL'] || '').trim();
  const isPaidFull = String(o['STATUS PEMBAYARAN'] || '').toLowerCase().includes('paid full');
  const statusClassDetail = String(o['TAHAPAN PESANAN'] || '').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '-');
  const reviewWAButton = hasFinalFiles
    ? '<button onclick="sendOrderFileLinkWA(\'' + id + '\', \'review\')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-violet-50 border border-violet-200 text-violet-700 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center gap-1"><i data-lucide="link-2" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> Kirim Link Review</button>'
    : '';
  const downloadWAButton = (hasFinalFiles && isPaidFull)
    ? '<button onclick="sendOrderFileLinkWA(\'' + id + '\', \'download\')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center gap-1"><i data-lucide="download" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> Kirim Link Download</button>'
    : '';

  let deadlineDisplay = 'Belum diatur';
  if (o.DEADLINE) {
    const d = new Date(o.DEADLINE);
    deadlineDisplay = d.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { 
      weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' 
    });
  }

  let workerInfoHTML = '';
  if (isAdmin && o.WORKER) {
    const workerData = DASHBOARD_CACHE.users.find(u => u.Username === o.WORKER) || {};
    workerInfoHTML = `
      <div class="mt-8 bg-slate-50 p-6 rounded-2xl border">
        <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 mb-4">INFORMASI WORKER</p>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-6 text-sm">
          <div><span class="text-slate-500 text-xs">Username</span><br><strong>${o.WORKER}</strong></div>
          <div><span class="text-slate-500 text-xs">Nama Lengkap</span><br><strong>${workerData['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || '-'}</strong></div>
          <div><span class="text-slate-500 text-xs">No. HP Worker</span><br>
            <strong class="flex items-center gap-2">
              ${workerData['No HP'] || '-'}
              <button onclick="copyText('${workerData['No HP'] || ''}')" class="text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700 text-xs px-3 py-1 bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-xl">📋 Copy</button>
            </strong>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }

  const html = `
    <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-indigo-900 to-purple-900 text-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl flex justify-between items-start">
        <div>
          <p class="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-white/70 font-bold">Detail Pesanan</p>
          <h3 class="text-3xl font-black mt-1">#${id}</h3>
          <p class="font-semibold text-xl mt-2">${o['NAMA PELANGGAN']}</p>
          <p class="text-white/80 text-sm">${o['EMAIL'] || '-'}</p>
        </div>
        <span class="status-pill status-${statusClassDetail} text-sm px-5 py-2">${o['TAHAPAN PESANAN']}</span>
      </div>

      <!-- NO HP PELANGGAN -->
      <div class="flex justify-end">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-3 bg-white px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl border shadow-sm">
          <span class="text-xs text-slate-500">No. WhatsApp</span>
          <p class="font-semibold">${o['NO HANDPHONE'] || '-'}</p>
          <button onclick="copyText('${o['NO HANDPHONE'] || ''}')" 
                  class="text-xs px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-50 hover:bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-1">
            📋 Copy
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- DEADLINE -->
      <div class="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border shadow-sm">
        <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 mb-2">DEADLINE PROYEK</p>
        <p class="font-bold text-2xl">${deadlineDisplay}</p>
      </div>

      <!-- RINCIAN PESANAN -->
      <div class="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border shadow-sm">
        <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 mb-3">RINCIAN PESANAN</p>
        <p class="text-slate-700 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap">${o['RINCIAN PESANAN'] || 'Tidak ada deskripsi tambahan.'}</p>
      </div>

      <!-- DOKUMEN AWAL & FINAL -->
      <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border shadow-sm space-y-3">
          <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400">DOKUMEN AWAL (Upload + Download + ZIP)</p>
          <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <button onclick="generateInitialFolder('${id}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center gap-1"><i data-lucide="folder-plus" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Generate Folder</button>
            <button onclick="refreshOrderDocs('${id}', 'initial')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl">Refresh</button>
          </div>
          <input type="file" id="upload-initial-${id}" multiple class="w-full text-xs border rounded-xl p-2">
          <button onclick="uploadOrderDocs('${id}', 'initial')" class="w-full py-2.5 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 font-bold rounded-xl text-xs">Upload Dokumen Awal</button>
          <div id="order-docs-initial-${id}" class="text-xs text-slate-500">Memuat dokumen awal...</div>
        </div>

        <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border shadow-sm space-y-3">
          <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400">DOKUMEN FINAL (View-only jika belum lunas)</p>
          <input type="file" id="upload-final-${id}" multiple class="w-full text-xs border rounded-xl p-2">
          <button onclick="uploadOrderDocs('${id}', 'final')" class="w-full py-2.5 bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-200 text-indigo-700 font-bold rounded-xl text-xs">Upload Dokumen Final</button>
          <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            ${reviewWAButton}
            ${downloadWAButton}
          </div>
          <div id="order-docs-final-${id}" class="text-xs text-slate-500">Memuat dokumen final...</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      ${workerInfoHTML}

      <!-- RINCIAN FINANSIAL -->
      <div class="bg-slate-50 p-6 rounded-2xl border shadow-sm">
        <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 mb-4">RINCIAN FINANSIAL</p>
        <div class="space-y-3 text-sm">
          <div class="flex justify-between"><span>Gross</span><strong>${formatIDR(fin.gross)}</strong></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between"><span>Diskon</span><strong class="text-red-500">-${formatIDR(fin.gross - fin.net)}</strong></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between border-b pb-3"><span>Net Transaksi</span><strong class="text-indigo-600">${formatIDR(fin.net)}</strong></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between"><span>Fee Admin Pusat</span><strong class="text-amber-600">${formatIDR(fin.feeAdmin)}</strong></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between"><span>Fee Perusahaan</span><strong class="text-orange-600">${formatIDR(fin.feeCompany)}</strong></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between pt-3 text-emerald-700"><span><strong>Worker Net</strong></span><strong class="text-xl">${formatIDR(fin.workerNet)}</strong></div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-3 pt-2">
        <button onclick="openOrderUpdate('${id}')" class="flex-1 py-3.5 gradient-primary text-white font-bold rounded-2xl shadow-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="edit-3" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Update Status / Deadline</button>
        <button onclick="openInvoiceExportModal('${id}')" class="flex-1 py-3.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-white font-bold rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="file-down" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Export Invoice</button>
        <button onclick="closeModal()" class="px-8 py-3.5 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 font-bold rounded-2xl">Tutup</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  document.getElementById('modal-content-body').innerHTML = html;
  document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
  refreshOrderDocs(id, 'initial');
  refreshOrderDocs(id, 'final');
};

    window.openOrderUpdate = function(id) {
      currentUpdateId = id;
      const o = DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.find(x => x['ID PESANAN'] === id);
      const fin = calculateOrderFinance(o);
      const isWorker = String(CURRENT_USER.Role).toLowerCase() !== 'admin' && o.WORKER === CURRENT_USER.Username;

      const html = `
        <div class="space-y-6">
          <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6">Update Pesanan #${id}</h3>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <select id="upd-status" class="w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl outline-none text-sm">
              <option value="Antrean" ${o['TAHAPAN PESANAN']==='Antrean'?'selected':''}>Antrean</option>
              <option value="Proses" ${o['TAHAPAN PESANAN']==='Proses'?'selected':''}>Proses</option>
              <option value="Revisi" ${o['TAHAPAN PESANAN']==='Revisi'?'selected':''}>Revisi</option>
              <option value="Penagihan Pembayaran Akhir" ${o['TAHAPAN PESANAN']==='Penagihan Pembayaran Akhir'?'selected':''}>Penagihan Pembayaran Akhir</option>
              <option value="Selesai" ${o['TAHAPAN PESANAN']==='Selesai'?'selected':''}>Selesai</option>
              <option value="Batal" ${o['TAHAPAN PESANAN']==='Batal'?'selected':''}>Batal</option>
            </select>
            <select id="upd-pay" onchange="toggleDPField()" class="w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl outline-none text-sm">
              <option value="Unpaid Invoice/Bill" ${o['STATUS PEMBAYARAN']==='Unpaid Invoice/Bill'?'selected':''}>Belum Bayar</option>
              <option value="Down Payment (DP)" ${o['STATUS PEMBAYARAN']==='Down Payment (DP)'?'selected':''}>Down Payment (DP)</option>
              <option value="Paid Full" ${o['STATUS PEMBAYARAN']==='Paid Full'?'selected':''}>Lunas</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div id="dp-section" class="hidden bg-slate-50 p-6 rounded-2xl border">
            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-4 text-slate-700">Jumlah Down Payment</label>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label class="block text-xs font-bold mb-2 text-slate-600">DP (% dari Net)</label>
                <input type="number" id="upd-dp-percent" value="${o.DP || 0}" min="0" max="100" step="0.01" oninput="syncDP('percent')" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-white outline-none text-lg font-semibold">
              </div>
              <div>
                <label class="block text-xs font-bold mb-2 text-slate-600">Nominal DP (Rp)</label>
                <input type="number" id="upd-dp-nominal" value="${fin.dpAmount || 0}" min="0" step="1000" oninput="syncDP('nominal')" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-white outline-none text-lg font-semibold">
              </div>
            </div>
            <p id="dp-info" class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-3"></p>
          </div>
          <textarea id="upd-note" placeholder="Catatan internal..." class="w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl h-24 outline-none text-sm">${o['CATATAN WORKER'] || ''}</textarea>
          ${isWorker ? `
            <div class="mt-4">
              <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Upload Dokumen Final</label>
              <input type="file" id="final-files" multiple accept=".pdf,.doc,.docx,.xls,.xlsx" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none">
            </div>
          ` : ''}
          <div class="flex gap-4">
            <button onclick="sendConfirmationWA('${id}')" class="flex-1 py-4 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg"><i data-lucide="send"></i> Kirim Konfirmasi WA</button>
            <button onclick="saveOrderUpdates('${id}')" class="flex-1 py-4 gradient-primary text-white font-bold rounded-xl shadow-lg">SIMPAN UPDATE</button>
            <button onclick="closeModal()" class="px-8 py-4 bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 font-bold rounded-xl">BATAL</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
      document.getElementById('modal-content-body').innerHTML = html;
      document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
      if(window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
      toggleDPField();
    }

    window.saveOrderUpdates = async function(id) {
      showLoading(true); 
      try {
        const filesInput = document.getElementById('final-files');
        let files = [];
        if (filesInput && filesInput.files.length > 0) {
          files = await Promise.all(Array.from(filesInput.files).map(async f => ({ filename: f.name, mimeType: f.type, data: await fileToBase64(f) })));
        }
        
        let statusVal = document.getElementById('upd-status').value;
        let payVal = document.getElementById('upd-pay').value;

        const updates = { status: statusVal, payment: payVal, note: document.getElementById('upd-note').value };
        
        if (payVal === 'Down Payment (DP)') {
           updates.dp = Number(document.getElementById('upd-dp-percent').value) || 0;
        }

        if (files.length > 0) { updates.finalFiles = files; updates.status = 'Penagihan Pembayaran Akhir'; }
        
        await serverCall('updateOrder', id, updates); 
        closeModal(); refreshData(); customAlert("Berhasil", "Pesanan berhasil diupdate");
      } catch(e) { console.error(e); customAlert("Error", "Gagal menyimpan update"); } finally { showLoading(false); } 
    }

    window.handleDeleteOrder = async function(id) {
      if (!confirm("Hapus pesanan ini?")) return;
      showLoading(true);
      try { await serverCall('deleteOrder', id); refreshData(); } catch (e) {} finally { showLoading(false); }
    }

    window.openTransferModal = function(orderId) {
      const order = DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.find(o => o['ID PESANAN'] === orderId);
      if (!order) return;
