      const otherWorkers = DASHBOARD_CACHE.users.filter(u => String(u.Role).toLowerCase() === 'worker' && u.Username !== CURRENT_USER.Username);
      let options = otherWorkers.map(w => `<option value="${w.Username}">${w['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || w.Username}</option>`).join('');
      const html = `
        <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6">Serahkan Pesanan #${orderId}</h3>
        <p class="mb-6 text-slate-600">Pesanan ini saat ini dikerjakan oleh <strong>${CURRENT_USER.Username}</strong></p>
        <div class="mb-6">
          <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Pilih Worker Tujuan</label>
          <select id="transfer-worker-select" class="w-full p-4 rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none">
            <option value="">-- Pilih Worker --</option>${options}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="flex gap-3">
          <button onclick="closeModal()" class="flex-1 py-4 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-bold rounded-2xl">Batal</button>
          <button onclick="confirmTransfer('${orderId}')" class="flex-1 py-4 gradient-primary text-white font-bold rounded-2xl">Serahkan Pesanan</button>
        </div>
      `;
      document.getElementById('modal-content-body').innerHTML = html;
      document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    window.confirmTransfer = async function(orderId) {
      const newWorker = document.getElementById('transfer-worker-select').value;
      if (!newWorker) { customAlert("Pilih Worker", "Silakan pilih worker tujuan"); return; }
      if (!confirm(`Serahkan pesanan #${orderId} ke ${newWorker}?`)) return;
      showLoading(true);
      try { await serverCall('transferOrder', orderId, newWorker); closeModal(); refreshData(); customAlert("✅ Berhasil", `Pesanan #${orderId} telah diserahkan ke ${newWorker}`); } catch(e) { customAlert("Gagal", "Terjadi kesalahan saat transfer"); } finally { showLoading(false); }
    }

    window.generateInitialFolder = async function(orderId) {
      if (!confirm("Buat folder awal kosong untuk pesanan ini?")) return;
      showLoading(true);
      try {
        const result = await serverCall('generateInitialFolder', orderId);
        if (result.success) { customAlert("✅ Berhasil", "Folder awal telah dibuat"); refreshOrderDocs(orderId, 'initial'); refreshData(); } 
        else { customAlert("Gagal", result.message); }
      } catch(e) { customAlert("Error", "Gagal membuat folder"); } finally { showLoading(false); }
    }

    window.refreshOrderDocs = async function(orderId, docType = 'initial') {
      const box = document.getElementById(`order-docs-${docType}-${orderId}`);
      if (!box) return;
      box.innerHTML = '<p class="text-slate-400">Memuat file...</p>';
      try {
        const res = await serverCall('getOrderDocumentFiles', orderId, docType);
        if (!res.success) {
          box.innerHTML = `<p class="text-rose-500">${res.message || 'Gagal memuat file.'}</p>`;
          return;
        }
        const order = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders || []).find(x => x['ID PESANAN'] === orderId) || {};
        const isPaidFull = String(order['STATUS PEMBAYARAN'] || '').toLowerCase().includes('paid full');
        const files = res.files || [];
        const canDownload = docType === 'initial' || isPaidFull;

        if (files.length === 0) {
          box.innerHTML = `<p class="text-slate-400 italic">Belum ada file ${docType === 'final' ? 'final' : 'awal'}.</p>`;
          return;
        }

        const folderActions = `<div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <a href="${res.folderUrl}" target="_blank" class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-bold text-[10px]">Buka Folder</a>
          <button onclick="copyText('${String(res.folderUrl || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-bold text-[10px]">Copy Link Folder</button>
        </div>`;

        const listHtml = files.map(f => {
          const safeName = String(f.name).replace(/'/g, "\\'");
          const encodedFileName = encodeURIComponent(String(f.name || 'Dokumen'));
          const reviewViewerUrl = `https://docs.google.com/gview?embedded=1&url=${encodeURIComponent(f.downloadUrl || f.url || '')}`;
          const safeReview = String(reviewViewerUrl).replace(/'/g, "\\'");
          const safeDownload = String(f.downloadUrl || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'");
          const reviewOnlyFlag = (docType === 'final' && !canDownload) ? 'true' : 'false';
          const previewBtn = `<button onclick="openOrderDocReview('${safeReview}', decodeURIComponent('${encodedFileName}'), ${reviewOnlyFlag})" class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 font-bold">Lihat</button>`;
          const reviewLinkBtn = `<button onclick="copyText('${safeReview}')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-violet-50 text-violet-700 font-bold">Copy Link Review</button>`;
          const downloadBtn = canDownload
            ? `<a href="${f.downloadUrl}" target="_blank" class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-bold">Download</a><button onclick="copyText('${safeDownload}')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-bold">Copy Link Download</button>`
            : `<span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 font-bold">Terkunci sampai lunas</span>`;
          return `<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border rounded-xl p-2">
            <div class="min-w-0">
              <p class="font-semibold text-slate-700 truncate">${f.name}</p>
              <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400">${f.sizeKb || 0} KB • ${f.updatedAt || '-'}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-1 text-[10px]">${previewBtn}${reviewLinkBtn}${downloadBtn}</div>
          </div>`;
        }).join('');

        const zipBtn = (files.length > 1 && canDownload)
          ? `<button onclick="downloadOrderDocsZip('${orderId}', '${docType}')" class="w-full mt-2 py-2 bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold">Download Semua (.ZIP)</button>`
          : '';
        const note = (!canDownload && docType === 'final')
          ? `<p class="text-[10px] text-amber-700 mt-2">Mode review aktif: pelanggan hanya bisa lihat dokumen final (watermark), download dikunci sampai status pembayaran Lunas.</p>`
          : '';
        box.innerHTML = folderActions + listHtml + zipBtn + note;
      } catch (e) {
        box.innerHTML = '<p class="text-rose-500">Gagal memuat file dokumen.</p>';
      }
    };

    window.uploadOrderDocs = async function(orderId, docType = 'initial') {
      const input = document.getElementById(`upload-${docType}-${orderId}`);
      if (!input || !input.files || input.files.length === 0) return customAlert('Info', 'Pilih file dulu sebelum upload.');
      showLoading(true, 'Upload dokumen...');
      try {
        const files = await Promise.all(Array.from(input.files).map(async f => ({ filename: f.name, mimeType: f.type, data: await fileToBase64(f) })));
        const res = await serverCall('uploadOrderDocumentFiles', orderId, docType, files);
        if (!res.success) throw new Error(res.message || 'Upload gagal');
        customAlert('✅ Berhasil', `${files.length} file berhasil diupload.`);
        input.value = '';
        refreshOrderDocs(orderId, docType);
      } catch (e) {
        customAlert('❌ Gagal', e.message || 'Tidak bisa upload dokumen.');
      } finally {
        showLoading(false);
      }
    };

    window.downloadOrderDocsZip = async function(orderId, docType = 'initial') {
      showLoading(true, 'Membuat file ZIP...');
      try {
        const res = await serverCall('downloadOrderDocumentZip', orderId, docType);
        if (!res.success) throw new Error(res.message || 'Gagal membuat zip');
        window.open(res.url || res.fileUrl, '_blank');
      } catch (e) {
        customAlert('❌ Gagal', e.message || 'Tidak bisa membuat ZIP.');
      } finally {
        showLoading(false);
      }
    };

    window.openOrderDocReview = function(reviewUrl, fileName = 'Dokumen', isReviewOnly = false) {
      const url = String(reviewUrl || '').trim();
      if (!url) {
        customAlert('⚠️ Link kosong', `Link review untuk ${fileName} tidak tersedia.`);
        return;
      }
      const w = window.open('', '_blank');
      if (!w) return;

      const safeName = String(fileName || 'Dokumen')
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
        .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
        .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');

      const label = isReviewOnly
        ? 'Preview review-only aktif. Download tetap dikunci sampai status pembayaran lunas.'
        : 'Preview dokumen aktif. Anda tetap bisa gunakan tombol download/copy link di dashboard.';
      const openDirectBtn = isReviewOnly ? '' : `<a href="${url}" target="_blank" style="color:#93c5fd;text-decoration:underline;font-weight:700;">Buka langsung</a>`;
      const sandboxAttr = isReviewOnly ? 'sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin"' : '';

      w.document.title = `Preview - ${fileName}`;
      w.document.body.style.margin = '0';
      w.document.body.style.background = '#0f172a';
      w.document.body.style.fontFamily = 'Arial, sans-serif';
      w.document.body.innerHTML = `
        <div style="padding:10px 14px;background:#0b1733;color:#fff;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;align-items:center;">
          <span>${label}</span>
          ${openDirectBtn}
        </div>
        <div style="position:fixed;inset:42px 0 0 0;">
          <iframe ${sandboxAttr} src="${url}" style="border:0;width:100%;height:100%;background:#fff;"></iframe>
          <div style="position:absolute;inset:0;pointer-events:none;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-content:space-around;justify-content:space-around;opacity:.13;color:#ef4444;font-weight:900;font-size:18px;transform:rotate(-20deg);">
            <span>REVIEW ONLY - ${safeName} - LENTERA CENDEKIA</span>
            <span>REVIEW ONLY - ${safeName} - LENTERA CENDEKIA</span>
            <span>REVIEW ONLY - ${safeName} - LENTERA CENDEKIA</span>
            <span>REVIEW ONLY - ${safeName} - LENTERA CENDEKIA</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
      if (isReviewOnly) {
        customAlert('Mode Review Aktif', 'Dokumen dibuka dengan watermark overlay.');
      }
    };

    // ==================== DP & FORM SYNC ====================
    window.toggleDPFieldManual = function() {
      const status = document.getElementById('manual-pay-status').value;
      const section = document.getElementById('dp-section-manual');
      if (status === 'Down Payment (DP)') { section.classList.remove('hidden'); syncDPManual('percent'); } 
      else { section.classList.add('hidden'); }
    }


    window.toggleDPField = function() {
      const payStatus = document.getElementById('upd-pay').value;
      const dpSection = document.getElementById('dp-section');
      if (payStatus === 'Down Payment (DP)') { dpSection.classList.remove('hidden'); syncDP('percent'); } 
      else { dpSection.classList.add('hidden'); }
    }

    window.syncDP = function(from) {
      const percentInput = document.getElementById('upd-dp-percent');
      const nominalInput = document.getElementById('upd-dp-nominal');
      const info = document.getElementById('dp-info');
      const order = DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.find(o => o['ID PESANAN'] === currentUpdateId);
      if (!order) return;
      const gross = Number(order['TOTAL HARGA']) || 0;
      const discount = Number(order.DISCOUNT) || 0;
      const net = gross * (1 - discount / 100);
      if (from === 'percent') { const p = Number(percentInput.value) || 0; nominalInput.value = Math.round(net * p / 100); } 
      else { const n = Number(nominalInput.value) || 0; percentInput.value = net > 0 ? Math.round((n / net) * 100 * 100) / 100 : 0; }
      info.innerHTML = `Net setelah diskon: <b>${formatIDR(net)}</b>`;
    }
window.updateAdminPrice = function(source = 'input') {
  const select = document.getElementById('admin-order-svc');
  if (!select || !DASHBOARD_CACHE?.allServices) return;

  const layananTerpilih = select.value.trim();
  const hargaInput = document.getElementById('admin-order-harga');
  const feeAdminInput = document.getElementById('admin-order-feeadmin');
  const feePerusahaanInput = document.getElementById('admin-order-feeperusahaan');
  const satuanInput = document.getElementById('admin-order-satuan');
  const shouldAutoFill = source === 'service' || source === 'init';

  // Auto-fill hanya saat pilih layanan / inisialisasi form.
  // Saat user edit harga/fee manual, nilainya tidak akan ditimpa lagi.
  if (layananTerpilih && shouldAutoFill) {
    const svc = DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices.find(s =>
      String(s['Detail Layanan'] || s['ID LAYANAN']).trim() === layananTerpilih
    );

    if (svc) {
      if (hargaInput) hargaInput.value = Number(svc['Harga Publish']) || 0;
      if (feeAdminInput) feeAdminInput.value = Number(svc['fee Admin(%)']) || 15;
      if (feePerusahaanInput) feePerusahaanInput.value = Number(svc['fee perusahaan(%)']) || 5;
      if (satuanInput) satuanInput.value = svc.Satuan || "Unit";
    }
  }

  // Hitung ulang Total Harga
  const qtyInput = document.getElementById('admin-order-qty');
  const qty = parseFloat(qtyInput ? qtyInput.value : 1) || 1;
  const totalHarga = (Number(hargaInput ? hargaInput.value : 0) || 0) * qty;

  const totalInput = document.getElementById('admin-order-total');
  if (totalInput) totalInput.value = Math.round(totalHarga);

  // Update badge (jika ada)
  const badge = document.getElementById('harga-jual-formatted');
  if (badge) badge.textContent = totalHarga.toLocaleString('id-ID');

  // === PERHITUNGAN LENGKAP RINGKASAN ===
  const diskon = parseFloat(document.getElementById('admin-order-diskon')?.value) || 0;
  const feeAdmin = parseFloat(feeAdminInput ? feeAdminInput.value : 0) || 0;
  const feePerusahaan = parseFloat(feePerusahaanInput ? feePerusahaanInput.value : 0) || 0;

  const gross = totalHarga;
  const diskonAmount = gross * (diskon / 100);
  const net = gross - diskonAmount;
  const feeAdminAmount = net * (feeAdmin / 100);
  const feePerusahaanAmount = net * (feePerusahaan / 100);
  const workerNet = net - feeAdminAmount - feePerusahaanAmount;

  // Update semua summary
  document.getElementById('summary-gross') && (document.getElementById('summary-gross').textContent = formatIDR(gross));
  document.getElementById('summary-diskon') && (document.getElementById('summary-diskon').textContent = `- ${formatIDR(diskonAmount)}`);
  document.getElementById('summary-net') && (document.getElementById('summary-net').textContent = formatIDR(net));
  document.getElementById('summary-feeadmin') && (document.getElementById('summary-feeadmin').textContent = formatIDR(feeAdminAmount));
  document.getElementById('summary-feeperusahaan') && (document.getElementById('summary-feeperusahaan').textContent = formatIDR(feePerusahaanAmount));
  document.getElementById('summary-workernet') && (document.getElementById('summary-workernet').textContent = formatIDR(workerNet));
  if (typeof renderAdminServiceInfo === 'function') renderAdminServiceInfo();
};

window.renderAdminServiceInfo = function() {
  const select = document.getElementById('admin-order-svc');
  const info = document.getElementById('admin-service-info');
  if (!select || !info) return;
  const opt = select.options[select.selectedIndex];
  if (!opt || !opt.value) {
    info.classList.add('hidden');
    info.innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }
  const price = Number(opt.getAttribute('data-price')) || 0;
  const feeA = Number(opt.getAttribute('data-fee-admin')) || 0;
  const feeP = Number(opt.getAttribute('data-fee-company')) || 0;
  const satuan = opt.getAttribute('data-satuan') || 'Unit';
  info.classList.remove('hidden');
  info.innerHTML = `
    <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
      <span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 font-bold">Harga: ${formatIDR(price)}</span>
      <span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-violet-100 text-violet-700 font-bold">Satuan: ${satuan}</span>
      <span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 font-bold">Fee Admin: ${feeA}%</span>
      <span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 font-bold">Fee Perusahaan: ${feeP}%</span>
    </div>
  `;
};

window.syncAdminServiceSearchFromSelect = function() {
  const select = document.getElementById('admin-order-svc');
  const search = document.getElementById('admin-order-svc-search');
  const results = document.getElementById('admin-order-svc-results');
  if (!select || !search) return;
  search.value = select.value || '';
  if (results) results.classList.add('hidden');
  renderAdminServiceInfo();
  renderAdminRincianTemplates(select.value || '');
};

window.chooseAdminService = function(serviceName = '') {
  const select = document.getElementById('admin-order-svc');
  if (!select) return;
  select.value = serviceName;
  syncAdminServiceSearchFromSelect();
  updateAdminPrice('service');
};

window.getRincianTemplatesForService = function(serviceName = '') {
  const name = String(serviceName || '').toLowerCase();
  const byType = [];
  if (name.includes('skripsi') || name.includes('tesis') || name.includes('disertasi')) {
    byType.push(
      'Topik: [isi topik penelitian] | Metode: [kualitatif/kuantitatif] | Target selesai: [tanggal] | Catatan dosen: [isi catatan].',
      'Butuh bantuan BAB [2/3/4/5], fokus pada [bagian], referensi minimal [x] jurnal 5 tahun terakhir.',
      'Revisi skripsi: perbaiki [bab], benahi sitasi Mendeley, dan rapikan format sesuai pedoman kampus.'
    );
  } else if (name.includes('jurnal') || name.includes('artikel')) {
    byType.push(
      'Tema jurnal: [isi tema] | Target indeks: [Sinta/Scopus] | Gaya sitasi: [APA/IEEE] | Deadline submit: [tanggal].',
      'Parafrase + proofreading artikel [jumlah kata], fokus kurangi similarity dan rapikan grammar akademik.',
      'Butuh struktur lengkap IMRAD + abstrak bilingual + kata kunci sesuai scope jurnal.'
    );
  } else if (name.includes('statistik') || name.includes('spss') || name.includes('olah data')) {
    byType.push(
      'Dataset: [jumlah responden] | Uji diminta: [uji] | Output wajib: tabel, interpretasi, dan kesimpulan.',
      'Validasi instrumen + uji asumsi + regresi/SEM, sertakan narasi hasil siap tempel ke BAB 4.',
      'Butuh bimbingan meeting 1x untuk review output analisis dan perbaikan model.'
    );
  } else if (name.includes('ppt') || name.includes('presentasi')) {
    byType.push(
      'Buat PPT [jumlah slide] gaya [formal/modern], dominan warna [warna], include icon + infografik.',
      'Materi presentasi sidang: ringkas BAB 1-5, tambah script presentasi 7 menit.',
      'Redesign slide existing agar lebih clean, konsisten font, dan storytelling lebih kuat.'
    );
  } else {
    byType.push(
      `Layanan: ${serviceName || '[isi layanan]'} | Tujuan: [isi kebutuhan] | Output: [format file] | Deadline: [tanggal].`,
      'Brief detail: latar belakang, scope pekerjaan, format penulisan, dan contoh referensi jika ada.',
      'Prioritas pengerjaan: [cepat/normal], revisi maksimal [x] kali, komunikasi via WA.'
    );
  }
  return byType;
};

window.renderAdminRincianTemplates = function(serviceName = '') {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('admin-rincian-templates');
  if (!wrap) return;
  const templates = getRincianTemplatesForService(serviceName);
  wrap.innerHTML = templates.map((tpl, i) =>
    `<button type="button" onclick="applyAdminRincianTemplate(${i})" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 hover:bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 text-xs font-bold transition-all">Template ${i + 1}</button>`
  ).join('');
  window.ADMIN_RINCIAN_TEMPLATES = templates;
};

window.applyAdminRincianTemplate = function(index = 0) {
  const area = document.getElementById('admin-order-rincian');
  const list = window.ADMIN_RINCIAN_TEMPLATES || [];
  if (!area || !list[index]) return;
  area.value = list[index];
  area.focus();
};

window.filterAdminServiceOptions = function(query = '') {
  const select = document.getElementById('admin-order-svc');
  const results = document.getElementById('admin-order-svc-results');
  if (!select || !DASHBOARD_CACHE?.allServices) return;
  const currentValue = select.value;
  const q = String(query || '').toLowerCase().trim();

  let filtered = DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices;
  if (q) {
    filtered = DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices.filter(s => {
      const name = String(s['Detail Layanan'] || s['ID LAYANAN'] || '').toLowerCase();
      const desc = String(s.Deskripsi || '').toLowerCase();
      return name.includes(q) || desc.includes(q);
    });
  }

  let options = '<option value="">Pilih layanan...</option>';
  options += filtered.map(s => {
    const nama = s['Detail Layanan'] || s['ID LAYANAN'] || 'Layanan';
    const harga = Number(s['Harga Publish']) || 0;
    const feeA = Number(s['fee Admin(%)']) || 0;
    const feeP = Number(s['fee perusahaan(%)']) || 0;
    const satuan = s.Satuan || 'Unit';
    return `<option value="${nama}" data-price="${harga}" data-fee-admin="${feeA}" data-fee-company="${feeP}" data-satuan="${satuan}">${nama} • Rp ${harga.toLocaleString('id-ID')} • ${satuan} • A:${feeA}% | P:${feeP}%</option>`;
  }).join('');

  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    options += `<option value="" disabled>Tidak ada layanan yang cocok</option>`;
  }

  select.innerHTML = options;
  if (currentValue && Array.from(select.options).some(o => o.value === currentValue)) select.value = currentValue;
  else if (!q && currentValue) select.value = currentValue;
  renderAdminServiceInfo();

  if (!results) return;
  if (!q) {
    results.classList.add('hidden');
    results.innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }

  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    results.classList.remove('hidden');
    results.innerHTML = `<div class="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-500">Tidak ada layanan cocok dengan "<strong>${query}</strong>".</div>`;
    return;
  }

  const top = filtered.slice(0, 8);
  results.classList.remove('hidden');
  results.innerHTML = top.map(s => {
    const nama = s['Detail Layanan'] || s['ID LAYANAN'] || 'Layanan';
    const harga = Number(s['Harga Publish']) || 0;
    const satuan = s.Satuan || 'Unit';
    return `<button type="button" onclick="chooseAdminService('${String(nama).replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" class="w-full text-left px-4 py-3 hover:bg-indigo-50 border-b border-slate-100 last:border-b-0">
      <div class="font-semibold text-slate-800">${nama}</div>
      <div class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Rp ${harga.toLocaleString('id-ID')} • ${satuan}</div>
    </button>`;
  }).join('');
};
    // ==================== AUTOCOMPLETE PELANGGAN - VERSI AMAN & CANGGIH ====================
window.showCustomerSuggestions = function(query) {
  const suggestionsBox = document.getElementById('customer-suggestions');
  suggestionsBox.innerHTML = '';
  suggestionsBox.classList.add('hidden');

  if (!query || query.length < 2) return;

  const q = query.toLowerCase().trim();

  const customers = [...new Map(
    DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.map(o => [
      o['NAMA PELANGGAN'],
      {
        nama: o['NAMA PELANGGAN'] || '',
        hp: o['NO HANDPHONE'] || '',
        email: o['EMAIL'] || ''
      }
    ])
  ).values()];

  const filtered = customers.filter(c => 
    c.nama.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
    String(c.hp).toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
    c.email.toLowerCase().includes(q)
  );

  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    suggestionsBox.innerHTML = `
      <div class="no-result">
        <div class="text-left">
          <div>Tidak ditemukan pelanggan dengan kata kunci "<strong>${query}</strong>"</div>
          <p class="text-xs mt-2 text-indigo-600 font-semibold">✨ Pelanggan baru akan dibuat saat pesanan disimpan.</p>
          <button type="button" onclick="useAsNewCustomer('${query.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" class="mt-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-indigo-700">Gunakan sebagai pelanggan baru</button>
        </div>
        <button onclick="closeCustomerSuggestions(event)" style="background:none;border:none;font-size:20px;color:#64748b;cursor:pointer;padding:2px 8px;">✕</button>
      </div>`;
    suggestionsBox.classList.remove('hidden');
    return;
  }

  filtered.forEach(c => {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'suggestion-item';
    div.innerHTML = `
      <div>
        <strong>${c.nama}</strong>
        <div class="text-xs text-slate-500">${c.hp ? `📱 ${c.hp}` : ''} ${c.email ? ` • ✉️ ${c.email}` : ''}</div>
      </div>
    `;
    div.onclick = () => {
      document.getElementById('admin-order-nama').value = c.nama;
      document.getElementById('admin-order-hp').value = c.hp;
      document.getElementById('admin-order-email').value = c.email;
      suggestionsBox.classList.add('hidden');
    };
    suggestionsBox.appendChild(div);
  });

  suggestionsBox.classList.remove('hidden');
};

// Fungsi close yang sudah diperbaiki (mencegah submit form)
window.closeCustomerSuggestions = function(e) {
  if (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  }
  const box = document.getElementById('customer-suggestions');
  if (box) box.classList.add('hidden');
};

window.useAsNewCustomer = function(name = '') {
  const nameEl = document.getElementById('admin-order-nama');
  if (nameEl) nameEl.value = name;
  const hpEl = document.getElementById('admin-order-hp');
  const emailEl = document.getElementById('admin-order-email');
  if (hpEl) hpEl.value = '';
  if (emailEl) emailEl.value = '';
  closeCustomerSuggestions();
};

document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  const search = document.getElementById('admin-order-svc-search');
  const results = document.getElementById('admin-order-svc-results');
  if (!search || !results) return;
  if (!search.contains(e.target) && !results.contains(e.target)) {
    results.classList.add('hidden');
  }
});
    window.sendConfirmationWA = async function(id) {
      if (!confirm("Kirim pesan konfirmasi WhatsApp ke pelanggan ini?\n\n(Template sudah otomatis lengkap)")) return;
      showLoading(true);
      try { const res = await serverCall('sendOrderConfirmation', id); if (res.success) { customAlert("✅ Berhasil", res.message); } else { customAlert("❌ Gagal", res.message || "Terjadi kesalahan"); } } catch (e) { customAlert("Error", "Gagal mengirim pesan WhatsApp"); } finally { showLoading(false); }
    }

    window.sendOrderFileLinkWA = async function(orderId, linkType) {
      const modeText = linkType === 'download' ? 'Download Final' : 'Review Final';
      if (!confirm("Kirim link " + modeText + " ke pelanggan via WhatsApp?")) return;
      showLoading(true, "Mengirim link file ke WhatsApp...");
      try {
        const res = await serverCall('sendOrderFileLinkWA', orderId, linkType);
        if (res && res.success) customAlert("✅ Berhasil", res.message || "Link berhasil dikirim.");
        else customAlert("❌ Gagal", (res && res.message) ? res.message : "Gagal mengirim link file.");
      } catch (e) {
        customAlert("❌ Error", "Terjadi error saat mengirim link file.");
      } finally {
        showLoading(false);
      }
    };

    window.getWAPaymentSelection = function() {
      const orderId = String(document.getElementById('wa-order-select')?.value || '').trim();
      const mode = String(document.getElementById('wa-payment-mode')?.value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (!orderId || !mode) return null;
      const order = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders || []).find(o => String(o['ID PESANAN'] || '') === orderId);
      if (!order) return null;
      const fin = calculateOrderFinance(order);
      const nominal = mode === 'DP' ? ((Number(fin.dpAmount) || 0) > 0 ? Number(fin.dpAmount) : Number(fin.net) * 0.5) : Math.max(0, Number(fin.balance) || 0);
      return { order: order, mode: mode, nominal: nominal };
    };

    window.openWAPaymentTemplateModal = function() {
      const orders = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders || []).slice(0, 200);
      const options = orders.map(function(o) {
        const id = o['ID PESANAN'] || '-';
        const nama = o['NAMA PELANGGAN'] || '-';
        const layanan = o['LAYANAN'] || '-';
        return '<option value="' + id + '">' + id + ' • ' + nama + ' • ' + layanan + '</option>';
      }).join('');

      const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
      body.innerHTML = ''
        + '<h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-4">WA Template DP / Lunas</h3>'
        + '<p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-4">Pilih pesanan dan jenis pembayaran. Sistem akan kirim template otomatis + link QRIS.</p>'
        + '<div class="space-y-4">'
        + '  <div>'
        + '    <label class="text-xs font-black text-slate-500 uppercase mb-1 block">Pilih Pesanan</label>'
        + '    <select id="wa-order-select" class="w-full p-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-sm font-semibold">' + options + '</select>'
        + '  </div>'
        + '  <div>'
        + '    <label class="text-xs font-black text-slate-500 uppercase mb-1 block">Jenis Pembayaran</label>'
        + '    <select id="wa-payment-mode" class="w-full p-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-sm font-semibold">'
        + '      <option value="DP">Down Payment (DP)</option>'
        + '      <option value="LUNAS">Lunas / Pelunasan</option>'
        + '    </select>'
        + '  </div>'
        + '  <div class="flex gap-2">'
        + '    <button onclick="previewWAPaymentTemplateModern()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">Preview</button>'
        + '    <button onclick="sendWAPaymentTemplateModern()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">Kirim WA</button>'
        + '  </div>'
        + '  <div id="wa-template-modern-preview" class="rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">Belum ada preview.</div>'
        + '</div>';
      document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
    };

    window.previewWAPaymentTemplateModern = function() {
      const parsed = getWAPaymentSelection();
      const box = document.getElementById('wa-template-modern-preview');
      if (!box) return;
      if (!parsed) {
        box.innerHTML = '<p class="text-red-500 font-semibold">Data pesanan/pembayaran tidak valid.</p>';
        return;
      }
      const qrisLink = 'https://ibb.co.com/cSksQpdd';
      const paymentLabel = parsed.mode === 'DP' ? 'Down Payment (DP)' : 'Pelunasan';
      box.innerHTML = ''
        + '<div class="space-y-2">'
        + '<p><strong>Order:</strong> ' + parsed.order['ID PESANAN'] + '</p>'
        + '<p><strong>Pelanggan:</strong> ' + (parsed.order['NAMA PELANGGAN'] || '-') + '</p>'
        + '<p><strong>Layanan:</strong> ' + (parsed.order['LAYANAN'] || '-') + '</p>'
        + '<p><strong>Jenis Bayar:</strong> ' + paymentLabel + '</p>'
        + '<p><strong>Nominal:</strong> ' + formatIDR(parsed.nominal) + '</p>'
        + '<p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Jika gambar QRIS tidak tampil di WA, gunakan link ini:</p>'
        + '<a target="_blank" href="' + qrisLink + '" class="text-indigo-600 font-bold text-xs underline break-all">' + qrisLink + '</a>'
        + '</div>';
    };

    window.sendWAPaymentTemplateModern = async function() {
      const parsed = getWAPaymentSelection();
      if (!parsed) return customAlert("Data Tidak Valid", "Pilih pesanan dan jenis pembayaran dulu.", "error");
      showLoading(true, "Mengirim template WA pembayaran...");
      try {
        const res = await serverCall('sendPaymentTemplateWA', parsed.order['ID PESANAN'], parsed.mode);
        if (res && res.success) customAlert("✅ Berhasil", "Template WA terkirim ke " + res.phone, "success");
        else customAlert("❌ Gagal", (res && res.message) ? res.message : "Gagal mengirim template WA", "error");
      } catch (e) {
        customAlert("❌ Error", "Terjadi error saat kirim template WA", "error");
      } finally {
        showLoading(false);
      }
    };


    // ==================== DASHBOARD: SERVICES & BULK ====================
    window.filterServices = function() {
      const q = document.getElementById('search-services').value.toLowerCase().trim();
      if (!q) { renderAdminServices(); return; }
      const filtered = DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices.filter(s => (s['Detail Layanan'] || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (s.Deskripsi || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) );
      renderAdminServices(filtered);
    }

    window.renderAdminServices = function(filtered = null) {
      const services = filtered || DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices;
      const container = document.getElementById('admin-services-list');
      if(!container) return;
      container.innerHTML = services.map(s => {
        let badge = '';
        if (s['Status Layanan'] === 'Tersedia') badge = `<span class="px-4 py-1 text-xs font-bold bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 rounded-full">Tersedia</span>`;
        else if (s['Status Layanan'] === 'Tidak Tersedia') badge = `<span class="px-4 py-1 text-xs font-bold bg-red-100 text-red-700 rounded-full">Tidak Tersedia</span>`;
        else if (s['Status Layanan'] === 'VIP') badge = `<span class="px-4 py-1 text-xs font-bold bg-violet-100 text-violet-700 rounded-full">VIP</span>`;

        return `
          <div class="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl border shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all relative group">
            <input type="checkbox" class="service-checkbox absolute top-6 right-6 w-5 h-5 accent-indigo-600 cursor-pointer" data-id="${s['ID LAYANAN']}" onchange="toggleServiceSelection('${s['ID LAYANAN']}', this.checked)">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-start mb-4"><h4 class="font-black text-lg pr-10">${s['Detail Layanan']}</h4>${badge}</div>
            <p class="text-slate-600 text-sm line-clamp-3 mb-5">${s.Deskripsi}</p>
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
              <div><span class="font-bold text-emerald-600">${formatIDR(s['Harga Publish'])}</span><span class="text-slate-400">/${s.Satuan}</span></div>
            </div>
            <div class="flex gap-3 mt-6">
              <button onclick="openServiceModal('${s['ID LAYANAN']}')" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-indigo-50 hover:bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 rounded-2xl font-bold text-sm">Edit</button>
              <button onclick="handleDeleteService('${s['ID LAYANAN']}')" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-700 rounded-2xl font-bold text-sm">Hapus</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    window.toggleServiceSelection = function(id, checked) {
      if (checked) SELECTED_SERVICES.add(id); else SELECTED_SERVICES.delete(id);
      updateServiceBulkBar();
    }

    window.updateServiceBulkBar = function() {
      const bar = document.getElementById('service-bulk-bar');
      if(!bar) return;
      if (SELECTED_SERVICES.size > 0) { bar.classList.remove('hidden'); document.getElementById('service-selected-count').innerText = SELECTED_SERVICES.size; } 
      else { bar.classList.add('hidden'); }
    }

    window.clearServiceSelection = function() {
      SELECTED_SERVICES.clear(); document.querySelectorAll('.service-checkbox').forEach(cb => cb.checked = false); updateServiceBulkBar();
    }

window.handleBulkServiceUpdate = async function() {
  const status = document.getElementById('bulk-service-status').value;
  if (!status) {
    customAlert("Pilih Status", "Silakan pilih status yang diinginkan");
    return;
  }
  if (SELECTED_SERVICES.size === 0) return;

  if (!confirm(`Ubah ${SELECTED_SERVICES.size} layanan menjadi "${status}"?`)) return;

  showLoading(true);
  try {
    const ids = Array.from(SELECTED_SERVICES);
    const result = await serverCall('bulkUpdateServiceStatus', { ids: ids, status: status });

    if (result.success) {
      customAlert("✅ Berhasil", result.message);
      clearServiceSelection();
      refreshData();
    } else {
      customAlert("❌ Gagal", result.message || "Terjadi kesalahan");
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.error(e);
    customAlert("❌ Error", "Gagal mengupdate status layanan");
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};

    window.openServiceModal = function(id = null) {
      const s = id ? DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices.find(x => x['ID LAYANAN'] === id) : null;
      const statusNow = s && s['Status Layanan'] ? s['Status Layanan'] : 'Tersedia';
      const hargaTypeNow = s && s['Harga Type'] ? s['Harga Type'] : 'belum';
      
      const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
      body.innerHTML = `
        <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6">${id ? 'Edit' : 'Tambah'} Layanan</h3>
        <form onsubmit="handleSaveService(event)" class="space-y-6">
          ${id ? `<input type="hidden" name="ID LAYANAN" value="${id}">` : ''}
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Kategori</label>
            <select id="svc-kat-select" name="Kategori" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none">
              <option value="">Pilih kategori...</option>
              ${ALL_KATEGORI.map(k => `<option value="${k.KATEGORI}" ${s && s.Kategori === k.KATEGORI ? 'selected' : ''}>${k.KATEGORI}</option>`).join('')}
              <option value="new">+ Tambah Kategori Baru</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Nama Layanan</label>
            <input type="text" name="Detail Layanan" id="svc-detail" value="${s ? s['Detail Layanan'] : ''}" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none">
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Deskripsi</label>
            <textarea name="Deskripsi" id="svc-desc" rows="3" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none">${s ? s.Deskripsi : ''}</textarea>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Satuan</label>
            <select name="Satuan" id="svc-satuan" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none">
              <option value="Unit" ${(!s || s.Satuan === 'Unit') ? 'selected' : ''}>Unit</option>
              <option value="Halaman" ${s && s.Satuan === 'Halaman' ? 'selected' : ''}>Halaman</option>
              <option value="Kata" ${s && s.Satuan === 'Kata' ? 'selected' : ''}>Kata</option>
              <option value="Lembar" ${s && s.Satuan === 'Lembar' ? 'selected' : ''}>Lembar</option>
              <option value="Proyek" ${s && s.Satuan === 'Proyek' ? 'selected' : ''}>Proyek</option>
              <option value="Paket" ${s && s.Satuan === 'Paket' ? 'selected' : ''}>Paket</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-3 text-slate-700">Jenis Harga Dasar</label>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <label class="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 border rounded-2xl cursor-pointer ${hargaTypeNow==='belum' ? 'border-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50' : ''}">
                <input type="radio" name="harga_type" value="belum" ${hargaTypeNow==='belum' ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="calcPub()" class="w-5 h-5 accent-indigo-600">
                <div><div class="font-semibold">Belum termasuk fee</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">Fee akan ditambahkan ke harga dasar</div></div>
              </label>
              <label class="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 border rounded-2xl cursor-pointer ${hargaTypeNow==='sudah' ? 'border-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50' : ''}">
                <input type="radio" name="harga_type" value="sudah" ${hargaTypeNow==='sudah' ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="calcPub()" class="w-5 h-5 accent-indigo-600">
                <div><div class="font-semibold">Sudah termasuk fee</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">Harga input = harga jual pelanggan<br>Worker terima setelah fee dipotong</div></div>
              </label>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-3 text-slate-700">Status Layanan</label>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
              <label class="flex items-center gap-2 p-4 border rounded-2xl cursor-pointer ${statusNow==='Tersedia' ? 'border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50' : ''}">
                <input type="radio" name="Status Layanan" value="Tersedia" ${statusNow==='Tersedia'?'checked':''} class="w-5 h-5 accent-emerald-600">
                <span class="font-semibold text-emerald-700">Tersedia</span>
              </label>
              <label class="flex items-center gap-2 p-4 border rounded-2xl cursor-pointer ${statusNow==='Tidak Tersedia' ? 'border-red-600 bg-red-50' : ''}">
                <input type="radio" name="Status Layanan" value="Tidak Tersedia" ${statusNow==='Tidak Tersedia'?'checked':''} class="w-5 h-5 accent-red-600">
                <span class="font-semibold text-red-700">Tidak Tersedia</span>
              </label>
              <label class="flex items-center gap-2 p-4 border rounded-2xl cursor-pointer ${statusNow==='VIP' ? 'border-violet-600 bg-violet-50' : ''}">
                <input type="radio" name="Status Layanan" value="VIP" ${statusNow==='VIP'?'checked':''} class="w-5 h-5 accent-violet-600">
                <span class="font-semibold text-violet-700">VIP (Hanya Admin)</span>
              </label>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Harga Dasar (Rp)</label>
              <input type="number" id="base-p" name="Harga dasar" value="${s ? s['Harga dasar'] : 0}" min="0" onchange="calcPub()" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none text-lg">
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Fee Admin (%)</label>
              <input type="number" id="fee-a" name="fee Admin(%)" value="${s ? s['fee Admin(%)'] : 0}" min="0" max="100" onchange="calcPub()" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none">
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Fee Perusahaan (%)</label>
              <input type="number" id="fee-p" name="fee perusahaan(%)" value="${s ? s['fee perusahaan(%)'] : 0}" min="0" max="100" onchange="calcPub()" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none">
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Harga Publish ke Pelanggan (otomatis)</label>
            <input type="number" id="pub-p" name="Harga Publish" readonly class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-bold text-xl">
          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-5 gradient-primary text-white rounded-2xl font-black shadow-lg">Simpan Layanan</button>
        </form>
      `;
      document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
      if(window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
      setTimeout(() => calcPub(), 100);
    }

    window.handleSaveService = async function(e) { 
      e.preventDefault(); showLoading(true); 
      const fd = new FormData(e.target);
      let kategori = fd.get('Kategori');
      if (kategori === 'new') kategori = document.getElementById('new-kat-name').value;
      fd.set('Kategori', kategori);
      try { await serverCall('saveService', Object.fromEntries(fd)); closeModal(); initApp(); refreshData(); } catch(e){} finally { showLoading(false); } 
    }

    window.handleDeleteService = async function(id) { if(!confirm("Hapus layanan ini?")) return; showLoading(true); try { await serverCall('deleteService', id); closeModal(); initApp(); refreshData(); } catch(e){} finally { showLoading(false); } }

    window.openQuickAddServiceModal = function() {
      const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
      body.innerHTML = `
        <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6">Tambah Layanan Baru</h3>
        <form onsubmit="handleQuickSaveService(event)" class="space-y-6">
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Nama Layanan</label><input type="text" id="quick-detail" placeholder="Contoh: Skripsi Bab 1-5 Full" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Kategori</label><select id="quick-kategori" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"><option value="">Pilih Kategori...</option>${ALL_KATEGORI.map(k => `<option value="${k.KATEGORI}">${k.KATEGORI}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Deskripsi Singkat</label><textarea id="quick-desc" rows="2" placeholder="Deskripsi singkat layanan..." class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></textarea></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Satuan</label><select id="quick-satuan" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"><option value="Unit">Unit</option><option value="Halaman">Halaman</option><option value="Kata">Kata</option><option value="Lembar">Lembar</option><option value="Proyek">Proyek</option><option value="Paket">Paket</option></select></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-3 text-slate-700">Status Layanan</label><div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3"><label class="flex items-center gap-2 p-4 border rounded-2xl cursor-pointer border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50"><input type="radio" name="quick_status" value="Tersedia" checked class="w-5 h-5 accent-emerald-600"><span class="font-semibold text-emerald-700">Tersedia</span></label><label class="flex items-center gap-2 p-4 border rounded-2xl cursor-pointer"><input type="radio" name="quick_status" value="Tidak Tersedia" class="w-5 h-5 accent-red-600"><span class="font-semibold text-red-700">Tidak Tersedia</span></label><label class="flex items-center gap-2 p-4 border rounded-2xl cursor-pointer"><input type="radio" name="quick_status" value="VIP" class="w-5 h-5 accent-violet-600"><span class="font-semibold text-violet-700">VIP</span></label></div></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-3 text-slate-700">Jenis Harga</label><div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4"><label class="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 border rounded-2xl cursor-pointer"><input type="radio" name="quick_harga_type" value="belum" checked onchange="quickCalcPub()" class="w-5 h-5 accent-indigo-600"><div><div class="font-semibold">Belum termasuk fee</div></div></label><label class="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 border rounded-2xl cursor-pointer"><input type="radio" name="quick_harga_type" value="sudah" onchange="quickCalcPub()" class="w-5 h-5 accent-indigo-600"><div><div class="font-semibold">Sudah termasuk fee</div></div></label></div></div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4"><div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Harga Dasar (Rp)</label><input type="number" id="quick-base" value="0" min="0" onchange="quickCalcPub()" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div><div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Fee Admin (%)</label><input type="number" id="quick-fee-a" value="0" min="0" max="100" onchange="quickCalcPub()" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div><div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Fee Perusahaan (%)</label><input type="number" id="quick-fee-p" value="0" min="0" max="100" onchange="quickCalcPub()" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Harga Publish ke Pelanggan</label><input type="number" id="quick-publish" readonly class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-bold text-xl"></div>
          <div class="flex gap-4"><button type="button" onclick="closeModal()" class="flex-1 py-4 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-bold rounded-2xl">Batal</button><button type="submit" class="flex-1 py-4 gradient-primary text-white font-bold rounded-2xl">Simpan Layanan Baru</button></div>
        </form>
      `;
      if(window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
      setTimeout(quickCalcPub, 100);
    }

    window.handleQuickSaveService = async function(e) {
      e.preventDefault(); showLoading(true);
      const hargaType = document.querySelector('input[name="quick_harga_type"]:checked').value;
      const status = document.querySelector('input[name="quick_status"]:checked').value;
      const newService = {
        'Detail Layanan': document.getElementById('quick-detail').value.trim(), 'Deskripsi': document.getElementById('quick-desc').value.trim(),
        'Kategori': document.getElementById('quick-kategori').value.trim() || 'Lainnya', 'Satuan': document.getElementById('quick-satuan').value,
        'Harga dasar': Number(document.getElementById('quick-base').value) || 0, 'fee Admin(%)': Number(document.getElementById('quick-fee-a').value) || 0,
        'fee perusahaan(%)': Number(document.getElementById('quick-fee-p').value) || 0, 'Harga Publish': Number(document.getElementById('quick-publish').value) || 0,
        'harga_type': hargaType, 'Status Layanan': status
      };
      try {
        await serverCall('saveService', newService); DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices = await serverCall('getAllServices');
        closeModal(); customAlert("Berhasil", "Layanan baru berhasil ditambahkan!"); setTimeout(() => openOrderModal(), 800); 
      } catch(e) { customAlert("Error", "Gagal menyimpan layanan"); } finally { showLoading(false); }
    }

    window.calcPub = function() {
      const base = Number(document.getElementById('base-p').value) || 0;
      const feeA = Number(document.getElementById('fee-a').value) || 0;
      const feeP = Number(document.getElementById('fee-p').value) || 0;
      const totalFee = (feeA + feeP) / 100;
      let type = 'belum';
      for (let radio of document.getElementsByName('harga_type')) if (radio.checked) type = radio.value;
      let publish = type === "sudah" ? base : Math.round(base * (1 + totalFee));
      document.getElementById('pub-p').value = publish;
    }

    window.quickCalcPub = function() {
      const base = Number(document.getElementById('quick-base').value) || 0;
      const feeA = Number(document.getElementById('quick-fee-a').value) || 0;
      const feeP = Number(document.getElementById('quick-fee-p').value) || 0;
      const totalFee = (feeA + feeP) / 100;
      const type = document.querySelector('input[name="quick_harga_type"]:checked').value;
      let publish = type === "sudah" ? base : Math.round(base * (1 + totalFee));
      document.getElementById('quick-publish').value = publish;
    }

    window.updateBulkActionBar = function() { const bar = document.getElementById('bulk-action-bar'); if(SELECTED_ORDERS.size > 0) { bar.style.display = 'block'; document.getElementById('selected-count').innerText = SELECTED_ORDERS.size; } else { bar.style.display = 'none'; } }
    window.toggleOrderSelection = function(id, checked) { if(checked) SELECTED_ORDERS.add(id); else SELECTED_ORDERS.delete(id); updateBulkActionBar(); }
    window.clearSelection = function() { SELECTED_ORDERS.clear(); document.querySelectorAll('.lc-checkbox').forEach(cb => cb.checked = false); updateBulkActionBar(); }
    window.handleBulkUpdate = async function() {
      const status = document.getElementById('bulk-status-select').value;
      if (!status) return customAlert("⚠️ Pilih Status", "Silakan pilih status baru terlebih dahulu.", "error");
      if (SELECTED_ORDERS.size === 0) return customAlert("⚠️ Tidak Ada Data", "Pilih minimal satu pesanan untuk diupdate.", "error");
      if (!confirm(`Update ${SELECTED_ORDERS.size} pesanan ke status "${status}"?`)) return;
      showLoading(true, "Memproses bulk update status...");
      try {
        const ids = Array.from(SELECTED_ORDERS);
        await Promise.all(ids.map(id => serverCall('updateOrder', id, { status: status })));
        clearSelection();
        await refreshData();
        customAlert("✅ Berhasil", `${ids.length} pesanan berhasil diupdate ke status ${status}.`, "success");
      } catch (e) {
        customAlert("❌ Gagal", "Bulk update status gagal diproses.", "error");
      } finally {
        showLoading(false);
      }
    }
    window.handleBulkDelete = async function() {
      if (SELECTED_ORDERS.size === 0) return customAlert("⚠️ Tidak Ada Data", "Pilih minimal satu pesanan untuk dihapus.", "error");
      if (!confirm(`Hapus permanen ${SELECTED_ORDERS.size} pesanan terpilih?`)) return;
      showLoading(true, "Menghapus pesanan terpilih...");
      try {
        const ids = Array.from(SELECTED_ORDERS);
        await Promise.all(ids.map(id => serverCall('deleteOrder', id)));
        clearSelection();
        await refreshData();
        customAlert("✅ Berhasil", `${ids.length} pesanan berhasil dihapus.`, "success");
      } catch (e) {
        customAlert("❌ Gagal", "Bulk delete gagal diproses.", "error");
      } finally {
        showLoading(false);
      }
    }


    // ==================== DASHBOARD: USERS & WALOG & SETTINGS ====================

    window.renderAdminUsers = function() {
      const list = document.getElementById('admin-users-list');
      if(!list) return;
      list.innerHTML = DASHBOARD_CACHE.users.map(u => `
        <div class="bg-white p-6 rounded-[2rem] border shadow-sm">
          <h4 class="font-black mb-2">${u['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || u.Username}</h4>
          <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-1">Role: ${u.Role}</p>
          <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-4">Email: ${u.Email}</p>
          <div class="flex gap-2">
            <button onclick="openUserModal('${u['ID UNIK']}')" class="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs hover:bg-indigo-100 transition-colors">Edit</button>
            <button onclick="handleDeleteUser('${u['ID UNIK']}')" class="px-4 py-2 bg-red-50 text-red-600 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs hover:bg-red-100 transition-colors">Hapus</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    window.openUserModal = function(id = null) {
      const u = id ? DASHBOARD_CACHE.users.find(x => x['ID UNIK'] === id) : null;
      const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
      body.innerHTML = `
        <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6">${id ? 'Edit' : 'Tambah'} User</h3>
        <form onsubmit="handleSaveUser(event)" class="space-y-4">
          ${id ? `<input type="hidden" name="ID UNIK" value="${id}">` : ''}
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Username</label><input type="text" name="Username" id="user-username" value="${u ? u.Username : ''}" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Password</label><input type="password" name="Password" id="user-password" value="${u ? u.Password : ''}" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Nama Lengkap</label><input type="text" name="Nama Lengkap Admin" id="user-name" value="${u ? u['Nama Lengkap Admin'] : ''}" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">No. HP</label><input type="tel" name="No HP" id="user-hp" value="${u ? u['No HP'] : ''}" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Email</label><input type="email" name="Email" id="user-email" value="${u ? u.Email : ''}" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Role</label><select name="Role" id="user-role" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"><option value="Admin" ${u && u.Role === 'Admin' ? 'selected' : ''}>Admin</option><option value="Worker" ${u && u.Role === 'Worker' ? 'selected' : ''}>Worker</option><option value="Bendahara" ${u && u.Role === 'Bendahara' ? 'selected' : ''}>Bendahara</option></select></div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-4 gradient-primary text-white rounded-2xl font-bold shadow-lg">Simpan</button>
        </form>
      `;
      document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
      if(window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
    }

    window.handleSaveUser = async function(e) { e.preventDefault(); showLoading(true); try { await serverCall('saveUser', Object.fromEntries(new FormData(e.target))); closeModal(); refreshData(); } catch(e){} finally { showLoading(false); } }
    window.handleDeleteUser = async function(id) { if(!confirm("Hapus user ini?")) return; showLoading(true); try { await serverCall('deleteUser', id); closeModal(); refreshData(); } catch(e){} finally { showLoading(false); } }

    window.handleUpdateRunningText = async function() {
      const val = document.getElementById('input-running-text').value;
      if(!val) return; showLoading(true);
      try { await serverCall('setRunningText', val); setRunningTextModern(val); customAlert("Berhasil", "Running text diperbarui."); } catch(e){} finally { showLoading(false); }
    }

 window.saveNotifSettings = async function() {
  showLoading(true);
  const payload = {
    waCustomer: document.getElementById('toggle-wa-customer').checked,
    emailCustomer: document.getElementById('toggle-email-customer').checked,
    waWorker: document.getElementById('toggle-wa-worker').checked,
    emailWorker: document.getElementById('toggle-email-worker').checked,
    waAdmin: document.getElementById('toggle-wa-admin').checked,
    emailAdmin: document.getElementById('toggle-email-admin').checked
  };

  try {
    const result = await serverCall('saveNotificationSettings', payload);
    if (result.success) {
      customAlert("✅ Berhasil", "Pengaturan notifikasi telah disimpan");
    } else {
      customAlert("❌ Gagal", "Terjadi kesalahan saat menyimpan");
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.error(e);
    customAlert("❌ Error", "Gagal menyimpan pengaturan notifikasi");
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};

    window.loadNotificationSettings = async function() {
      try {
        const settings = await serverCall('getNotificationSettings');
        document.getElementById('toggle-wa-customer').checked = settings.waCustomer; document.getElementById('toggle-email-customer').checked = settings.emailCustomer; document.getElementById('toggle-wa-worker').checked = settings.waWorker; document.getElementById('toggle-email-worker').checked = settings.emailWorker; document.getElementById('toggle-wa-admin').checked = settings.waAdmin; document.getElementById('toggle-email-admin').checked = settings.emailAdmin;
      } catch(e) {}
    }

    window.loadWebSettings = async function() {
      try {
        const settings = await serverCall('getWebSettings');
        document.getElementById('web-name1').value = settings.name1 || 'Lentera'; document.getElementById('web-name2').value = settings.name2 || 'Cendekia'; document.getElementById('web-tagline1').value = settings.tagline1 || 'Solusi Terpercaya Sejak 2020'; document.getElementById('web-tagline2').value = settings.tagline2 || 'Solusi Terbaik Untuk'; document.getElementById('web-tagline3').value = settings.tagline3 || 'Karya Akademik'; document.getElementById('web-slogan').value = settings.slogan || 'Premium Academic Support';
        document.getElementById('web-logo-url').value = settings.logoUrl || '';
        document.getElementById('web-discount-label').value = settings.discountLabel || '$ DISKON SPESIAL';
        applyWebIdentity(settings);
      } catch(e) {}
    }

    window.saveWebSettings = async function() {
      showLoading(true);
      const payload = { name1: document.getElementById('web-name1').value.trim(), name2: document.getElementById('web-name2').value.trim(), tagline1: document.getElementById('web-tagline1').value.trim(), tagline2: document.getElementById('web-tagline2').value.trim(), tagline3: document.getElementById('web-tagline3').value.trim(), slogan: document.getElementById('web-slogan').value.trim(), logoUrl: document.getElementById('web-logo-url').value.trim(), discountLabel: document.getElementById('web-discount-label').value.trim() };
      try { await serverCall('saveWebSettings', payload); applyWebIdentity(payload); customAlert("✅ Berhasil", "Pengaturan website disimpan.<br>Identitas brand langsung diperbarui."); } catch(e) { customAlert("❌ Gagal", "Terjadi kesalahan"); } finally { showLoading(false); }
    }

   window.renderWALog = async function() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('walog-table-body');
  if (!tbody) return;
  tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" class="py-12 text-center text-slate-400">⏳ Memuat data WA Log...</td></tr>';

  try {
    const logs = await serverCall('getWALogData');
    if (!logs || logs.length === 0) {
      tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" class="py-12 text-center text-slate-400">Belum ada riwayat pengiriman WA</td></tr>';
      return;
    }

    tbody.innerHTML = logs.map(log => {
      let statusText = String(log.Status || 'Unknown').trim();
      if (statusText === 'undefined' || statusText === '') statusText = 'Unknown';

      return `
        <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
          <td class="px-6 py-4 text-xs text-slate-500">${log.Timestamp || '-'}</td>
          <td class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">${log['ID Pesanan'] || '-'}</td>
          <td class="px-6 py-4 font-mono">${log['Nomor HP'] || ''}</td>
          <td class="px-6 py-4">${log['Tipe Pesan'] || ''}</td>
          <td class="px-6 py-4 text-slate-600">${log['Pesan Ringkas'] || ''}</td>
          <td class="px-6 py-4 text-center">
            <span class="px-4 py-1 text-xs font-bold rounded-full ${statusText.toLowerCase() === 'sukses' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'}">
              ${statusText}
            </span>
          </td>
        </tr>`;
    }).join('');
  } catch(e) {
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" class="py-12 text-center text-red-500">Gagal memuat data WA Log</td></tr>';
  }
};

window.clearWALog = async function() {
  const ok = confirm("Hapus seluruh riwayat WA Log? Tindakan ini tidak bisa dibatalkan.");
  if (!ok) return;
  showLoading(true, "Menghapus riwayat WA Log...");
  try {
    const candidates = ['clearWALogData', 'clearWALog', 'deleteWALogData', 'truncateWALog'];
    let called = null;

    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
      const runner = google.script.run;
      called = candidates.find(name => typeof runner[name] === 'function') || null;
    }

    const targetFn = called || candidates[0];
    const res = await serverCall(targetFn);
    if (res && res.success === false) {
      customAlert("❌ Gagal", res.message || "Riwayat WA tidak berhasil dihapus.", "error");
      return;
    }
    customAlert("✅ Berhasil", "Riwayat WA Log berhasil dihapus.", "success");
    renderWALog();
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Clear WA Log error:", e);
    const msg = String(e && e.message ? e.message : e);
    if (msg.includes('tidak tersedia di Apps Script')) {
      customAlert("❌ Error", "Fungsi hapus WA Log belum ada di backend Apps Script. Tambahkan fungsi clearWALogData/clearWALog di server.", "error");
    } else {
      customAlert("❌ Error", "Gagal menghapus riwayat WA Log.", "error");
    }
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};


    // ==================== DASHBOARD: BENDAHARA / KEUANGAN ====================
 // Variabel pagination modal (tambahkan di atas initApp)
// Variabel pagination modal (tambahkan di atas initApp)
let currentSummaryPage = 1;
const SUMMARY_PER_PAGE = 8;

window.showFinancialSummary = function(source = 'monitor') {
  const roleStr = String(CURRENT_USER.Role).toLowerCase();
  const isBendahara = roleStr === 'bendahara' || roleStr === 'finance';
  const isAdmin = roleStr === 'admin';
  const isWorker = !isAdmin && !isBendahara;

  if ((isBendahara || isAdmin) && source !== 'pengeluaran') {
    customAlert("Info", "Buka rincian kas dari kartu di tab Pengeluaran agar detail fee & sisa gaji tampil lengkap.", "info");
  }

  let orders = DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.filter(o => window.safeGet(o, 'TAHAPAN PESANAN') === 'Selesai');

  if (isWorker) {
    orders = orders.filter(o => window.safeGet(o, 'WORKER').toLowerCase() === CURRENT_USER.Username.toLowerCase());
  }

  let totalHak = 0;
  const tableRows = [];

  orders.forEach(o => {
    const fin = calculateOrderFinance(o);
    if (isBendahara) totalHak += fin.net;
    else if (isAdmin) totalHak += fin.feeAdmin;
    else if (isWorker) totalHak += fin.workerNet;

    tableRows.push(`
      <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
        <td class="p-3">
          <div class="font-medium">#${o['ID PESANAN'] || '-'}</div>
          <div class="text-slate-500 text-xs">${o['NAMA PELANGGAN'] || '-'}</div>
          <div class="text-slate-600 text-xs">${o.LAYANAN || '-'}</div>
        </td>
        <td class="p-3 text-right">${formatIDR(fin.gross)}</td>
        <td class="p-3 text-right text-red-500">-${formatIDR(fin.gross - fin.net)}</td>
        <td class="p-3 text-right font-semibold">${formatIDR(fin.net)}</td>
        <td class="p-3 text-right text-amber-600">${formatIDR(fin.feeAdmin)}</td>
        <td class="p-3 text-right text-orange-600">${formatIDR(fin.feeCompany)}</td>
        <td class="p-3 text-right font-bold text-emerald-600">${formatIDR(fin.workerNet)}</td>
      </tr>
    `);
  });

  let totalGajiDiterima = 0;
  if (DASHBOARD_CACHE.gaji) {
    const myGaji = DASHBOARD_CACHE.gaji.filter(g => {
      const namaGaji = String(g['Nama Pegawai'] || g['Nama'] || g['Worker Username'] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const userName = String(CURRENT_USER.Nama || CURRENT_USER.Username || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      return namaGaji === userName;
    });
    totalGajiDiterima = myGaji.reduce((sum, g) => sum + (Number(g['Total Terima']) || Number(g['Jumlah']) || 0), 0);
  }

  let sisaSaldo = isBendahara 
    ? totalHak - (DASHBOARD_CACHE.pengeluaran || []).reduce((sum, p) => sum + (Number(p['Jumlah']) || 0), 0) 
    : totalHak - totalGajiDiterima;

  const totalPengeluaran = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.pengeluaran || []).reduce((sum, p) => sum + (Number(p['Jumlah']) || 0), 0);
  const totalPendapatanLain = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.pendapatan || []).reduce((sum, p) => sum + (Number(p['Jumlah']) || 0), 0);
  const totalFeePerusahaan = orders.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (Number(calculateOrderFinance(o).feeCompany) || 0), 0);
  const totalNetOrder = orders.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (Number(calculateOrderFinance(o).net) || 0), 0);
  const totalHakAdmin = orders.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (Number(calculateOrderFinance(o).feeAdmin) || 0), 0);
  const totalHakWorker = orders.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (Number(calculateOrderFinance(o).workerNet) || 0), 0);

  const roleByUsername = {};
  (DASHBOARD_CACHE.users || []).forEach(u => {
    const key = String(u.Username || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    roleByUsername[key] = String(u.Role || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  });

  let totalGajiAdmin = 0;
  let totalGajiWorker = 0;
  (DASHBOARD_CACHE.gaji || []).forEach(g => {
    const uname = String(g['Worker Username'] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const nilai = Number(g['Total Terima']) || Number(g['Jumlah']) || 0;
    const role = roleByUsername[uname] || '';
    if (role === 'admin') totalGajiAdmin += nilai;
    else totalGajiWorker += nilai;
  });

  const sisaGajiAdmin = Math.max(0, totalHakAdmin - totalGajiAdmin);
  const sisaGajiWorker = Math.max(0, totalHakWorker - totalGajiWorker);
  const kasSaatIni = Math.max(0, totalNetOrder + totalPendapatanLain - totalPengeluaran);
  const sisaFeePerusahaan = Math.max(0, kasSaatIni - sisaGajiAdmin - sisaGajiWorker);

  let html = `
    <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6 text-center">${isBendahara ? "Rincian Keuangan Perusahaan" : isAdmin ? "Rincian Keuangan Keseluruhan" : "Rincian Saldo Pribadi Saya"}</h3>
    <div class="bg-slate-50 p-6 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 mb-8 animate__animated animate__fadeInUp">
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center border-b pb-4 mb-4">
        <span class="font-bold text-slate-500">${isBendahara || isAdmin ? "Total Pendapatan Net" : "Total Hak Saya"}</span>
        <span class="text-xl font-black text-emerald-600">${formatIDR(totalHak)}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center border-b pb-4 mb-4">
        <span class="font-bold text-slate-500">${isBendahara || isAdmin ? "Total Pengeluaran" : "Total Gaji yang Sudah Diterima"}</span>
        <span class="text-xl font-black text-red-600">-${formatIDR(totalGajiDiterima)}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
        <span class="font-black text-lg">${isBendahara || isAdmin ? "Sisa Kas Bersih" : "Sisa Saldo Saya"}</span>
        <span class="text-3xl font-black text-indigo-600">${formatIDR(sisaSaldo)}</span>
      </div>
    </div>`;

  if (isBendahara || isAdmin) {
    const baseKas = totalNetOrder + totalPendapatanLain;
    const adminShare = baseKas > 0 ? Math.min(100, (totalGajiAdmin / baseKas) * 100) : 0;
    const workerShare = baseKas > 0 ? Math.min(100, (totalGajiWorker / baseKas) * 100) : 0;
    const expenseShare = baseKas > 0 ? Math.min(100, (totalPengeluaran / baseKas) * 100) : 0;

    html += `
      <div class="bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-900 via-indigo-900 to-slate-800 text-white rounded-3xl p-6 md:p-7 mb-8 shadow-2xl animate__animated animate__fadeIn">
        <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-5">
          <div>
            <p class="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-white/70 font-bold">Detail Sisa Kas Bersih</p>
            <h4 class="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black mt-1">Financial Insight Breakdown</h4>
            <p class="text-xs text-white/70 mt-2">Kas saat ini sudah dikurangi pembayaran gaji. Ditampilkan juga sisa kewajiban gaji yang belum dibayar.</p>
          </div>
          <div class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white/10 border border-white/20 text-[11px] font-bold">Realtime Preview</div>
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-5">
          <div class="bg-white/10 border border-white/20 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <p class="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-white/70 font-bold">Gaji Admin</p>
            <p class="text-2xl font-black mt-2" data-animate-currency="${totalGajiAdmin}">Rp 0</p>
            <p class="text-[11px] text-white/70 mt-1">Sisa belum dibayar: <span class="font-bold text-amber-200">${formatIDR(sisaGajiAdmin)}</span></p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-white/10 border border-white/20 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <p class="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-white/70 font-bold">Gaji Worker</p>
            <p class="text-2xl font-black mt-2" data-animate-currency="${totalGajiWorker}">Rp 0</p>
            <p class="text-[11px] text-white/70 mt-1">Sisa belum dibayar: <span class="font-bold text-amber-200">${formatIDR(sisaGajiWorker)}</span></p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-white/10 border border-white/20 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <p class="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-white/70 font-bold">Pendapatan Lainnya</p>
            <p class="text-2xl font-black mt-2 text-emerald-300" data-animate-currency="${totalPendapatanLain}">Rp 0</p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-white/10 border border-white/20 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <p class="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-white/70 font-bold">Sisa Fee Perusahaan (Setelah kewajiban gaji)</p>
            <p class="text-2xl font-black mt-2 text-cyan-300" data-animate-currency="${sisaFeePerusahaan}">Rp 0</p>
            <p class="text-[11px] text-white/70 mt-1">Fee terkumpul: <span class="font-bold text-cyan-200">${formatIDR(totalFeePerusahaan)}</span></p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-white/10 border border-white/20 rounded-2xl p-4 md:col-span-2">
            <p class="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-white/70 font-bold">Kas Saat Ini</p>
            <p class="text-2xl font-black mt-2 text-lime-300" data-animate-currency="${kasSaatIni}">Rp 0</p>
            <p class="text-[11px] text-white/70 mt-1">Rumus: Net Order + Pendapatan Lain - Pengeluaran Dibayar</p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-white/10 border border-white/20 rounded-2xl p-4 md:col-span-2">
            <p class="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-white/70 font-bold">Total Sisa Kewajiban Gaji</p>
            <p class="text-2xl font-black mt-2 text-amber-200" data-animate-currency="${sisaGajiAdmin + sisaGajiWorker}">Rp 0</p>
            <p class="text-[11px] text-white/70 mt-1">Admin: ${formatIDR(sisaGajiAdmin)} • Worker: ${formatIDR(sisaGajiWorker)}</p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-white/10 border border-white/20 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <p class="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-white/70 font-bold">Fee Perusahaan (Order Selesai)</p>
            <p class="text-2xl font-black mt-2 text-cyan-300" data-animate-currency="${totalFeePerusahaan}">Rp 0</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="space-y-3">
          <div>
            <div class="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1"><span class="text-white/80">Proporsi Gaji Admin</span><span class="font-black">${adminShare.toFixed(1)}%</span></div>
            <div class="h-2.5 bg-white/10 rounded-full overflow-hidden"><div class="h-full bg-fuchsia-400 rounded-full transition-all duration-1000" style="width:${adminShare}%;"></div></div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div class="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1"><span class="text-white/80">Proporsi Gaji Worker</span><span class="font-black">${workerShare.toFixed(1)}%</span></div>
            <div class="h-2.5 bg-white/10 rounded-full overflow-hidden"><div class="h-full bg-amber-300 rounded-full transition-all duration-1000" style="width:${workerShare}%;"></div></div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div class="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1"><span class="text-white/80">Rasio Total Pengeluaran</span><span class="font-black">${expenseShare.toFixed(1)}%</span></div>
            <div class="h-2.5 bg-white/10 rounded-full overflow-hidden"><div class="h-full bg-rose-400 rounded-full transition-all duration-1000" style="width:${expenseShare}%;"></div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }

  if (tableRows.length > 0) {
    html += `
      <h4 class="font-bold text-sm mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
        <i data-lucide="history" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
        ${isWorker ? "Riwayat Order Saya" : "Semua Order Selesai"} (${tableRows.length} data)
      </h4>
      <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border overflow-hidden">
        <table class="w-full text-xs">
          <thead class="bg-slate-50">
            <tr>
              <th class="p-3 text-left">ID • Pelanggan • Layanan</th>
              <th class="p-3 text-right">Gross</th>
              <th class="p-3 text-right text-red-500">Diskon</th>
              <th class="p-3 text-right">Net</th>
              <th class="p-3 text-right text-amber-600">Fee Admin</th>
              <th class="p-3 text-right text-orange-600">Fee Perusahaan</th>
              <th class="p-3 text-right font-bold text-emerald-600">Worker Net</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody class="divide-y">${tableRows.join('')}</tbody>
        </table>
      </div>`;
  } else {
    html += `<p class="text-slate-400 text-center py-12 text-lg">Belum ada order yang selesai untuk Anda.</p>`;
  }

  // Modal lebih besar
  document.getElementById('modal-content').style.maxWidth = '1100px';
  document.getElementById('modal-content').style.width = '95%';
  document.getElementById('modal-content').style.maxHeight = '92vh';

  document.getElementById('modal-content-body').innerHTML = html;
  document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-animate-currency]').forEach(el => {
    const target = Number(el.getAttribute('data-animate-currency')) || 0;
    const start = 0;
    const duration = 900;
    let startTime = null;
    const step = (timestamp) => {
      if (!startTime) startTime = timestamp;
      const progress = Math.min((timestamp - startTime) / duration, 1);
      const value = Math.floor(start + (target - start) * progress);
      el.textContent = formatIDR(value);
      if (progress < 1) window.requestAnimationFrame(step);
      else el.textContent = formatIDR(target);
    };
    window.requestAnimationFrame(step);
  });
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};
    window.changeExpensePage = function(page) { currentExpensePage = page; renderPengeluaranTable(); };
    window.changeExpenseFilter = function(filter) { currentExpenseFilter = filter; currentExpensePage = 1; renderPengeluaranTable(); };

    window.deletePengeluaran = async function(id) {
      if(!confirm("Yakin ingin menghapus pengeluaran ini?")) return;
      showLoading(true);
      try {
          await serverCall('deletePengeluaran', id);
          refreshData();
          customAlert("Berhasil", "Data pengeluaran dihapus");
      } catch(e) { customAlert("Error", "Gagal menghapus pengeluaran"); } finally { showLoading(false); }
    };

    window.editPengeluaran = function(id) {
      const p = DASHBOARD_CACHE.pengeluaran.find(x => x['ID Pengeluaran'] === id);
      if(!p) return;
      
      const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
      body.innerHTML = `
        <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6">Edit Pengeluaran</h3>
        <form onsubmit="handleEditPengeluaran(event, '${id}')" class="space-y-6">
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Tanggal</label><input type="date" id="edit-pengeluaran-tanggal" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Kategori</label><select id="edit-pengeluaran-kategori" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"><option value="Operasional">Operasional</option><option value="Marketing">Marketing</option><option value="Aset">Aset</option><option value="Gaji Pegawai">Gaji Pegawai</option><option value="Lainnya">Lainnya</option></select></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Deskripsi</label><input type="text" id="edit-pengeluaran-deskripsi" value="${p['Deskripsi']}" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Jumlah (Rp)</label><input type="number" id="edit-pengeluaran-jumlah" required min="0" value="${p['Jumlah']}" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-4 gradient-primary text-white rounded-2xl font-bold shadow-lg">Update Pengeluaran</button>
        </form>
      `;
      
      let d = new Date(p['Tanggal'] || p['TANGGAL']);
      let month = '' + (d.getMonth() + 1); let day = '' + d.getDate(); let year = d.getFullYear();
      if (month.length < 2) month = '0' + month; if (day.length < 2) day = '0' + day;
      document.getElementById('edit-pengeluaran-tanggal').value = [year, month, day].join('-');
      document.getElementById('edit-pengeluaran-kategori').value = p['Kategori'];
