      document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
    };

    window.handleEditPengeluaran = async function(e, id) {
  e.preventDefault(); 
  showLoading(true);
  const payload = {
    id: id,
    tanggal: document.getElementById('edit-pengeluaran-tanggal').value,
    deskripsi: document.getElementById('edit-pengeluaran-deskripsi').value,
    jumlah: document.getElementById('edit-pengeluaran-jumlah').value,
    kategori: document.getElementById('edit-pengeluaran-kategori').value
  };
  try {
    await serverCall('updatePengeluaran', payload);
    closeModal();
    customAlert("Berhasil", "Pengeluaran diupdate (Gaji terkait otomatis disesuaikan)");
    refreshData();
  } catch (e) {
    customAlert("Error", "Gagal mengupdate pengeluaran");
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};

    window.openPengeluaranModal = function() {
      const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
      body.innerHTML = `
        <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6">Input Pengeluaran Baru</h3>
        <form onsubmit="handleSavePengeluaran(event)" class="space-y-6">
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Tanggal</label><input type="date" id="pengeluaran-tanggal" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Kategori</label><select id="pengeluaran-kategori" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"><option value="">Pilih Kategori...</option><option value="Operasional">Operasional</option><option value="Marketing">Marketing</option><option value="Aset">Aset</option><option value="Lainnya">Lainnya</option></select></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Deskripsi</label><input type="text" id="pengeluaran-deskripsi" placeholder="Contoh: Beli pulsa admin" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Jumlah (Rp)</label><input type="number" id="pengeluaran-jumlah" required min="0" placeholder="0" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-4 gradient-primary text-white rounded-2xl font-bold shadow-lg">Simpan Pengeluaran</button>
        </form>
      `;
      document.getElementById('pengeluaran-tanggal').valueAsDate = new Date();
      document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
      if(window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
    }

    window.handleSavePengeluaran = async function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const payload = { tanggal: document.getElementById('pengeluaran-tanggal').value, deskripsi: document.getElementById('pengeluaran-deskripsi').value, jumlah: document.getElementById('pengeluaran-jumlah').value, kategori: document.getElementById('pengeluaran-kategori').value, inputBy: CURRENT_USER.Username };
      showLoading(true);
      try { const res = await serverCall('savePengeluaran', payload); if (res.success) { closeModal(); customAlert("Berhasil", "Data pengeluaran berhasil disimpan."); refreshData(); } } catch (e) { customAlert("Error", "Gagal menyimpan pengeluaran"); } finally { showLoading(false); }
    }

    window.populateGajiDropdowns = function() {
      const monthNames = ["Januari", "Februari", "Maret", "April", "Mei", "Juni", "Juli", "Agustus", "September", "Oktober", "November", "Desember"];
      const date = new Date(); let monthOptions = '';
      for(let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
        let m = new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth() - i, 1);
        let text = monthNames[m.getMonth()] + " " + m.getFullYear();
        monthOptions += `<option value="${text}">${text}</option>`;
      }
      document.getElementById('gaji-bulanTahun').innerHTML = monthOptions;
      let userOptions = '<option value="">Pilih Pegawai...</option>';
      DASHBOARD_CACHE.users.forEach(u => {
        const bank = u['Nama Bank'] || u['Bank'] || '';
        const rek = u['No Rekening'] || u['Rekening'] || '';
        userOptions += `<option value="${u.Username}" data-role="${String(u.Role).toLowerCase()}" data-bank="${String(bank).replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" data-rek="${String(rek).replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}">${u['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || u.Username} (${u.Role})</option>`;
      });
      document.getElementById('gaji-worker').innerHTML = userOptions;
    }

    window.getBankAdminFee = function(bankName = '', nominal = 0) {
      const bank = String(bankName || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      if (!nominal || nominal <= 0) return 0;
      return bank.includes('mandiri') ? 0 : 2500;
    }

    // AUTO FILL GAJI DAN INFORMASI SISA
    window.autoFillGaji = function() {
      const select = document.getElementById('gaji-worker');
      const selectedOption = select.options[select.selectedIndex];
      const infoPanel = document.getElementById('gaji-worker-info');

      if (!selectedOption || !selectedOption.value) { infoPanel.classList.add('hidden'); return; }
      
      const username = selectedOption.value;
      const role = selectedOption.getAttribute('data-role');
      const userObj = DASHBOARD_CACHE.users.find(u => u.Username === username) || {};
      
      let totalHak = 0;
      DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.filter(o => o['TAHAPAN PESANAN'] === 'Selesai').forEach(o => {
         const fin = calculateOrderFinance(o);
         if (role === 'worker' && o.WORKER === username) { totalHak += fin.workerNet; }
         else if (role === 'admin') { totalHak += fin.feeAdmin; }
      });
      
      let totalDibayar = 0;
      if (DASHBOARD_CACHE.gaji) {
         DASHBOARD_CACHE.gaji.filter(g => g['Worker Username'] === username).forEach(g => { totalDibayar += (Number(g['Total Terima']) || 0); });
      }
      
      const sisaBelumDibayar = Math.max(0, totalHak - totalDibayar);

      document.getElementById('gw-hak').innerText = formatIDR(totalHak);
      document.getElementById('gw-dibayar').innerText = formatIDR(totalDibayar);
      document.getElementById('gw-sisa').innerText = formatIDR(sisaBelumDibayar);
      
      // Auto find bank and rekening key robustly
      let bankKey = Object.keys(userObj).find(k => k.toLowerCase().includes('bank')) || 'Nama Bank';
      let rekKey = Object.keys(userObj).find(k => k.toLowerCase().includes('rekening')) || 'No Rekening';
      
      const bName = userObj[bankKey] || 'Belum diatur';
      const bRek = userObj[rekKey] || '-';
      document.getElementById('gw-bank').innerText = bName;
      document.getElementById('gw-rek').innerText = bRek;
      select.setAttribute('data-selected-bank', bName);
      select.setAttribute('data-selected-rek', bRek);
      
      document.getElementById('gw-copy-btn').onclick = () => copyText(bRek);

      if (role === 'worker' || role === 'admin') { document.getElementById('gaji-jumlah').value = sisaBelumDibayar; } 
      else { document.getElementById('gaji-jumlah').value = 1500000; }
      
      infoPanel.classList.remove('hidden');
      if (typeof updateGajiCalculator === 'function') updateGajiCalculator();
    }

    window.openGajiModal = function() {
      const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
      body.innerHTML = `
        <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6">Input Gaji Pegawai</h3>
        <form onsubmit="handleSaveGaji(event)" class="space-y-6">
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Bulan & Tahun</label><select id="gaji-bulanTahun" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></select></div>
          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Pilih Pegawai</label><select id="gaji-worker" onchange="autoFillGaji()" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"><option value="">Memuat...</option></select></div>
          
          <div id="gaji-worker-info" class="hidden bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-100 p-5 rounded-2xl">
            <h4 class="text-indigo-900 font-bold mb-3 text-sm">Informasi Finansial Pegawai</h4>
            <div class="space-y-2 text-sm">
              <div class="flex justify-between"><span>Total Hak Order (Net)</span><strong id="gw-hak">Rp 0</strong></div>
              <div class="flex justify-between"><span>Total Telah Dibayarkan</span><strong id="gw-dibayar">Rp 0</strong></div>
              <div class="flex justify-between border-t border-indigo-200 pt-2 text-red-600"><span>Sisa Belum Dibayar</span><strong id="gw-sisa">Rp 0</strong></div>
            </div>
            <h4 class="text-indigo-900 font-bold mb-2 mt-4 text-sm">Informasi Rekening</h4>
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-indigo-100">
              <div><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1" id="gw-bank">BANK</p><p class="font-mono font-bold text-slate-800" id="gw-rek">1234567890</p></div>
              <button type="button" id="gw-copy-btn" class="p-2 text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50 rounded-lg hover:bg-indigo-600 hover:text-white transition-all"><i data-lucide="copy" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Gaji Bersih / Pokok (Rp)</label><input type="number" id="gaji-jumlah" oninput="updateGajiCalculator()" required min="0" value="0" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4"><div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Bonus (Rp)</label><input type="number" id="gaji-bonus" oninput="updateGajiCalculator()" value="0" min="0" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div><div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Potongan (Rp)</label><input type="number" id="gaji-potongan" oninput="updateGajiCalculator()" value="0" min="0" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div></div>
          <div id="gaji-calc-preview" class="salary-calc-card text-sm"></div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-4 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-2xl font-bold shadow-lg">Simpan Gaji</button>
        </form>
      `;
      document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
      populateGajiDropdowns(); updateGajiCalculator(); if(window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
    }

    window.updateGajiCalculator = function() {
      const gajiPokok = Number(document.getElementById('gaji-jumlah')?.value) || 0;
      const bonus = Number(document.getElementById('gaji-bonus')?.value) || 0;
      const potongan = Number(document.getElementById('gaji-potongan')?.value) || 0;
      const total = gajiPokok + bonus - potongan;
      const workerSelect = document.getElementById('gaji-worker');
      const bankName = workerSelect?.getAttribute('data-selected-bank') || workerSelect?.selectedOptions?.[0]?.getAttribute('data-bank') || '';
      const bankAdmin = getBankAdminFee(bankName, total);
      const totalTransfer = Math.max(0, total - bankAdmin);
      const el = document.getElementById('gaji-calc-preview');
      if (!el) return;
      const warning = total < 0 ? '<p class="text-xs text-red-600 font-bold mt-2">Total minus! Cek bonus/potongan dulu ya.</p>' : '';
      el.innerHTML = `<p class="font-black text-indigo-700 mb-2">Simulasi Take Home Pay</p><div class="space-y-1"><div class="flex justify-between"><span>Gaji Pokok</span><strong>${formatIDR(gajiPokok)}</strong></div><div class="flex justify-between text-emerald-700"><span>+ Bonus</span><strong>${formatIDR(bonus)}</strong></div><div class="flex justify-between text-rose-700"><span>- Potongan</span><strong>${formatIDR(potongan)}</strong></div><div class="flex justify-between"><span>- Admin Bank (${bankName || '-'})</span><strong class="text-red-600">${formatIDR(bankAdmin)}</strong></div><div class="flex justify-between border-t pt-2 font-black text-slate-800"><span>Total Pengeluaran (catat)</span><strong>${formatIDR(total)}</strong></div><div class="flex justify-between font-black text-emerald-700"><span>Total Transfer Diterima</span><strong>${formatIDR(totalTransfer)}</strong></div></div>${warning}`;
    }

    window.handleSaveGaji = async function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const workerSelect = document.getElementById('gaji-worker');
      const workerName = workerSelect.options[workerSelect.selectedIndex].text.split(' (')[0]; 
      const gajiPokok = Number(document.getElementById('gaji-jumlah').value) || 0;
      const bonus = Number(document.getElementById('gaji-bonus').value) || 0;
      const potongan = Number(document.getElementById('gaji-potongan').value) || 0;
      const totalTerima = gajiPokok + bonus - potongan;
      const bankName = workerSelect.getAttribute('data-selected-bank') || workerSelect.selectedOptions?.[0]?.getAttribute('data-bank') || '';
      const rekening = workerSelect.getAttribute('data-selected-rek') || workerSelect.selectedOptions?.[0]?.getAttribute('data-rek') || '';
      if (!bankName || bankName === 'Belum diatur' || !rekening || rekening === '-') {
        showLoading(false);
        return customAlert("⚠️ Rekening Belum Lengkap", "Worker belum input nama bank / nomor rekening. Lengkapi dulu di menu Akun sebelum simpan gaji.");
      }
      const bankAdmin = getBankAdminFee(bankName, totalTerima);
      const payloadGaji = { workerUsername: workerSelect.value, bulanTahun: document.getElementById('gaji-bulanTahun').value, jumlahGaji: gajiPokok, bonus: bonus, potongan: potongan, inputBy: CURRENT_USER.Username };
      const payloadPengeluaran = { tanggal: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0], deskripsi: `Pembayaran Gaji: ${workerName} - ${payloadGaji.bulanTahun}`, jumlah: totalTerima, kategori: 'Gaji Pegawai', inputBy: CURRENT_USER.Username };
      showLoading(true);
      try { await serverCall('saveGaji', payloadGaji); await serverCall('savePengeluaran', payloadPengeluaran); closeModal(); customAlert("Berhasil", `Data gaji disimpan. Pengeluaran tercatat ${formatIDR(totalTerima)}; transfer diterima ${formatIDR(Math.max(0,totalTerima-bankAdmin))}.`); refreshData(); } catch (e) { customAlert("Error", "Gagal menyimpan gaji"); } finally { showLoading(false); }
    }

    window.downloadSlipGaji = async function(idGaji) {
      const g = DASHBOARD_CACHE.gaji.find(x => x['ID Gaji'] === idGaji);
      if (!g) { customAlert("Error", "Data gaji tidak ditemukan"); return; }
      window.CURRENT_SLIP_GAJI = g;
      openSlipExportModal();
    }

    window.populateSlipGajiFields = function(g) {
      const userObj = DASHBOARD_CACHE.users.find(u => u.Username === g['Worker Username']);
      const role = userObj ? userObj.Role : '-';
      const nama = userObj ? (userObj['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || userObj.Username) : g['Worker Username'];
      const bankKey = userObj ? Object.keys(userObj).find(k => k.toLowerCase().includes('bank')) : null;
      const rekKey = userObj ? Object.keys(userObj).find(k => k.toLowerCase().includes('rekening')) : null;
      const bank = userObj ? (userObj[bankKey] || userObj['Nama Bank'] || userObj['Bank'] || '-') : '-';
      const rek = userObj ? (userObj[rekKey] || userObj['No Rekening'] || userObj['Rekening'] || '-') : '-';
      const totalTerima = Number(g['Total Terima']) || 0;
      const bankAdmin = getBankAdminFee(bank, totalTerima);
      const totalTransfer = Math.max(0, totalTerima - bankAdmin);
      document.getElementById('sg-periode').innerText = `Periode: ${g['Bulan/Tahun']}`;
      document.getElementById('sg-nama').innerText = nama;
      document.getElementById('sg-role').innerText = `Jabatan: ${role}`;
      document.getElementById('sg-bank-account').innerText = `Bank Tujuan: ${bank} • No Rek: ${rek}`;
      document.getElementById('sg-tgl').innerText = new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', {day:'numeric', month:'long', year:'numeric'});
      document.getElementById('sg-pokok').innerText = formatIDR(g['Gaji Pokok']);
      document.getElementById('sg-bonus').innerText = `+ ${formatIDR(g['Bonus'])}`;
      document.getElementById('sg-potongan').innerText = `- ${formatIDR(g['Potongan'])}`;
      document.getElementById('sg-admin-fee').innerText = `- ${formatIDR(bankAdmin)} ${bankAdmin === 0 ? '(Mandiri bebas admin)' : '(biaya admin transfer)'}`;
      document.getElementById('sg-total').innerText = formatIDR(totalTransfer);
      document.getElementById('sg-ttd-nama').innerText = nama;
      const sign = `LC-SIGN|${g['ID Gaji']}|${g['Bulan/Tahun']}|${nama}|${bank}|${rek}|${totalTransfer}|${new Date().toISOString()}`;
      document.getElementById('sg-digital-sign').innerText = sign;
      return { nama, bankAdmin, totalTransfer };
    };

    window.generateSlipCanvas = async function() {
      const g = window.CURRENT_SLIP_GAJI;
      if (!g) throw new Error('Slip belum dipilih');
      const mTop = Number(document.getElementById('slip-margin-top')?.value || 24);
      const mRight = Number(document.getElementById('slip-margin-right')?.value || 24);
      const mBottom = Number(document.getElementById('slip-margin-bottom')?.value || 24);
      const mLeft = Number(document.getElementById('slip-margin-left')?.value || 24);
      populateSlipGajiFields(g);
      const base = document.getElementById('slip-gaji-capture');
      const wrap = document.createElement('div');
      wrap.style.background = '#fff';
      wrap.style.padding = `${mTop}px ${mRight}px ${mBottom}px ${mLeft}px`;
      wrap.style.width = (595 + mLeft + mRight) + 'px';
      const clone = base.cloneNode(true);
      clone.id = 'slip-gaji-clone-temp';
      wrap.appendChild(clone);
      document.body.appendChild(wrap);
      const canvas = await html2canvas(wrap, { scale: 3, useCORS: true, backgroundColor: '#ffffff' });
      wrap.remove();
      return canvas;
    };

    window.openSlipExportModal = function() {
      const g = window.CURRENT_SLIP_GAJI;
      if (!g) return;
      const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
      body.innerHTML = `
        <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-4">Export Slip Gaji</h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-4">Pilih format export (Gambar/PDF), atur margin, lalu preview sebelum download.</p>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 mb-4">
          <div><label class="text-xs font-bold">Margin Top</label><input id="slip-margin-top" type="number" value="24" min="0" class="w-full p-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="text-xs font-bold">Margin Right</label><input id="slip-margin-right" type="number" value="24" min="0" class="w-full p-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="text-xs font-bold">Margin Bottom</label><input id="slip-margin-bottom" type="number" value="24" min="0" class="w-full p-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="text-xs font-bold">Margin Left</label><input id="slip-margin-left" type="number" value="24" min="0" class="w-full p-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="flex gap-2 mb-4">
          <button onclick="previewSlipGajiExport()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">Preview</button>
          <button onclick="exportSlipGaji('png')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">Download Gambar</button>
          <button onclick="exportSlipGaji('pdf')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-white text-xs font-bold">Download PDF</button>
        </div>
        <div id="slip-preview-box" class="border rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-3 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">Belum ada preview.</div>
      `;
      document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
    };

    window.previewSlipGajiExport = async function() {
      showLoading(true, 'Membuat preview slip...');
      try {
        const canvas = await generateSlipCanvas();
        const box = document.getElementById('slip-preview-box');
        box.innerHTML = `<img src="${canvas.toDataURL('image/png')}" class="w-full rounded-xl border">`;
      } catch (e) {
        customAlert('Error', 'Gagal membuat preview slip.');
      } finally { showLoading(false); }
    };

    window.exportSlipGaji = async function(format = 'pdf') {
      showLoading(true, `Menyiapkan slip ${format.toUpperCase()}...`);
      try {
        const g = window.CURRENT_SLIP_GAJI;
        const { nama } = populateSlipGajiFields(g);
        const canvas = await generateSlipCanvas();
        if (format === 'png') {
          const link = document.createElement('a');
          link.href = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
          link.download = `LC-SlipGaji-${nama.replace(/ /g, '_')}-${g['Bulan/Tahun']}.png`;
          link.click();
        } else {
          const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf;
          const pdf = new jsPDF('p', 'mm', 'a4', true);
          const imgData = canvas.toDataURL('image/png', 1.0);
          const pdfWidth = pdf.internal.pageSize.getWidth();
          const pdfHeight = (canvas.height * pdfWidth) / canvas.width;
          pdf.addImage(imgData, 'PNG', 0, 0, pdfWidth, pdfHeight, undefined, 'SLOW');
          pdf.save(`LC-SlipGaji-${nama.replace(/ /g, '_')}-${g['Bulan/Tahun']}.pdf`);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        customAlert('Error', `Gagal export slip ${format.toUpperCase()}.`);
      } finally { showLoading(false); }
    };
window.fillInvoiceCaptureSimple = function(orderId) {
  const order = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders || []).find(o => o['ID PESANAN'] === orderId);
  if (!order) throw new Error('Pesanan tidak ditemukan');
  const fin = calculateOrderFinance(order);
  const orderDate = order['WAKTU  INPUT PESANAN']
    ? new Date(order['WAKTU  INPUT PESANAN']).toLocaleDateString('id-ID')
    : new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID');
  const isPaidFull = String(order['STATUS PEMBAYARAN'] || '').toLowerCase().includes('paid full')
    || String(order['TAHAPAN PESANAN'] || '').toLowerCase() === 'selesai';
  const dpPercent = Number(fin.dpP) || 0;
  const originalDpAmount = Number(fin.dpAmount) || 0; // tetap dipakai untuk riwayat
  const summaryDpAmount = isPaidFull ? 0 : originalDpAmount; // ringkasan invoice
  const pelunasan = isPaidFull ? (Number(fin.net) - originalDpAmount) : 0;
  const sisaTagihan = isPaidFull ? 0 : Math.max(0, Number(fin.net) - originalDpAmount);

  document.getElementById('inv-id-display').innerText = orderId;
  document.getElementById('inv-tgl-display').innerText = new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID');
  document.getElementById('inv-nama-display').innerText = order['NAMA PELANGGAN'] || '-';
  document.getElementById('inv-hp-display').innerText = order['NO HANDPHONE'] || '-';
  document.getElementById('inv-status-display').innerText = isPaidFull ? 'Paid Full' : (order['STATUS PEMBAYARAN'] || '-');
  document.getElementById('inv-layanan-display').innerText = order['LAYANAN'] || '-';
  document.getElementById('inv-qty-display').innerText = order['QTY'] || 1;
  document.getElementById('inv-subtotal-display1').innerText = formatIDR(fin.gross);
  document.getElementById('inv-subtotal-display2').innerText = formatIDR(fin.gross);
  document.getElementById('inv-rincian-display').innerText = order['RINCIAN PESANAN'] || '-';
  document.getElementById('inv-subtotal-display3').innerText = formatIDR(fin.gross);
  document.getElementById('inv-discount-display').innerText = `- ${formatIDR(fin.gross - fin.net)}`;
  document.getElementById('inv-net-display').innerText = formatIDR(fin.net);
  document.getElementById('inv-dp-display').innerText = formatIDR(summaryDpAmount);
  document.getElementById('inv-total-big').innerText = formatIDR(sisaTagihan);

  const historyBody = document.getElementById('inv-history-body');
  if (historyBody) {
    const rows = [];
    if (originalDpAmount > 0) {
      rows.push(`
        <tr style="border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;">
          <td style="padding:6px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;">${orderDate}</td>
          <td style="padding:6px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;">Down Payment (DP)</td>
          <td style="padding:6px;text-align:center;">${dpPercent}%</td>
          <td style="padding:6px;text-align:right;color:#16a34a;font-weight:700;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;">${formatIDR(originalDpAmount)}</td>
        </tr>
      `);
    }
    if (isPaidFull) {
      rows.push(`
        <tr>
          <td style="padding:6px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;">${new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID')}</td>
          <td style="padding:6px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;">Pelunasan</td>
          <td style="padding:6px;text-align:center;">${originalDpAmount > 0 ? (100 - dpPercent) : 100}%</td>
          <td style="padding:6px;text-align:right;color:#16a34a;font-weight:700;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;">${formatIDR(Math.max(0, pelunasan))}</td>
        </tr>
      `);
    }
    if (rows.length === 0) {
      rows.push(`<tr><td colspan="4" style="padding:8px;color:#64748b;text-align:center;">Belum ada riwayat pembayaran</td></tr>`);
    }
    historyBody.innerHTML = rows.join('');
  }
  return { order, fin };
};

window.generateInvoiceCanvas = async function(orderId) {
  fillInvoiceCaptureSimple(orderId);
  const mTop = Number(document.getElementById('inv-margin-top')?.value || 24);
  const mRight = Number(document.getElementById('inv-margin-right')?.value || 24);
  const mBottom = Number(document.getElementById('inv-margin-bottom')?.value || 24);
  const mLeft = Number(document.getElementById('inv-margin-left')?.value || 24);
  const base = document.getElementById('invoice-capture');
  const wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.style.background = '#fff';
  wrap.style.padding = `${mTop}px ${mRight}px ${mBottom}px ${mLeft}px`;
  wrap.style.width = (595 + mLeft + mRight) + 'px';
  const clone = base.cloneNode(true);
  clone.id = 'invoice-capture-clone-temp';
  wrap.appendChild(clone);
  document.body.appendChild(wrap);
  const canvas = await html2canvas(wrap, { scale: 3, useCORS: true, backgroundColor: '#ffffff' });
  wrap.remove();
  return canvas;
};

window.openInvoiceExportModal = function(orderId) {
  window.CURRENT_INVOICE_ORDER = orderId;
  const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
  body.innerHTML = `
    <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-4">Export Invoice Professional</h3>
    <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-4">Atur margin, preview, lalu unduh PDF atau gambar.</p>
    <div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 mb-4">
      <div><label class="text-xs font-bold">Margin Top</label><input id="inv-margin-top" type="number" value="24" min="0" class="w-full p-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
      <div><label class="text-xs font-bold">Margin Right</label><input id="inv-margin-right" type="number" value="24" min="0" class="w-full p-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
      <div><label class="text-xs font-bold">Margin Bottom</label><input id="inv-margin-bottom" type="number" value="24" min="0" class="w-full p-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
      <div><label class="text-xs font-bold">Margin Left</label><input id="inv-margin-left" type="number" value="24" min="0" class="w-full p-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="flex gap-2 mb-4">
      <button onclick="previewInvoiceExport()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">Preview</button>
      <button onclick="exportInvoiceFile('png')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">Unduh Gambar</button>
      <button onclick="exportInvoiceFile('pdf')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-white text-xs font-bold">Unduh PDF</button>
    </div>
    <div id="invoice-preview-box" class="border rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-3 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">Belum ada preview.</div>`;
  document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
};

window.previewInvoiceExport = async function() {
  showLoading(true, 'Membuat preview invoice...');
  try {
    const canvas = await generateInvoiceCanvas(window.CURRENT_INVOICE_ORDER);
    document.getElementById('invoice-preview-box').innerHTML = `<img src="${canvas.toDataURL('image/png')}" class="w-full rounded-xl border">`;
  } catch (e) { customAlert('Error', 'Gagal membuat preview invoice.'); } finally { showLoading(false); }
};

window.exportInvoiceFile = async function(format = 'pdf') {
  showLoading(true, `Menyiapkan invoice ${format.toUpperCase()}...`);
  try {
    const canvas = await generateInvoiceCanvas(window.CURRENT_INVOICE_ORDER);
    if (format === 'png') {
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
      link.download = `LC-Invoice-${window.CURRENT_INVOICE_ORDER}.png`;
      link.click();
    } else {
      const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf;
      const pdf = new jsPDF('p', 'mm', 'a4');
      const imgData = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.9);
      const pdfWidth = pdf.internal.pageSize.getWidth();
      const pdfHeight = (canvas.height * pdfWidth) / canvas.width;
      pdf.addImage(imgData, 'JPEG', 0, 0, pdfWidth, pdfHeight);
      pdf.save(`LC-Invoice-${window.CURRENT_INVOICE_ORDER}.pdf`);
    }
  } catch (e) { customAlert('Error', 'Gagal export invoice.'); } finally { showLoading(false); }
};

// ==================== FIX: INVOICE & LAPORAN ====================
window.generateInvoice = async function(orderId) {
  const order = DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.find(o => o['ID PESANAN'] === orderId);
  if (!order) return customAlert("❌ Error", "Pesanan tidak ditemukan");

  const fin = calculateOrderFinance(order);
  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: '2-digit', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
  const orderDate = order['WAKTU  INPUT PESANAN'] 
    ? new Date(order['WAKTU  INPUT PESANAN']).toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: '2-digit', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }) 
    : today;

  // Logika status pembayaran
  let sisaTagihan = fin.balance;
  let statusBayar = order['STATUS PEMBAYARAN'] || 'Unpaid';

  if (statusBayar === 'Paid Full' || order['TAHAPAN PESANAN'] === 'Selesai') {
    sisaTagihan = 0;
    statusBayar = 'Paid Full';
  }

  const invHTML = `
    <div style="font-family: system-ui, sans-serif; max-width:595px; margin:0 auto; background:white; padding:40px 35px; color:#1e293b; line-height:1.6; font-size:13px;">
      
      <!-- HEADER -->
      <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; border-bottom:3px solid #6366f1; padding-bottom:20px; margin-bottom:30px;">
        <div>
          <h1 style="margin:0; font-size:26px; font-weight:900; color:#6366f1;">LENTERA CENDEKIA</h1>
          <p style="margin:4px 0 0; font-size:13px; color:#64748b;">Premium Academic Support</p>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align:right;">
          <h2 style="margin:0; font-size:24px; font-weight:800;">INVOICE</h2>
          <p style="margin:6px 0 0; color:#6366f1; font-weight:700;">#${orderId}</p>
          <p style="margin:4px 0 0; font-size:13px; color:#64748b;">${today}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- INFO PELANGGAN -->
      <div style="margin-bottom:30px;">
        <strong style="font-size:11px; text-transform:uppercase; color:#64748b;">DITERBITKAN UNTUK</strong>
        <p style="margin:8px 0 4px; font-size:17px; font-weight:700;">${order['NAMA PELANGGAN']}</p>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#475569;">WA: ${order['NO HANDPHONE']}</p>
      </div>

      <!-- TABEL LAYANAN -->
      <table style="width:100%; border-collapse:collapse; margin-bottom:30px; border:1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius:12px; overflow:hidden;">
        <thead>
          <tr style="background:#f8fafc;">
            <th style="text-align:left; padding:14px 18px; font-size:12px; color:#64748b;">Layanan</th>
            <th style="text-align:center; padding:14px 18px; font-size:12px; color:#64748b;">Qty</th>
            <th style="text-align:right; padding:14px 18px; font-size:12px; color:#64748b;">Total</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr>
            <td style="padding:18px; font-weight:600;">${order.LAYANAN}</td>
            <td style="padding:18px; text-align:center;">${order.QTY || 1} ${order.SATUAN || 'Unit'}</td>
            <td style="padding:18px; text-align:right; font-weight:700;">${formatIDR(fin.net)}</td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

      <!-- RIWAYAT PEMBAYARAN (BARU) -->
      <div style="background:#f8fafc; padding:25px; border-radius:16px; margin-bottom:30px;">
        <strong style="font-size:13px; margin-bottom:15px; display:block;">RIWAYAT PEMBAYARAN</strong>
        <table style="width:100%; border-collapse:collapse; font-size:13px;">
          <thead>
            <tr style="background:#e2e8f0;">
              <th style="text-align:left; padding:10px 12px;">Tanggal</th>
              <th style="text-align:left; padding:10px 12px;">Jenis Pembayaran</th>
              <th style="text-align:center; padding:10px 12px;">Persen</th>
              <th style="text-align:right; padding:10px 12px;">Nominal</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            <!-- DP -->
            ${fin.dpAmount > 0 ? `
            <tr style="border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;">
              <td style="padding:10px 12px;">${orderDate}</td>
              <td style="padding:10px 12px; font-weight:600;">Down Payment (DP)</td>
              <td style="padding:10px 12px; text-align:center;">${fin.dpP}%</td>
              <td style="padding:10px 12px; text-align:right; color:#16a34a; font-weight:700;">${formatIDR(fin.dpAmount)}</td>
            </tr>` : ''}
            
            <!-- Pelunasan (jika full paid) -->
            ${sisaTagihan === 0 ? `
            <tr>
              <td style="padding:10px 12px;">${today}</td>
              <td style="padding:10px 12px; font-weight:600;">Pelunasan</td>
              <td style="padding:10px 12px; text-align:center;">100%</td>
              <td style="padding:10px 12px; text-align:right; color:#16a34a; font-weight:700;">${formatIDR(fin.net - fin.dpAmount)}</td>
            </tr>` : ''}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <!-- TOTAL & SISA -->
      <div style="background:#f1f5f9; padding:25px; border-radius:16px; text-align:right;">
        <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; font-size:18px;">
          <span style="font-weight:800;">SISA TAGIHAN</span>
          <span style="font-weight:900; color: ${sisaTagihan > 0 ? '#ef4444' : '#16a34a'};">${formatIDR(sisaTagihan)}</span>
        </div>
        ${sisaTagihan === 0 ? `<p style="margin-top:12px; color:#16a34a; font-weight:700;">✅ LUNAS • PAID FULL</p>` : ''}
      </div>

      <div style="margin-top:40px; text-align:center; font-size:12px; color:#64748b;">
        Terima kasih telah mempercayakan karya akademik Anda kepada kami.<br>
        <strong>Lentera Cendekia</strong> • Pontianak • WA: 0858-1169-9699
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  // Render & Generate PDF (sama seperti sebelumnya)
  const captureDiv = document.getElementById('invoice-capture');
  captureDiv.innerHTML = invHTML;

  const container = document.getElementById('invoice-capture-container');
  container.style.visibility = 'visible';
  container.style.zIndex = '1';

  showLoading(true);

  try {
    const canvas = await html2canvas(captureDiv, { scale: 2, useCORS: true, backgroundColor: "#ffffff" });
    const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf;
    const pdf = new jsPDF('p', 'mm', 'a4');
    const imgData = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85);
    const pdfWidth = pdf.internal.pageSize.getWidth();
    const pdfHeight = (canvas.height * pdfWidth) / canvas.width;

    pdf.addImage(imgData, 'JPEG', 0, 0, pdfWidth, pdfHeight);
    pdf.save(`LC-Invoice-${orderId}.pdf`);

    customAlert("✅ Sukses", "Invoice dengan Riwayat Pembayaran telah diunduh");
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    customAlert("❌ Gagal", "Terjadi kesalahan saat membuat invoice");
  } finally {
    container.style.visibility = 'hidden';
    container.style.zIndex = '-999';
    showLoading(false);
  }
};

// ==================== LAPORAN PDF PROFESSIONAL (Harian / Bulanan / Tahunan) ====================
window.generateReport = function(type) {
  showLoading(true);

  try {
    let filteredOrders = DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.filter(o => o['TAHAPAN PESANAN'] === 'Selesai');
    let periodText = '';
    const now = new Date();

    if (type === 'hari') {
      filteredOrders = filteredOrders.filter(o => {
        const d = new Date(o['WAKTU  INPUT PESANAN'] || Date.now());
        return d.toDateString() === now.toDateString();
      });
      periodText = `LAPORAN HARIAN - ${now.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })}`;
    } 
    else if (type === 'bulan') {
      const m = now.getMonth();
      const y = now.getFullYear();
      filteredOrders = filteredOrders.filter(o => {
        const d = new Date(o['WAKTU  INPUT PESANAN']);
        return d.getMonth() === m && d.getFullYear() === y;
      });
      periodText = `LAPORAN BULANAN - ${now.toLocaleString('id-ID', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })}`;
    } 
    else if (type === 'tahun') {
      const y = now.getFullYear();
      filteredOrders = filteredOrders.filter(o => new Date(o['WAKTU  INPUT PESANAN']).getFullYear() === y);
      periodText = `LAPORAN TAHUNAN - ${y}`;
    }

    if (filteredOrders.length === 0) {
      customAlert("Info", "Tidak ada data pesanan selesai untuk periode ini.");
      return;
    }

    const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf;
    const doc = new jsPDF('landscape', 'pt', 'a4');
    const pageWidth = doc.internal.pageSize.getWidth();

    // ==================== HEADER ====================
    doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold");
    doc.setFontSize(24);
    doc.setTextColor(99, 102, 241);
    doc.text("LENTERA CENDEKIA", 40, 50);

    doc.setFontSize(11);
    doc.setTextColor(100, 116, 139);
    doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal");
    doc.text("Premium Academic Support • Pontianak", 40, 68);

    doc.setFontSize(16);
    doc.setTextColor(30, 41, 59);
    doc.text("LAPORAN KEUANGAN", pageWidth - 40, 50, { align: "right" });

    doc.setFontSize(12);
    doc.text(periodText, pageWidth - 40, 68, { align: "right" });

    doc.setDrawColor(226, 232, 240);
    doc.line(40, 85, pageWidth - 40, 85);

    // ==================== TABEL DETAIL ====================
    const tableData = [];

    let grandGross = 0;
    let grandDiskon = 0;
    let grandNet = 0;
    let grandFeeAdmin = 0;
    let grandFeePerusahaan = 0;
    let grandWorkerNet = 0;
    let grandAdminNet = 0;

    filteredOrders.forEach(o => {
      const fin = calculateOrderFinance(o);

      grandGross += fin.gross;
      grandDiskon += (fin.gross - fin.net);
      grandNet += fin.net;
      grandFeeAdmin += fin.feeAdmin;
      grandFeePerusahaan += fin.feeCompany;
      grandWorkerNet += fin.workerNet;
      grandAdminNet += fin.feeAdmin;   // Fee Admin Pusat

      tableData.push([
        o['ID PESANAN'],
        `${o['NAMA PELANGGAN']}\n${o.LAYANAN}`,           // Nama Pelanggan + Layanan (1 kolom)
        o['RINCIAN PESANAN'] ? o['RINCIAN PESANAN'].substring(0, 80) + (o['RINCIAN PESANAN'].length > 80 ? '...' : '') : '-',
        formatIDR(fin.gross),
        formatIDR(fin.gross - fin.net),
        formatIDR(fin.net),
        formatIDR(fin.feeAdmin),
        formatIDR(fin.feeCompany),
        formatIDR(fin.workerNet),
        formatIDR(fin.feeAdmin)   // Diterima Admin
      ]);
    });

    doc.autoTable({
      startY: 110,
      head: [[
        'ID Pesanan',
        'Pelanggan & Layanan',
        'Rincian',
        'Gross',
        'Diskon',
        'Net',
        'Fee Admin',
        'Fee Perusahaan',
        'Bersih Worker',
        'Diterima Admin'
      ]],
      body: tableData,
      theme: 'grid',
      headStyles: {
        fillColor: [99, 102, 241],
        textColor: 255,
        fontStyle: 'bold',
        fontSize: 9,
        halign: 'center'
      },
      bodyStyles: { fontSize: 8, cellPadding: 5 },
      columnStyles: {
        0: { cellWidth: 55, halign: 'center' },
        1: { cellWidth: 110, halign: 'left' },
        2: { cellWidth: 110, halign: 'left' },
        3: { halign: 'right' },
        4: { halign: 'right', textColor: [239, 68, 68] },
        5: { halign: 'right', fontStyle: 'bold' },
        6: { halign: 'right', textColor: [220, 38, 38] },
        7: { halign: 'right', textColor: [249, 115, 22] },
        8: { halign: 'right', textColor: [16, 163, 74], fontStyle: 'bold' },
        9: { halign: 'right', textColor: [99, 102, 241], fontStyle: 'bold' }
      },
      alternateRowStyles: { fillColor: [248, 250, 252] }
    });

    // ==================== TOTAL BAR ====================
    const finalY = doc.lastAutoTable.finalY + 25;

    doc.setFontSize(10);
    doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold");
    doc.text("TOTAL KESELURUHAN", 40, finalY);

    doc.autoTable({
      startY: finalY + 10,
      head: [['Keterangan', 'Jumlah']],
      body: [
        ['Total Gross', formatIDR(grandGross)],
        ['Total Diskon', formatIDR(grandDiskon)],
        ['Total Net', formatIDR(grandNet)],
        ['Total Fee Admin Pusat', formatIDR(grandFeeAdmin)],
        ['Total Fee Perusahaan', formatIDR(grandFeePerusahaan)],
        ['Total Bersih Diterima Worker', formatIDR(grandWorkerNet)],
        ['Total Diterima Admin', formatIDR(grandAdminNet)]
      ],
      theme: 'grid',
      headStyles: { fillColor: [99, 102, 241], textColor: 255, fontStyle: 'bold' },
      columnStyles: { 1: { halign: 'right', fontStyle: 'bold' } }
    });

    // Footer
    const footerY = doc.internal.pageSize.getHeight() - 40;
    doc.setFontSize(9);
    doc.setTextColor(148, 163, 184);
    doc.text("Lentera Cendekia • Laporan Keuangan", pageWidth / 2, footerY, { align: "center" });
    doc.text(`Dicetak pada: ${new Date().toLocaleString('id-ID')}`, pageWidth / 2, footerY + 12, { align: "center" });

    // Simpan PDF
    doc.save(`LC-Laporan-${type.toUpperCase()}-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.pdf`);

    customAlert("✅ Berhasil", `Laporan ${type} telah diunduh dengan format profesional`);

  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    customAlert("❌ Gagal", "Terjadi kesalahan saat membuat laporan PDF");
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};


// ==================== PENDAPATAN LAINNYA ====================
let currentPendapatanPage = 1;
let currentPendapatanFilter = 'all';
const PENDAPATAN_PER_PAGE = 10;

window.openPendapatanModal = function(id = null) {
  const p = id ? DASHBOARD_CACHE.pendapatan.find(x => x['ID Pendapatan'] === id) : null;
  const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
  body.innerHTML = `
    <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6">${id ? 'Edit' : 'Input'} Pendapatan Lainnya</h3>
    <form onsubmit="handleSavePendapatan(event, '${id || ''}')" class="space-y-6">
      <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Tanggal</label><input type="date" id="pendapatan-tanggal" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
      <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Kategori</label>
        <select id="pendapatan-kategori" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none">
          <option value="Penjualan">Penjualan</option>
          <option value="Sponsor">Sponsor</option>
          <option value="Donasi">Donasi</option>
          <option value="Lainnya">Lainnya</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Deskripsi</label><input type="text" id="pendapatan-deskripsi" value="${p ? p['Deskripsi'] : ''}" required class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
      <div><label class="block text-sm font-bold mb-2 text-slate-700">Jumlah (Rp)</label><input type="number" id="pendapatan-jumlah" value="${p ? p['Jumlah'] : ''}" required min="0" class="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 outline-none"></div>
      <button type="submit" class="w-full py-4 gradient-primary text-white rounded-2xl font-bold shadow-lg">Simpan Pendapatan</button>
    </form>
  `;
  document.getElementById('pendapatan-tanggal').valueAsDate = p ? new Date(p['Tanggal']) : new Date();
  document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
};

window.handleSavePendapatan = async function(e, id) {
  e.preventDefault();
  showLoading(true);
  const payload = {
    tanggal: document.getElementById('pendapatan-tanggal').value,
    deskripsi: document.getElementById('pendapatan-deskripsi').value,
    jumlah: document.getElementById('pendapatan-jumlah').value,
    kategori: document.getElementById('pendapatan-kategori').value,
    inputBy: CURRENT_USER.Username
  };
  try {
    if (id) await serverCall('updatePendapatan', {...payload, id});
    else await serverCall('savePendapatan', payload);
    closeModal();
    refreshData();
    customAlert("Berhasil", id ? "Pendapatan diupdate" : "Pendapatan berhasil disimpan");
  } catch(e) {
    customAlert("Error", "Gagal menyimpan pendapatan");
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};

window.renderPendapatanTable = function() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('pendapatan-table-body');
  const pagination = document.getElementById('pendapatan-pagination');
  if (!tbody || !DASHBOARD_CACHE?.pendapatan) return;

  let data = DASHBOARD_CACHE.pendapatan;

  if (currentPendapatanFilter !== 'all') {
    data = data.filter(p => p['Kategori'] === currentPendapatanFilter);
  }

  const totalPages = Math.ceil(data.length / PENDAPATAN_PER_PAGE) || 1;
  currentPendapatanPage = Math.min(currentPendapatanPage, totalPages);

  const start = (currentPendapatanPage - 1) * PENDAPATAN_PER_PAGE;
  const paginated = data.slice(start, start + PENDAPATAN_PER_PAGE);

  tbody.innerHTML = paginated.map(p => `
    <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
      <td class="px-6 py-4">${new Date(p['Tanggal']).toLocaleDateString('id-ID')}</td>
      <td class="px-6 py-4 font-mono text-xs">${p['ID Pendapatan']}</td>
      <td class="px-6 py-4"><span class="px-3 py-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 rounded-full text-xs font-bold">${p['Kategori']}</span></td>
      <td class="px-6 py-4">${p['Deskripsi']}</td>
      <td class="px-6 py-4 text-right font-bold text-emerald-600">${formatIDR(p['Jumlah'])}</td>
      <td class="px-6 py-4 text-xs">${p['Input By']}</td>
     <td class="px-6 py-4 flex justify-center gap-2">
        <button onclick="editPendapatan('${p['ID Pendapatan']}')" 
                class="p-3 bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200 text-amber-700 rounded-xl transition-all border border-amber-200 shadow-sm">
          <i data-lucide="edit" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
        </button>
        <button onclick="deletePendapatan('${p['ID Pendapatan']}')" 
                class="p-3 bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-700 rounded-xl transition-all border border-red-200 shadow-sm">
          <i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
        </button>
      </td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');

  // Pagination
  let html = '';
  for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
    html += i === currentPendapatanPage 
      ? `<button class="w-8 h-8 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">${i}</button>` 
      : `<button onclick="changePendapatanPage(${i})" class="w-8 h-8 border hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">${i}</button>`;
  }
  pagination.innerHTML = `
    <button onclick="changePendapatanPage(${currentPendapatanPage-1})" ${currentPendapatanPage===1?'disabled class="opacity-50"':''} class="w-8 h-8 border rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center"><i data-lucide="chevron-left" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>
    ${html}
    <button onclick="changePendapatanPage(${currentPendapatanPage+1})" ${currentPendapatanPage===totalPages?'disabled class="opacity-50"':''} class="w-8 h-8 border rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center"><i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>
  `;
    if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

window.changePendapatanPage = function(page) { currentPendapatanPage = page; renderPendapatanTable(); };
window.changePendapatanFilter = function(filter) { currentPendapatanFilter = filter; currentPendapatanPage = 1; renderPendapatanTable(); };
window.editPendapatan = function(id) { openPendapatanModal(id); };
window.deletePendapatan = async function(id) {
  if (!confirm("Hapus data pendapatan ini?")) return;
  showLoading(true);
  try {
    await serverCall('deletePendapatan', id);
    refreshData();
    customAlert("Berhasil", "Pendapatan dihapus");
  } catch(e) { customAlert("Error", "Gagal menghapus"); } finally { showLoading(false); }
};

window.openExpenseReportModal = function() {
  const body = document.getElementById('modal-content-body');
  const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
  let yearOptions = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) yearOptions += `<option value="${currentYear - i}">${currentYear - i}</option>`;
  body.innerHTML = `
    <h3 class="text-2xl font-black mb-6 flex items-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="file-text" class="text-slate-700"></i> Unduh Laporan Pengeluaran</h3>
    <div class="space-y-5">
      <div class="bg-slate-50 p-5 rounded-2xl border flex items-center justify-between">
        <div><h4 class="font-bold">Laporan Harian</h4><p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Pengeluaran hari ini</p></div>
        <button onclick="downloadExpensePDF('hari')" class="px-5 py-2.5 bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-bold">Unduh PDF</button>
      </div>
      <div class="bg-slate-50 p-5 rounded-2xl border">
        <div class="flex gap-3 items-end">
          <div class="flex-1"><label class="text-xs font-bold">Bulan</label><select id="exp-month" class="w-full p-3 rounded-xl border text-sm"><option value="0">Januari</option><option value="1">Februari</option><option value="2">Maret</option><option value="3">April</option><option value="4">Mei</option><option value="5">Juni</option><option value="6">Juli</option><option value="7">Agustus</option><option value="8">September</option><option value="9">Oktober</option><option value="10">November</option><option value="11">Desember</option></select></div>
          <div class="w-40"><label class="text-xs font-bold">Tahun</label><select id="exp-month-year" class="w-full p-3 rounded-xl border text-sm">${yearOptions}</select></div>
          <button onclick="downloadExpensePDF('bulan')" class="px-5 py-2.5 bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-bold">Unduh</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="bg-slate-50 p-5 rounded-2xl border flex items-center justify-between">
        <div><h4 class="font-bold">Laporan Tahunan</h4><select id="exp-year" class="mt-2 p-2 rounded-xl border text-sm w-32">${yearOptions}</select></div>
        <button onclick="downloadExpensePDF('tahun')" class="px-5 py-2.5 bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-bold">Unduh PDF</button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  document.getElementById('exp-month') && (document.getElementById('exp-month').value = new Date().getMonth());
  document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
};

window.downloadExpensePDF = function(type) {
  const all = DASHBOARD_CACHE.pengeluaran || [];
  const now = new Date();
  let data = all;
  let title = '';
  let periodLabel = '';
  if (type === 'hari') {
    data = all.filter(p => new Date(p['Tanggal'] || p['TANGGAL']).toDateString() === now.toDateString());
    title = `Laporan Pengeluaran Harian - ${now.toLocaleDateString('id-ID')}`;
    periodLabel = now.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: '2-digit', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
  } else if (type === 'bulan') {
    const m = Number(document.getElementById('exp-month')?.value || now.getMonth());
    const y = Number(document.getElementById('exp-month-year')?.value || now.getFullYear());
    data = all.filter(p => { const d = new Date(p['Tanggal'] || p['TANGGAL']); return d.getMonth()===m && d.getFullYear()===y; });
    title = `Laporan Pengeluaran Bulanan - ${m+1}/${y}`;
    periodLabel = new Date(y, m, 1).toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
  } else {
    const y = Number(document.getElementById('exp-year')?.value || now.getFullYear());
    data = all.filter(p => new Date(p['Tanggal'] || p['TANGGAL']).getFullYear() === y);
    title = `Laporan Pengeluaran Tahunan - ${y}`;
    periodLabel = `${y}`;
  }

  const totalPengeluaran = data.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (Number(p['Jumlah']) || 0), 0);
  const totalTransaksi = data.length;
  const ringkasanKategori = data.reduce((acc, p) => {
    const key = String(p['Kategori'] || 'Lainnya').trim() || 'Lainnya';
    acc[key] = (acc[key] || 0) + (Number(p['Jumlah']) || 0);
    return acc;
  }, {});
  const topKategori = Object.entries(ringkasanKategori).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0];

  const signedAt = new Date().toLocaleString('id-ID');
  const financeOfficer = CURRENT_USER?.['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || CURRENT_USER?.Username || 'Divisi Keuangan';
  const digitalSignature = `LC-FIN|${financeOfficer}|${type}|${periodLabel}|${totalPengeluaran}|${new Date().toISOString()}`;

  const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf;
  const doc = new jsPDF('p', 'mm', 'a4');

  // Header profesional
  doc.setFillColor(30, 41, 59);
  doc.rect(0, 0, 210, 36, 'F');
  doc.setTextColor(255, 255, 255);
  doc.setFontSize(15);
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.text('LENTERA CENDEKIA', 14, 14);
  doc.setFontSize(10);
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'normal');
  doc.text('Laporan Keuangan • Pengeluaran', 14, 20);
  doc.text(`Generated: ${signedAt}`, 14, 26);
  doc.text(`Periode: ${periodLabel}`, 14, 31);

  doc.setTextColor(30, 41, 59);
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(13);
  doc.text(title, 14, 46);

  // Ringkasan
  doc.setDrawColor(226, 232, 240);
  doc.setFillColor(248, 250, 252);
  doc.roundedRect(14, 51, 182, 30, 3, 3, 'FD');
  doc.setFontSize(9);
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'normal');
  doc.text(`Total Transaksi: ${totalTransaksi}`, 18, 60);
  doc.text(`Total Pengeluaran: ${formatIDR(totalPengeluaran)}`, 18, 67);
  doc.text(`Kategori Dominan: ${topKategori ? `${topKategori[0]} (${formatIDR(topKategori[1])})` : '-'}`, 18, 74);

  const rows = data.map(p => [
    new Date(p['Tanggal'] || p['TANGGAL']).toLocaleDateString('id-ID'),
    p['ID Pengeluaran'],
    p['Kategori'],
    p['Deskripsi'],
    formatIDR(p['Jumlah'])
  ]);
  doc.autoTable({
    startY: 88,
    head: [['Tanggal','ID','Kategori','Deskripsi','Jumlah']],
    body: rows,
    styles: { fontSize: 8, cellPadding: 2.8, textColor: [30, 41, 59] },
    headStyles: { fillColor: [51, 65, 85], textColor: 255, halign: 'center' },
    alternateRowStyles: { fillColor: [248, 250, 252] },
    columnStyles: { 4: { halign: 'right' } }
  });

  const endY = (doc.lastAutoTable && doc.lastAutoTable.finalY) ? doc.lastAutoTable.finalY : 100;
  const signY = Math.max(endY + 16, 238);
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'normal');
  doc.setFontSize(9);
  doc.text('Disahkan oleh,', 140, signY);
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.text('Divisi Keuangan', 140, signY + 7);
  doc.setFont('courier', 'normal');
  doc.setFontSize(7);
  doc.text(`Digital Sign: ${digitalSignature}`, 14, signY + 17, { maxWidth: 180 });

  doc.save(`LC-Pengeluaran-${type}.pdf`);
};

              <option value="6">Juli</option><option value="7">Agustus</option><option value="8">September</option>
              <option value="9">Oktober</option><option value="10">November</option><option value="11">Desember</option>

          <div class="w-1/3"><select id="pend-month-year" class="w-full p-3 rounded-xl border outline-none text-sm">${yearOptions}</select></div>
          <button onclick="downloadPendapatanPDF('bulan')" class="px-6 py-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-sm shadow-md transition-all">Unduh</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="bg-slate-50 p-6 rounded-3xl border flex items-center justify-between">
        <div><h4 class="font-bold text-slate-800">Laporan Tahunan</h4><div class="mt-2"><select id="pend-year" class="p-2 rounded-xl border outline-none text-sm w-32">${yearOptions}</select></div></div>
        <button onclick="downloadPendapatanPDF('tahun')" class="px-6 py-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-sm shadow-md transition-all">Unduh PDF</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
  document.getElementById('pend-month').value = new Date().getMonth();
  document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');
};

window.downloadPendapatanPDF = async function(type) {
  if (!DASHBOARD_CACHE || !DASHBOARD_CACHE.pendapatan) {
    customAlert("Error", "Data pendapatan belum dimuat.");
    return;
  }

  let data = [...DASHBOARD_CACHE.pendapatan];
  const now = new Date();
  let periodText = '';

  if (type === 'hari') {
    data = data.filter(p => new Date(p['Tanggal']).toDateString() === now.toDateString());
    periodText = `HARIAN - ${now.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })}`;
  } else if (type === 'bulan') {
    const m = parseInt(document.getElementById('pend-month').value);
    const y = parseInt(document.getElementById('pend-month-year').value);
    data = data.filter(p => {
      const d = new Date(p['Tanggal']);
      return d.getMonth() === m && d.getFullYear() === y;
    });
    const monthNames = ["Januari","Februari","Maret","April","Mei","Juni","Juli","Agustus","September","Oktober","November","Desember"];
    periodText = `BULANAN - ${monthNames[m]} ${y}`;
  } else if (type === 'tahun') {
    const y = parseInt(document.getElementById('pend-year').value);
    data = data.filter(p => new Date(p['Tanggal']).getFullYear() === y);
    periodText = `TAHUNAN - ${y}`;
  }

  if (data.length === 0) {
    customAlert("Info", "Tidak ada data pendapatan untuk periode tersebut.");
    return;
  }

  let totalPendapatan = 0;
  const tableData = data.map(p => {
    const jml = Number(p['Jumlah']) || 0;
    totalPendapatan += jml;
    return [
      p['ID Pendapatan'],
      new Date(p['Tanggal']).toLocaleDateString('id-ID'),
      p['Kategori'],
      p['Deskripsi'],
      p['Input By'],
      formatIDR(jml)
    ];
  });

  const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf;
  const doc = new jsPDF('landscape', 'pt', 'a4');
  const pageWidth = doc.internal.pageSize.getWidth();

  doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); 
  doc.setFontSize(22); 
  doc.text("LENTERA CENDEKIA", 40, 50);
  doc.setFontSize(10); 
  doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); 
  doc.text("Premium Academic Support", 40, 65);
  doc.setFontSize(14); 
  doc.setTextColor(16, 163, 74); 
  doc.text("LAPORAN PENDAPATAN", pageWidth - 40, 50, { align: 'right' });
  doc.setFontSize(10); 
  doc.text(periodText, pageWidth - 40, 65, { align: 'right' });

  doc.autoTable({
    startY: 100,
    head: [['ID', 'Tanggal', 'Kategori', 'Deskripsi', 'Input Oleh', 'Jumlah (Rp)']],
    body: tableData,
    theme: 'grid',
    headStyles: { fillColor: [16, 163, 74], textColor: 255, fontStyle: 'bold' },
    columnStyles: { 5: { halign: 'right', fontStyle: 'bold' } }
  });

  let finalY = doc.lastAutoTable.finalY + 30;
  doc.setFontSize(14);
  doc.text(`TOTAL PENDAPATAN: ${formatIDR(totalPendapatan)}`, 40, finalY);

  doc.save(`LC-Pendapatan-${type}-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.pdf`);
  customAlert("✅ Sukses", "Laporan Pendapatan telah diunduh");
};

  openPendapatanModal(id);   // ini akan membuka form edit
};

window.deletePendapatan = async function(id) {
  if (!confirm("Yakin ingin menghapus data pendapatan ini?")) return;
  
  showLoading(true);
  try {
    await serverCall('deletePendapatan', id);
    refreshData();                    // refresh tabel
    customAlert("Berhasil", "Pendapatan berhasil dihapus");
  } catch(e) {
    customAlert("Error", "Gagal menghapus pendapatan");
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};

// ==================== ANIMASI STATISTIK ====================
function animateCount(id, target, duration = 1800) {
  const obj = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!obj) return;
  let start = 0;
  const range = target - start;
  let startTime = null;

  const step = (timestamp) => {
    if (!startTime) startTime = timestamp;
    const progress = Math.min((timestamp - startTime) / duration, 1);
    const value = Math.floor(progress * range + start);
    obj.textContent = value;
    if (progress < 1) window.requestAnimationFrame(step);
    else obj.textContent = target;
  };
  window.requestAnimationFrame(step);
}

// ==================== TESTIMONIAL CAROUSEL ====================
// Tunggu sampai semua elemen HTML dimuat
// ==================== LOAD EVENT - FINAL FIX ====================
window.addEventListener('load', () => {
  // Fix error yang kamu laporkan
  renderPromoSlider();
  loadRunningText();           // ← sudah diganti dari updateRunningText
  
  // Testimonial carousel
  const track = document.getElementById('testimonial-track');
  const totalSlides = track.children.length;
  let currentSlide = 0;
  function slideTestimonial() {
    currentSlide = (currentSlide + 1) % totalSlides;
    track.style.transform = `translateX(-${currentSlide * 100}%)`;
  }
  let slideInterval = setInterval(slideTestimonial, 4500);

  // Animasi statistik
  if (typeof animateCount === "function") {
    animateCount('stat-mahasiswa', 623);
    animateCount('stat-proyek', 780);
    animateCount('stat-tahun', 4);
    animateCount('stat-kepuasan', 90);
  }

  // Inisialisasi lucide icons
  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
});

// ==================== UPDATE KARTU MONITORING (ADMIN & WORKER) ====================
window.updateMonitorStats = function() {
  if (!DASHBOARD_CACHE || !CURRENT_USER) return;

  const role = CURRENT_USER.Role.toLowerCase();
  const isBendahara = role.includes('bendahara');
  const isAdmin = role.includes('admin');
  const isWorker = role.includes('worker');

  let totalHak = 0;
  let totalDiklaim = 0;

  if (DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders) {
    DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.forEach(o => {
      const status = window.safeGet(o, 'TAHAPAN PESANAN');
      if (status !== 'Selesai') return;

      const fin = calculateOrderFinance(o);
      const assignedWorker = window.safeGet(o, 'WORKER');

      if (isAdmin) {
        totalHak += Number(fin.feeAdmin) || 0;
      } else if (isWorker && assignedWorker.toLowerCase() === CURRENT_USER.Username.toLowerCase()) {
        totalHak += Number(fin.workerNet) || 0;
      } else if (isBendahara) {
        totalHak += Number(fin.net) || 0;
      }
    });
  }

  if (DASHBOARD_CACHE.gaji && (isAdmin || isWorker)) {
    DASHBOARD_CACHE.gaji.forEach(g => {
      const namaGaji = String(g['Nama Pegawai'] || g['Nama'] || g['Worker Username'] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const userName = String(CURRENT_USER.Nama || CURRENT_USER.Username || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (namaGaji === userName) {
        totalDiklaim += Number(g['Jumlah']) || Number(g['Total Terima']) || 0;
      }
    });
  }

  const sisaBelumDiklaim = totalHak - totalDiklaim;

  if (isBendahara) {
    const totalPendapatanLain = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.pendapatan || []).reduce((sum, p) => sum + (Number(p['Jumlah']) || 0), 0);
    const totalPendapatanKeseluruhan = totalHak + totalPendapatanLain;
    const totalPengeluaran = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.pengeluaran || []).reduce((sum, p) => sum + (Number(p['Jumlah']) || 0), 0);
    const sisaKas = totalPendapatanKeseluruhan - totalPengeluaran;

    document.getElementById('stat-income').innerText = formatIDR(totalPendapatanKeseluruhan);
    document.getElementById('stat-pengeluaran').innerText = formatIDR(totalPengeluaran);
    document.getElementById('stat-net-balance').innerText = formatIDR(sisaKas);

    document.getElementById('label-stat-income').innerText = 'Total Pendapatan Keseluruhan';
    document.getElementById('label-stat-pengeluaran').innerText = 'Total Pengeluaran';
    document.getElementById('label-stat-sisa').innerText = 'Sisa Kas Bersih Perusahaan';
  } else {
    document.getElementById('stat-income').innerText = formatIDR(totalHak);
    document.getElementById('stat-pengeluaran').innerText = formatIDR(totalDiklaim);
    document.getElementById('stat-net-balance').innerText = formatIDR(sisaBelumDiklaim);

    document.getElementById('label-stat-income').innerText = 'Total Hak Pendapatan';
    document.getElementById('label-stat-pengeluaran').innerText = 'Sudah Diklaim';
    document.getElementById('label-stat-sisa').innerText = 'Sisa Belum Diklaim';
  }

  // Update kartu di tab Pengeluaran Bendahara
  if (isBendahara) {
    const totalPendapatanLain = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.pendapatan || []).reduce((sum, p) => sum + (Number(p['Jumlah']) || 0), 0);
    const totalPendapatanKeseluruhan = totalHak + totalPendapatanLain;
    const totalPengeluaran = (DASHBOARD_CACHE.pengeluaran || []).reduce((sum, p) => sum + (Number(p['Jumlah']) || 0), 0);
    const sisaKas = totalPendapatanKeseluruhan - totalPengeluaran;

    if (document.getElementById('peng-stat-income')) document.getElementById('peng-stat-income').innerText = formatIDR(totalPendapatanKeseluruhan);
    if (document.getElementById('peng-stat-pengeluaran')) document.getElementById('peng-stat-pengeluaran').innerText = formatIDR(totalPengeluaran);
    if (document.getElementById('peng-stat-net')) document.getElementById('peng-stat-net').innerText = formatIDR(sisaKas);
  }

  const chartCard = document.getElementById('business-chart-card');
  const summaryNote = document.getElementById('business-summary-note');
  if (chartCard) {
    chartCard.classList.toggle('hidden', !(isAdmin || isBendahara));
  }
  if (isAdmin || isBendahara) {
    renderOrderPerWorkerChart();
    if (summaryNote) {
      const allOrders = DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders || [];
      const total = allOrders.length;
      const selesai = allOrders.filter(o => o['TAHAPAN PESANAN'] === 'Selesai').length;
      const proses = allOrders.filter(o => o['TAHAPAN PESANAN'] === 'Proses').length;
      const antrean = allOrders.filter(o => o['TAHAPAN PESANAN'] === 'Antrean').length;
      const completionRate = total > 0 ? Math.round((selesai / total) * 100) : 0;
      const insight = completionRate >= 60
        ? 'Performa bisnis sangat baik. Fokus tingkatkan akuisisi order baru.'
        : completionRate >= 35
          ? 'Performa stabil. Dorong percepatan proses order agar tingkat selesai naik.'
          : 'Perlu optimasi operasional. Prioritaskan antrean dan percepat eksekusi order.';
      summaryNote.innerHTML = `
        <p class="font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Kesimpulan Monitoring</p>
        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm">Total order <strong>${total}</strong> • Selesai <strong>${selesai}</strong> • Proses <strong>${proses}</strong> • Antrean <strong>${antrean}</strong> • Completion Rate <strong>${completionRate}%</strong>.</p>
        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm mt-1 text-indigo-700 font-semibold">Insight: ${insight}</p>
      `;
    }
  }

  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
};

// ==================== CLAIM ORDER - VERSI FINAL ====================
window.claimOrder = async function(orderId) {
  if (!confirm("Ambil pekerjaan ini? Anda akan menjadi penanggung jawab order ini.")) return;

  showLoading(true);
  try {
    const result = await serverCall('claimOrder', orderId, CURRENT_USER.Username);
    
    if (result.success) {
      customAlert("✅ Berhasil", "Anda berhasil mengambil pekerjaan ini!");

      // FORCE REFRESH FULL + RENDER ULANG
      await refreshData();           // Ambil data terbaru dari server
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 600)); // tunggu sebentar agar cache update
      filterOrdersFull();            // Render ulang daftar order

    } else {
      customAlert("❌ Gagal", result.message || "Order sudah diambil orang lain");
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.error(e);
    customAlert("❌ Error", "Gagal mengambil pekerjaan");
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};

// ==================== GRAFIK ORDER PER WORKER (NAMA LENGKAP + WARNA BERBEDA) ====================
window.renderOrderPerWorkerChart = function() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('orderPerWorkerChart');
  if (!canvas || !DASHBOARD_CACHE || !DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders) return;

  const orders = DASHBOARD_CACHE.orders.filter(o => window.safeGet(o, 'TAHAPAN PESANAN') === 'Selesai');
  const nameMap = {};
  if (DASHBOARD_CACHE.users) {
    DASHBOARD_CACHE.users.forEach(u => {
      const username = String(u.Username || '').trim();
      const fullName = String(u['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || u.Nama || username).trim();
      if (username) nameMap[username] = fullName || username;
    });
  }

  const workerStats = {};
  orders.forEach(o => {
    const username = window.safeGet(o, 'WORKER');
    if (username) workerStats[nameMap[username] || username] = (workerStats[nameMap[username] || username] || 0) + 1;
  });

  const labels = Object.keys(workerStats);
  const values = Object.values(workerStats);
  const avg = values.length ? (values.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0) / values.length) : 0;
  const grad = canvas.getContext('2d').createLinearGradient(0,0,0,260);
  grad.addColorStop(0,'rgba(99,102,241,.95)');
  grad.addColorStop(1,'rgba(192,38,211,.45)');

  if (window.orderChartInstance) window.orderChartInstance.destroy();
  window.orderChartInstance = new Chart(canvas, {
    data: {
      labels: labels.length ? labels : ['Belum ada data'],
      datasets: [
        { type:'bar', label:'Order Selesai', data: values.length ? values : [0], backgroundColor: grad, borderRadius: 12, barThickness: 34 },
        { type:'line', label:'Rata-rata', data: (values.length ? values : [0]).map(()=>avg), borderColor:'#0f172a', borderDash:[6,4], tension:0.3, pointRadius:0 }
      ]
    },
    options: {
      responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
      plugins: { legend: { display: true, labels: { usePointStyle:true, pointStyle:'circle' } } },
      scales: {
        y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { color: '#eef2ff' }, ticks: { stepSize: 1 } },
        x: { grid: { display: false } }
      }
    }
  });
};

// Render Promo Slider
window.renderPromoSlider = async function() {
  const wrapper = document.getElementById('slider-wrapper');
  if (!wrapper) return;

  wrapper.innerHTML = `
    <div class="swiper-slide flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-[#7c3aed]/10 to-[#c026d3]/10 h-full">
      <div class="flex flex-col items-center">
        <div class="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-[#7c3aed] border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
        <p class="text-slate-400 text-sm mt-4">Memuat animasi slide...</p>
      </div>
    </div>`;

  try {
    const images = await serverCall('getPromoImages');
    console.log("📸 Slider animasi menerima:", images.length, "gambar");

    if (images && images.length > 0) {
      wrapper.innerHTML = images.map(img => `
        <div class="swiper-slide relative overflow-hidden">
          <img src="${img.url}" 
               alt="Promo"
               class="w-full h-full object-cover ken-burns-animation">
        </div>
      `).join('');
    } else {
      wrapper.innerHTML = `
        <div class="swiper-slide flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-[#7c3aed]/10 to-[#c026d3]/10 h-full text-center">
          <div>
            <i data-lucide="image-off" class="w-20 h-20 mx-auto text-[#7c3aed]/30"></i>
            <p class="text-slate-400 font-medium mt-4">Belum ada gambar di folder Drive</p>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Slider error:", err);
    wrapper.innerHTML = `<div class="swiper-slide flex items-center justify-center bg-red-50 text-red-400 h-full">Gagal memuat slider</div>`;
  }

  // Restart Swiper dengan animasi premium
  if (window.mySwiper) window.mySwiper.destroy();
  window.mySwiper = new Swiper('.mySwiper', {
    loop: true,
    autoplay: { delay: 3800, disableOnInteraction: false },
    pagination: { el: '.swiper-pagination', clickable: true },
    navigation: { nextEl: '.swiper-button-next', prevEl: '.swiper-button-prev' },
    effect: 'creative',
    creativeEffect: {
      prev: { shadow: true, translate: ['-120%', 0, -500] },
      next: { shadow: true, translate: ['120%', 0, -500] },
      limitProgress: 1
    },
    speed: 1100,
    grabCursor: true
  });

  lucide.createIcons();
};
// Cek Status Pesanan
window.checkOrderStatus = async function() {
  const input = document.getElementById('order-id-input').value.trim();
  if (!input) return customAlert("⚠️", "Masukkan kode pesanan");

  const result = await serverCall('checkPublicOrderStatus', input);
  const div = document.getElementById('status-result');

  if (result.success) {
    const d = result.data;
    div.classList.remove('hidden');
    div.innerHTML = `
      <div class="bg-emerald-50 p-6 rounded-2xl">
        <p class="font-mono text-emerald-700 mb-2">ID: <strong>${d.id}</strong></p>
        <p class="font-semibold">${d.nama} • ${d.layanan}</p>
        <div class="mt-4 flex justify-between text-sm">
          <div>Status: <span class="status-${d.status} px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold">${d.status}</span></div>
          <div>Bayar: <span class="font-medium">${d.payment}</span></div>
        </div>
        <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-3">${d.rincian}</p>
      </div>`;
  } else {
    div.innerHTML = `<p class="text-red-500 text-center">${result.message}</p>`;
  }
};

window.showStatusChecker = () => document.getElementById('cek-status-section').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });

// Modal functions

window.hideOrderModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-order').classList.add('hidden');

window.showOrderModal = async function(serviceName = '') {
  const modal = document.getElementById('modal-order');
  if (!modal) return;

  modal.classList.remove('hidden');

  // Pastikan opsi layanan publik sudah terisi dulu, lalu prefill layanan dari katalog.
  if (typeof populatePublicServicesSelect === 'function') await populatePublicServicesSelect();
  const serviceSelect = document.getElementById('select-layanan-public');
  if (serviceSelect) {
    const normalized = String(serviceName || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (normalized) {
      const matched = Array.from(serviceSelect.options).find(o => String(o.value || '').trim().toLowerCase() === normalized);
      if (matched) {
        serviceSelect.value = matched.value;
      }
    } else {
      serviceSelect.value = '';
    }
  }

  const titleEl = modal.querySelector('h3');
  if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = serviceName ? `Formulir Pemesanan • ${serviceName}` : 'Formulir Pemesanan';
};


window.closeModal = function(e) {
  if (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  }
  // Tutup SEMUA modal yang terbuka
  document.getElementById('modal-status').classList.add('hidden');
  document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.add('hidden');
  document.getElementById('modal-order').classList.add('hidden');
  document.getElementById('notification-modal').classList.add('hidden');
};

window.hidePromoBubble = function(e) {
  if (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  }
  const bubble = document.getElementById('promo-bubble');
  if (bubble) {
    bubble.style.transition = 'all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)';
    bubble.style.opacity = '0';
    bubble.style.transform = 'translateY(40px)';
    setTimeout(() => {
      bubble.classList.add('hidden');
      bubble.style.opacity = '1';
      bubble.style.transform = 'none';
    }, 400);
  }
};
window.showStatusModal = function(orderId = null) {
  const modal = document.getElementById('modal-status');
  modal.classList.remove('hidden');
  if (orderId) {
    document.getElementById('order-id-input-modal').value = orderId;
  }
};




window.checkOrderStatusModal = async function() {
  const id = document.getElementById('order-id-input-modal').value.trim();
  const resultDiv = document.getElementById('status-result-modal');
  if (!id) return resultDiv.innerHTML = `<p class="text-red-500 text-sm">Masukkan kode pesanan dulu bestie 👀</p>`;
  showLoading(true, 'Lagi cek progres order kamu...');
  try {
    const res = await serverCall('checkPublicOrderStatus', id);
    if (!res.success) {
      resultDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="status-result-animate bg-rose-50 border border-rose-100 p-5 rounded-2xl text-rose-700 text-sm font-semibold">${res.message}</div>`;
      return;
    }
    const d = res.data || {};
    const status = String(d.status || '-');
    const statusMap = { 'Antrean': 25, 'Proses': 65, 'Revisi': 80, 'Selesai': 100, 'Batal': 100 };
    const pct = statusMap[status] || 15;
    resultDiv.innerHTML = `
      <div class="status-result-animate bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-5 rounded-2xl space-y-3">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between"><p class="font-mono text-xs text-slate-500">ID: ${d.id || id}</p><span class="text-xs px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 font-bold">${status}</span></div>
        <p class="font-black text-slate-800">${d.nama || '-'} • ${d.layanan || '-'}</p>
        <div>
          <div class="h-2.5 bg-slate-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden"><div class="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-fuchsia-500" style="width:${pct}%"></div></div>
          <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1">Progress estimasi: ${pct}%</p>
        </div>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-[11px]">
          <div class="bg-white border rounded-xl p-2"><p class="text-slate-400">Tahap 1</p><p class="font-bold">Brief</p></div>
          <div class="bg-white border rounded-xl p-2"><p class="text-slate-400">Tahap 2</p><p class="font-bold">Produksi</p></div>
          <div class="bg-white border rounded-xl p-2"><p class="text-slate-400">Tahap 3</p><p class="font-bold">Finalisasi</p></div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};
