// ==================== HANDLE LOGIN (yang hilang) ====================
// ==================== HANDLE LOGIN (VERSI FIXED & LENGKAP) ====================
window.prefillRememberedUsername = function() {
  const usernameInput = document.getElementById('login-username');
  const rememberCheckbox = document.getElementById('remember-username');
  if (!usernameInput || !rememberCheckbox) return;
  const rememberedUsername = localStorage.getItem('lc_remembered_username') || '';
  usernameInput.value = rememberedUsername;
  rememberCheckbox.checked = !!rememberedUsername;
};

window.handleLogin = async function(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  showLoading(true, "Sedang masuk ke dashboard...");

  try {
    const username = document.getElementById('login-username').value.trim();
    const password = document.getElementById('login-password').value.trim();
    const rememberUsername = !!document.getElementById('remember-username')?.checked;

    const res = await serverCall('login', username, password);

    if (res.success) {
      if (rememberUsername) localStorage.setItem('lc_remembered_username', username);
      else localStorage.removeItem('lc_remembered_username');
      CURRENT_USER = res.user;
      DASHBOARD_CACHE = {};                    // ← Penting: reset cache dulu

      switchView('dashboard');

      // === LOGIC ROLE & UI ANDA TETAP SAMA ===
      const roleStr = String(CURRENT_USER.Role).toLowerCase();
      const isAdmin = roleStr === 'admin';
      const isBendahara = roleStr === 'bendahara' || roleStr === 'finance';

      document.getElementById('admin-only-nav').classList.toggle('hidden', !isAdmin);
      document.getElementById('bendahara-only-nav').classList.toggle('hidden', !isBendahara);
      const adminMobile = document.getElementById('admin-only-nav-mobile');
      const bendaharaMobile = document.getElementById('bendahara-only-nav-mobile');
      if (adminMobile) adminMobile.classList.toggle('hidden', !isAdmin);
      if (bendaharaMobile) bendaharaMobile.classList.toggle('hidden', !isBendahara);

      document.getElementById('user-display-name').innerText = CURRENT_USER['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || CURRENT_USER.Username;
      document.getElementById('user-display-role').innerText = CURRENT_USER.Role;
      document.getElementById('profile-initial').innerText = (CURRENT_USER['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || CURRENT_USER.Username)[0].toUpperCase();

      document.getElementById('admin-running-text-box').classList.toggle('hidden', !isAdmin);
      document.getElementById('admin-add-order-btn').classList.toggle('hidden', !isAdmin);
      document.getElementById('admin-bulk-delete').classList.toggle('hidden', !isAdmin);

      if (isBendahara) document.getElementById('btn-input-gaji').classList.remove('hidden');

      // === LOAD DATA SETELAH LOGIN ===
      await refreshData();                     // ← Ini yang memperbaiki "orders is null"

      // Pilih tampilan awal
      if (isBendahara && !isAdmin) {
        showSubView('pengeluaran');
      } else {
        showSubView('monitor');
      }

      updateAuthHeader();
      if (typeof startNotificationPolling === 'function') startNotificationPolling();

      customAlert("✅ Berhasil Masuk", `Selamat datang, ${CURRENT_USER['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || CURRENT_USER.Username}!`, "success");

    } else {
      customAlert("❌ Gagal Login", res.message || "Username atau password salah", "error");
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Login Error:", e);
    customAlert("❌ Error", "Terjadi kesalahan saat login. Silakan coba lagi.", "error");
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};

  window.open(`https://wa.me/6285811699699?text=${text}`, '_blank');
};

  }
};

// ==================== ANIMASI SETIAP TINDAKAN ====================
window.addActionAnimation = function(elementId) {
  const el = document.getElementById(elementId) || document.querySelector(elementId);
  if (el) {
    el.classList.add('action-pulse');
    setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('action-pulse'), 800);
  }
};

// Contoh: panggil otomatis di refreshData
window.refreshData = async function() {
  showLoading(true, "Memuat semua data dari server...");
  
  try {
    const data = await serverCall('getDashboardData', CURRENT_USER.Role, CURRENT_USER.Username);
    DASHBOARD_CACHE = data || {};
    
    console.log("✅ Data berhasil dimuat:", Object.keys(DASHBOARD_CACHE));
    
    renderDashboardData();   // Render semua tabel
    return true;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Refresh Data Error:", e);
    customAlert("❌ Gagal Memuat Data", "Cek koneksi internet atau refresh halaman", "error");
    return false;
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};
// ==================== CUSTOM ALERT (WAJIB) ====================
window.customAlert = function(title, message, type = 'success') {
  const colors = {
    success: '#10b981',
    error: '#ef4444',
    info: '#3b82f6'
  };
  const alertBox = document.createElement('div');
  alertBox.style.cssText = `
    position:fixed; top:20px; right:20px; z-index:99999; 
    background:${colors[type] || '#3b82f6'}; color:white; 
    padding:16px 24px; border-radius:16px; box-shadow:0 10px 30px -10px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
    font-weight:600; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:12px; min-width:280px;
  `;
  alertBox.innerHTML = `
    <i data-lucide="${type==='success'?'check-circle':type==='error'?'x-circle':'info'}"></i>
    <div>
      <div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:700">${title}</div>
      <div style="font-size:13px;opacity:0.9">${message}</div>
    </div>
  `;
  document.body.appendChild(alertBox);

  if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
  setTimeout(() => {
    alertBox.style.transition = 'all 0.4s ease';
    alertBox.style.opacity = '0';
    alertBox.style.transform = 'translateY(-20px)';
    setTimeout(() => alertBox.remove(), 400);
  }, 3500);
};

  };
}


window.handleSaveOrder = async function(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  showLoading(true, "Menyimpan pesanan...");

  const nama = document.getElementById('admin-order-nama').value.trim();
  const hp = document.getElementById('admin-order-hp').value.trim();
  const emailRaw = document.getElementById('admin-order-email').value;
  const email = String(emailRaw || '').trim().replace(/\u200B/g, '').toLowerCase();
  const layanan = document.getElementById('admin-order-svc').value.trim();
  const qty = parseFloat(document.getElementById('admin-order-qty').value);
  const harga = parseFloat(document.getElementById('admin-order-harga').value);
  const rincian = document.getElementById('admin-order-rincian').value.trim();
  const deadline = document.getElementById('admin-order-deadline').value;

  if (!nama || !hp || !email || !layanan || !rincian || !deadline) {
    showLoading(false);
    return customAlert("⚠️ Validasi", "Semua field utama wajib diisi (nama, HP, email, layanan, rincian, deadline).", "error");
  }
  if (Number.isNaN(qty) || qty <= 0) {
    showLoading(false);
    return customAlert("⚠️ Validasi", "Kuantitas harus lebih dari 0.", "error");
  }
  if (Number.isNaN(harga) || harga <= 0) {
    showLoading(false);
    return customAlert("⚠️ Validasi", "Harga jual wajib diisi dan harus lebih dari 0.", "error");
  }
  if (rincian.length < 8) {
    showLoading(false);
    return customAlert("⚠️ Validasi", "Rincian pesanan minimal 8 karakter agar instruksi jelas.", "error");
  }
  if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) {
    showLoading(false);
    return customAlert("⚠️ Validasi", "Format email tidak valid.", "error");
  }

  const formData = {
    'NAMA PELANGGAN': nama,
    'NO HANDPHONE': hp,
    'EMAIL': email,
    'LAYANAN': layanan,
    'QTY': qty,
    'TOTAL HARGA': harga,
    'RINCIAN PESANAN': rincian,
    'DEADLINE': deadline,
    'WORKER': document.getElementById('admin-order-worker') ? document.getElementById('admin-order-worker').value : '',
    'STATUS PEMBAYARAN': document.getElementById('admin-order-statusbayar').value,
    'JENIS PEMBAYARAN': document.getElementById('admin-order-jenispembayaran').value,
    'DISCOUNT': parseFloat(document.getElementById('admin-order-diskon')?.value) || 0,
    'fee Admin(%)': parseFloat(document.getElementById('admin-order-feeadmin')?.value) || 10,
    'fee perusahaan(%)': parseFloat(document.getElementById('admin-order-feeperusahaan')?.value) || 15,
    'DP': 0
  };

  // Jika DP
  if (formData['STATUS PEMBAYARAN'] === 'Down Payment (DP)') {
    formData['DP'] = parseFloat(document.getElementById('manual-dp-percent')?.value) || 0;
  }

  console.log("📤 Data yang akan dikirim:", formData);
  // AUTO PENGINPUT PESANAN untuk Kolom S
  formData['PENGINPUT PESANAN'] = CURRENT_USER ? 
    (CURRENT_USER.Username || CURRENT_USER['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || 'ADMIN') : 'ADMIN';
  const result = await serverCall('saveOrder', formData);

  if (result.success) {
    closeModal();
    await refreshData();
    customAlert("✅ Berhasil", "Pesanan berhasil disimpan", "success");
  } else {
    customAlert("❌ Gagal", result.message || "Terjadi kesalahan", "error");
  }

  showLoading(false);
};


// Toggle tampilan DP
window.toggleDPSection = function() {
  const status = document.getElementById('admin-order-statusbayar').value;
  const dpSection = document.getElementById('dp-section-manual');
  dpSection.classList.toggle('hidden', status !== 'Down Payment (DP)');
  if (status === 'Down Payment (DP)') {
    const percentInput = document.getElementById('manual-dp-percent');
    if (percentInput && (!percentInput.value || Number(percentInput.value) <= 0)) {
      percentInput.value = 50;
    }
    syncDPManual('percent');
  }
};

// Sync DP % dan Nominal
window.syncDPManual = function(source) {
  const harga = parseFloat(document.getElementById('admin-order-harga').value) || 0;
  const qty = parseFloat(document.getElementById('admin-order-qty').value) || 1;
  const gross = harga * qty;
  const net = gross; // bisa ditambah diskon nanti

  const percentInput = document.getElementById('manual-dp-percent');
  const nominalInput = document.getElementById('manual-dp-nominal');
  if (!percentInput || !nominalInput) return;

  if (source === 'percent') {
    if (percentInput.value === '') {
      nominalInput.value = '';
      return;
    }
    const percent = parseFloat(percentInput.value) || 0;
    nominalInput.value = Math.round(net * percent / 100);
    return;
  }

  if (source === 'nominal') {
    if (nominalInput.value === '') {
      percentInput.value = '';
      return;
    }
    const nominal = parseFloat(nominalInput.value) || 0;
    percentInput.value = net > 0 ? ((nominal / net) * 100).toFixed(2) : '0';
    return;
  }

  const percent = parseFloat(percentInput.value) || 0;
  nominalInput.value = Math.round(net * percent / 100);
};

// Hitung total harga (Qty × Harga)
window.hitungTotalHargaAdmin = function() {
  const harga = parseFloat(document.getElementById('admin-order-harga')?.value) || 0;
  const qty   = parseFloat(document.getElementById('admin-order-qty')?.value) || 1;
  const totalEl = document.getElementById('admin-order-total');
  if (totalEl) totalEl.value = Math.round(harga * qty);
};

// ==================== DROPDOWN SATUAN - VERSI FINAL & ROBUST ====================
window.loadUniqueSatuan = function() {
  const select = document.getElementById('admin-order-satuan');
  if (!select) {
    console.error("❌ Element #admin-order-satuan tidak ditemukan di HTML!");
    return;
  }

  if (!DASHBOARD_CACHE?.allServices || DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices.length === 0) {
    console.warn("⚠️ DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices masih kosong. Refresh dashboard dulu.");
    select.innerHTML = `<option value="">Memuat data satuan...</option>`;
    return;
  }

  // Ambil dari kolom "Satuan" di Sheet Layanan (kolom H)
  const satuanList = DASHBOARD_CACHE.allServices
    .map(s => String(s['Satuan'] || s.Satuan || 'Unit').trim())
    .filter(s => s && s.length > 0);

  const uniqueSatuan = [...new Set(satuanList)].sort();

  console.log("✅ Satuan berhasil dimuat:", uniqueSatuan);

  let html = `<option value="">Pilih Satuan...</option>`;
  uniqueSatuan.forEach(s => {
    html += `<option value="${s}">${s}</option>`;
  });

  html += `<option value="Lainnya">Lainnya (ketik manual)</option>`;

  select.innerHTML = html;
};

let currentOrderIdForAction = null;

// Proses Order (Worker)
window.startProsesOrder = function(orderId) {
  currentOrderIdForAction = orderId;
  document.getElementById('modal-worker-proses').classList.remove('hidden');
  document.getElementById('catatan-worker-input').value = ''; // kosongkan dulu
};

window.hideWorkerProsesModal = function() {
  document.getElementById('modal-worker-proses').classList.add('hidden');
};

window.useTemplateCatatan = function(text) {
  document.getElementById('catatan-worker-input').value = text;
};

window.confirmProsesOrder = async function() {
  const catatan = document.getElementById('catatan-worker-input').value.trim();
  hideWorkerProsesModal();

  if (!currentOrderIdForAction) return;

  showLoading(true);
  try {
    await serverCall('updateOrder', currentOrderIdForAction, {
      status: "Proses",
      note: catatan,
      worker: CURRENT_USER.Username   // pastikan worker terisi
    });
    customAlert("✅ Berhasil", "Status diubah menjadi Proses", "success");
    await refreshData();
  } catch(e) {
    customAlert("❌ Gagal", "Terjadi kesalahan", "error");
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};

// Transfer ke Worker Lain
window.showTransferModal = async function(orderId) {
  currentOrderIdForAction = orderId;
  const select = document.getElementById('transfer-worker-select');
  select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Pilih Worker...</option>';

  const workers = DASHBOARD_CACHE.users.filter(u => 
    String(u.Role).toLowerCase() === 'worker' && 
    u.Username !== CURRENT_USER.Username
  );

  workers.forEach(w => {
    const opt = document.createElement('option');
    opt.value = w.Username;
    opt.textContent = w['Nama Lengkap Admin'] || w.Username;
    select.appendChild(opt);
  });

  document.getElementById('modal-transfer').classList.remove('hidden');
};

window.hideTransferModal = function() {
  document.getElementById('modal-transfer').classList.add('hidden');
};

window.confirmTransferOrder = async function() {
  const newWorker = document.getElementById('transfer-worker-select').value;
  hideTransferModal();

  if (!newWorker || !currentOrderIdForAction) return;

  showLoading(true);
  try {
    await serverCall('transferOrder', currentOrderIdForAction, newWorker, CURRENT_USER);
    customAlert("✅ Berhasil", "Pesanan berhasil diserahkan", "success");
    await refreshData();
  } catch(e) {
    customAlert("❌ Gagal", "Terjadi kesalahan", "error");
  } finally {
    showLoading(false);
  }
};

window.calculateWorkingDuration = function(startTime, endTime = null) {
  if (!startTime) return '';
  const start = new Date(startTime);
  const stop = endTime ? new Date(endTime) : new Date();
  let diffMs = stop - start;
  if (diffMs < 0) return '0m';

  const days = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  diffMs %= 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24;
  const hours = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60));
  diffMs %= 1000 * 60 * 60;
  const minutes = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60));

  if (days > 0) return `${days}h ${hours}j ${minutes}m`;
  if (hours > 0) return `${hours}j ${minutes}m`;
  return `${minutes}m`;
};

    // Initialize Application
    initApp();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
